Chapter 1101: 1100 Why does this cook’s cooking seem like a fight?

All the Spirit Chefs looked toward the golden Star armband worn by Lu Yang, their eyes full of envy. This was the supreme honor that all Spirit Chefs dreamed of, yet compared to the status of Immortal Cook’s Successor, the golden Star armband seemed somewhat dimmed.

It could be said that if Lu Yang hadn’t obtained the golden Star armband, it would have diminished its value!

"Little Yang, be confident, you are a Spirit Chef personally taught by me, becoming number one is only natural." Fairy Eternity was quite pleased with herself, as Lu Yang won in the final round relying on her Yin Yang Tofu!

Though there were some twists and unexpected events, it was an undeniable fact that Lu Yang triumphed in the Five Star Spirit Chef Team.

After all five competitions had ended, Song Tianjing, the winner of the Two Star Spirit Chef Team, stepped onto the award platform under everyone’s watchful eyes, and Yu Qian’er’s eyes shone when she looked at Song Tianjing.

"Song Tianjing, what kind of ingredient would you like as a reward?" asked Daoist Ming Yuan.

Song Tianjing initially wanted Firmament Tree, as all three dishes in the Complete Culinary Compendium used it for firewood.

But since Firmament Tree wasn’t exactly an ingredient, and it was unlikely that the Spirit Chef Alliance had it.

"Star Core, I want the Star Core."

"Oh, that’s interesting," Daoist Ming Yuan handed a square wooden box to Song Tianjing, containing the Star Core inside.

"Lu Yang, what ingredient do you desire?" Daoist Ming Yuan’s attitude towards Lu Yang was noticeably more gentle than towards the other winners.

"Immortal Fruit."

That embarrassing thing from Fairy Eternity had to be recovered.

Daoist Ming Yuan solemnly handed half of an Immortal Fruit to Lu Yang: "Our alliance failed to recognize the Immortal Object as a mere Spiritual Object, truly a shame. I hope this fruit will shine uniquely in your hands."

"It will!" Lu Yang responded with the same solemnity.

The winners of the other three groups were Jin Tan’s men, and according to his orders, they obtained three legendary ingredients from Daoist Ming Yuan.

"Sir, mission accomplished!"

The three group winners knelt before Jin Tan, presenting the three legendary ingredients.

"Bodhi Glazed Fruit, Nine Revolutions Coiled Dragon Bamboo Shoot, Proverb Fish, we’re just missing the one from the Five Star Spirit Chef Team..."

Jin Tan’s eyes narrowed slightly, he hadn’t expected Lu Yang to pull off a Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth, pretending to be a member of their team.

"Well, missing one doesn’t matter!"

Jin Tan strode with the vigor of a tiger and dragon to confront Daoist Ming Yuan, pointing at his nose.

"Old Daoist Ming Yuan, I hereby challenge you for the position of Alliance Leader. Do you dare to accept the battle?"

He had been biding his time for three hundred years for this day, only he hadn’t anticipated the unforeseen emergence of Lu Yang and Song Tianjing.

He was never one for elaborate schemes, but for the Alliance Leader’s position, he had clashed wits and courage with Daoist Ming Yuan for three hundred years, unwilling to continue the fight. Now that they had fallen out, it was time to settle it once and for all!

The crowd that had dispersed started to get agitated.

"What, what, Jin Tan is issuing an Alliance Leader challenge?"

"I heard that he was terribly defeated by Daoist Ming Yuan three hundred years ago, he’s surely well-prepared if he’s come back today."

"There’s excitement on the way!"

"Will Alliance Leader Ming Yuan accept the challenge?"

"He has to, rejecting it would undermine his authority as leader, and there would be no point in holding his position!"

Daoist Ming Yuan looked at Jin Tan’s determined gaze, sighed and lowered his head: "You have finally taken this step, so be it, I will accept your challenge."

The crowd erupted in cheers, a duel between two Eighth Star Spirit Chefs, a scene many Spirit Chefs would never witness in their lifetimes!

"Second-in-command, I knew you would win!" Yun Mengmeng said excitedly, with the Chief Housekeeper himself giving pointers, how could it be otherwise?

Now, everyone was more excited than Lu Yang himself.

"Thank you," Lu Yang replied calmly, taking a bite of the half Immortal Fruit from four hundred thousand years ago.

Hmm, quite delicious, juicy and plump, as if just picked.

"Let’s watch the competition before leaving," Lu Yang was also curious about what a battle between Eighth Star Spirit Chefs would look like.

The judging panel had been temporarily transformed into cooking stations, and two great Eighth Star Spirit Chefs stood facing each other, the atmosphere heavy.

With a gong sound, both chefs sprang into action.

Exorcising demons, chopping vegetables, extracting juice, seasoning the pot... The aroma quickly spread throughout the venue.

"Look, there’s something behind them!" someone exclaimed.

Behind each chef rose the illusory shadows of a black dragon and a white phoenix, and as dragon and phoenix cries resonated, the shadows fiercely battled!

"What is that?" Lu Yang was confused. "Fairy, do you recognize it?"

"I’ve never seen such a thing; that’s not how we cooked in ancient times."

At that moment, Ma Bin came over to explain to Lu Yang, "According to ancient texts, when ancient Immortal Cooks prepared food, the shadows of dragons and phoenixes often accompanied them, with constant cries of dragons and phoenixes."

"The founder of the Spirit Chef Alliance theorized that this is a sign of reaching the Nine Star Spirit Chef Realm."

"To approach the Nine Star Spirit Chef Realm, the founder of the Spirit Chef Alliance created a unique Spirit Chef Technique. Once one becomes an Eighth Star Spirit Chef, their essence, qi, and spirit merge into the dishes, and the dishes summon partial illusory shadows for battle. The side whose shadow wins offers the tastier dish!"

"...That’s really challenging the founder of the Spirit Chef Alliance, inventing such a technique," Lu Yang couldn’t help scoffing.

"Hehe, Fellow Daoist Lu Yang," Ma Bin took the opportunity to get closer with Lu Yang, rubbing his hands with a smile, only to be pushed away in disgust by Lu Yang.

"Speak plainly."

"Fellow Daoist Lu Yang, considering you are a successor of the Immortal Chef, could you perhaps teach me a technique or two? I would surely repay you generously on behalf of the Ma Family!"

"Aren’t you from the Imperial Kitchen?"

"Yes, the Blades of the Imperial Kitchen!"

Lu Yang thought it over, considering Emperor Xia was, after all, the Prime Minister of the Great Bean Dynasty, and it wouldn’t do well to have scandal before he even took office — it would look bad as internal strife within the Great Bean Dynasty.

"No."

Ma Bin looked disappointed, but it was understandable, as Immortal Chef’s skills were not to be imparted lightly: "I was inappropriate to ask."

At that moment, the two Eighth Star Spirit Chefs were in the heat of battle, focused solely on the pure competition.

"Daoist Ming Yuan, the position of Alliance Leader should rightfully be mine, behold the ultimate dish I diligently mastered!"

"Jin Tan, you’re still too young, not solid enough, leaving the Spirit Chef Alliance in your hands would only lead it to decline!"

"Behold my Devouring Life Blade Technique!"

"Willow Leaf Light Wind Blade Technique!"

Jin Tan’s chef’s knife emanated devouring power, absorbing all impurities from the vegetables during chopping!

Ming Yuan’s blade moved as though a gentle breeze, not intense, yet offering an immensely reassuring and comfortable sensation.

"Heavenly Fire, heed my command, ignite!"

"Waters of the Far Northern Ice Layer, appear!"

Dragons and phoenixes clashed, water and fire confronted each other, sending countless ripples through the space; dragon and phoenix cries could be heard miles away, startling Emperor Xia in the Imperial Palace.

"Scared me; I thought Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi had brought their fight here."

"Why do these chefs cook as if they are fighting?"

Among them was Jiang Lianyi, one of the top Semi-Immortals and one who shared the same Dao Fruit Outline as him; he definitely couldn’t stop her.

But mere two Transcendance Tribulation Stage chefs couldn’t stir up any trouble in his Demon City!

Chapter 1102: 1101: Falling into a Trap!

Illusory shadows of dragons and phoenixes twirled above Demon City, prompting the demons there to all look up, involuntarily recalling the scene when the Nation Master and the Phoenix Ancestor fought during the founding of their nation.

Although the spectacle was not comparable to that from the early days, it was still counted among the top tier among the Pass Tribulation Cultivators, making it the perfect finale for the Spirit Chef contest.

A wide golden path stretched from the Imperial Palace, extending to the site of the Spirit Chef contest.

Eight blood-colored Dragon Colts galloped on the golden path, their neighs signifying Zhu Tian was on his way.

But what was being pulled in the carriage was not Zhu Tian, but an edict.

The edict unfurled, hovering over the contest site, crushing the heavens beneath it, and sealing all divinities. The illusion of Zhu Tian emerged, suppressing the illusions of the dragon and phoenix.

Zhu Tian lifted his hand, calling for an end to the conflict, "Both of you, stop."

His deep and resounding voice echoed in all directions of Demon City, causing the citizens to hail the Emperor like a tsunami, wave after wave of shouts, such that even Lu Yang could feel the overwhelming sound waves!

"Your Majesty," Jin Tan, seeing the edict, quickly bowed respectfully.

The edict represented Zhu Tian’s personal involvement, demanding reverence.

"Greetings, Nation Master Zhu," Daoist Ming Yuan stopped his actions given he was in Zhu Tian’s territory, and propriety was demanded.

Merely an illusion, merely five words, yet they made two Transcendance Tribulation Cultivators cease their hostilities, not daring to harbor any covetous thoughts, illustrating the profound authority of Zhu Tian!

"We have not fought for a long time and have forgotten our manners; please, Nation Master Zhu, punish us," Daoist Ming Yuan bowed deeply as he spoke.

Zhu Tian waved his hand, his large sleeves fluttering, wrapping around his arms and then behind his back, "It’s been long since I’ve seen a dispute among Spirit Chefs, reminding me of the ancient times. What talk is there of punishment? Just ensure that you two do not fight within Demon City, lest it disturbs the lives of the citizens."

His gaze was profound, as if gazing into the Dragon and Phoenix battles of ancient times, yet also seeming concerned about the current livelihood of Demon City’s citizens, merging past and present in an inscrutable manner.

"Yes."

"Thank you, Nation Master Zhu."

The illusion of Zhu Tian dissipated, the edict returning to the Imperial Palace by carriage, the golden path also vanished into the sky, turning into golden light like numerous stars, resonating beautifully with the sunset.

Throughout this process, Daoist Ming Yuan continued to show his respect towards Zhu Tian by bowing deeply without once raising his head.

...

"At last, it’s over," Lu Yang stretched lazily, feeling somewhat disappointed he hadn’t seen the outcome of Jin Tan and Daoist Ming Yuan’s duel, though he understood Zhu Tian’s concerns—their cooking had indeed been excessively disruptive.

"Sister Mengmeng, let’s rest tonight, we should head back tomorrow."

Yun Mengmeng was still reluctant to leave the delicacies of the Spirit Chef Alliance, but she also knew once everyone from the alliance left, the place wouldn’t have much to offer.

"Then Second-in-command, you go back first, can I meet someone to say goodbye?"

Lu Yang chuckled and shook his head, "That Spirit Chef from the small tavern you visit every evening for chats? Go ahead."

"See you at the inn!"

"It seems you really got along well," Lu Yang laughed, seeing Yun Mengmeng’s interest in someone else for the first time.

Yun Mengmeng rushed back to the small shop, hoping to leave before the owner returned to avoid causing trouble for Little Lotus.

"Little Lotus, are you busy?"

The shop was as deserted as ever, Yun Mengmeng having never seen another customer there besides herself in all her visits.

"Everyone went to watch the contest, who would come here?" Little Lotus spoke, not stopping her hands as she pounded burnt chili with a mortar and pestle.

"The contest is over; you’ll have people here tomorrow!" Yun Mengmeng cheerfully said.

"Yes, and tomorrow you’ll be gone," Little Lotus said reluctantly, sad to part with a friend she’d gotten along with so well, whom she’d not see again tomorrow. She always felt Yun Mengmeng had an aura of an adult.

It was a pity she only had a vague impression of the adult, obscured as though by a layer of mist, impossible to dispel.

Seeing Little Lotus downcast, Yun Mengmeng also felt disheartened.

"Sure, sure, do you want to learn cooking from my second-in-command? He just won the title in the Five Star Spirit Chef Team, and everyone says he’s the successor to the Immortal Cook!"

"Immortal Cook... successor?"

Hearing this term, Little Lotus showed a strange look in her eyes.

"Impressive, right? I’m not bragging, the second-in-command really has genuine skills, he even learned the Complete Culinary Compendium!"

"Complete Culinary Compendium? Wait for me a moment!"

Little Lotus dashed to the kitchen, rummaging through cabinets in search of her own Complete Culinary Compendium.

Just then, the wooden door of the small store was pushed open. Yun Mengmeng looked back in surprise, seeing a customer for the first time and preparing to help Little Lotus.

"Welcome... Alliance Leader Ming Yuan?"

Yun Mengmeng was even more surprised. Why was Alliance Leader Ming Yuan here?

Daoist Ming Yuan did not answer but just smiled mischievously and secretly snapped his fingers.

Outside of Demon City, a series of Array Methods that had been buried illuminated one after another, silently spreading throughout the entire city, isolating it from external contact temporarily, turning Demon City into a self-contained small world.

"My lord, everything is ready."

Daoist Ming Yuan spoke respectfully to the jade pendant hanging on his chest shaped like an eye.

This was the jade pendant he found in his youth in the East Sea, which marked the beginning of his fortune.

The shadow sitting on the throne of bones stood up, took a step forward, and vanished from the jade pendant’s small world, appearing right in front of Yun Mengmeng.

As soon as the shadow emerged, Yun Mengmeng felt an immense pressure, reminding her of her previous battle at the Sword Tower against the Ying Tian Corpse.

"Yun Mengmeng, you fell into the trap after all!" the shadow said with a faint smile, its body trembling slightly with uncontrollable excitement.

He, driven to obtain the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, had the power to flee from the Silent Extermination Immortal. He had set his sights on Yun Mengmeng’s Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit.

But Yun Mengmeng was at the Seek Dao Sect and Seek Dao Sect had Yun Zhi, so it was absolutely impossible to take action there.

Therefore, he found out that Yun Mengmeng referred to Lu Yang as the second-in-command and had a deep connection with Lu Yang, and also learned that Yun Mengmeng enjoyed good food. So, he ordered Daoist Ming Yuan to open the Spirit Chef Competition in Demon City and invited Lu Yang to come.

If Lu Yang came, Yun Mengmeng would surely follow!

So, he also had Daoist Ming Yuan set up a Great Formation in Demon City, allowing it to become a self-contained small world, isolated from the Silent Extermination Immortal’s sensing.

In this way, he could act without any hesitation!

Setting up such a formation was difficult; Daoist Ming Yuan spent more than ten days completing it.

Everything went according to his plan, Lu Yang accepted the invitation and came, with Yun Mengmeng following.

The Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit was right before his eyes!

"Zuo Shi Immortal, why are you here?" Little Lotus asked the shadow, confused.

...

As evening approached, the setting sun bathed the clouds in red. Lu Yang walked alone in the streets, weaving through the bustling crowd where vendors shouted their wares and children played and frolicked, the distant sound of chickens crowing and dogs barking, seeing the Human race and Demon race living together, everyone’s faces filled with happy smiles, and he couldn’t help but smile too.

The smell of incense from the mundane world, perhaps that was why most cultivators chose to become Worldly Cultivators.

Recalling this Spirit Chef Competition, though there were many coincidences, it ended safely without any trouble.

A stunning yet cold Female Immortal appeared at the end of the street.

In theory, her beauty should have caused passersby to exclaim, but in reality, apart from Lu Yang, no one noticed her presence.

The Female Immortal was a transcendent being, standing there alone, the crowd unconsciously moving around her.

She blocked Lu Yang’s path, her tone calm,

"Where is the Immortal Fairy, I need to see her."

Chapter 1103: 1102: She’s Doing Well With Me

Lu Yang stood frozen in place, his forehead covered in a dense layer of sweat beads.

Why on earth was Ashy Bean here, and why had she come looking for him directly?!

Lu Yang noticed that the passersby were oblivious to Ashy Bean’s presence, and by extension, they ignored his existence as well.

Was this the characteristic of nearing Transcendence that Eldest Senior Sister and Fairy had mentioned about Ashy Bean?

He suspected that if Ashy Bean hadn’t wanted to know the whereabouts of Fairy Eternity, she would have killed him in the instant they met!

Ashy Bean had been able to sense his presence all along; why had she only come looking for him now? Had something changed, or did Ashy Bean have a compelling reason to find him?

Lu Yang and Ashy Bean locked eyes, his gaze as serene as an ancient well. Despite bearing the same visage as Fairy Eternity, she imparted a completely different feeling to Lu Yang.

Ashy Bean had no interest in Lu Yang; whether he was the foremost of the Geniuses in this world or someone who had defeated Si Chen God Lord with an Immortal Body, none of it mattered to her. She just wanted to know the whereabouts of Immortal Fairy.

Instinct told Ashy Bean that finding Lu Yang would lead her to Immortal Fairy.

Ever since Lu Yang had come to Demon City, she had been struggling with whether or not to ask about the whereabouts of Immortal Fairy, until today, when she finally made up her mind to put a full stop to her connection with Immortal Fairy!

"Senior, perhaps we could change locations..."

Lu Yang wanted to speak and diffuse the tension, but Ashy Bean cut him off with a cold voice.

"Stop babbling. Where is she?"

The air of death and destruction enveloped Lu Yang; with just a thought, Ashy Bean could grind him into dust!

"Enough!"

A stunning figure floated out from Lu Yang’s body, dispersing the aura of destruction that had shrouded him, glaring angrily at Ashy Bean.

Even with mental preparation, seeing Ashy Bean, who looked exactly like her, was still startling.

"Immortal Fairy, so you’ve been in this lad’s Spiritual Space all along."

When she saw Fairy Eternity, Ashy Bean clenched her fists as if ready to lash out at any moment, yet ultimately, she relaxed and released her grip weakly.

With a whirlwind of emotions swaying her previously, Ashy Bean had now calmed down, and her demeanor was no longer as aggressively demanding as before.

"Walk with me."

The two women walked side by side on the street, resembling stylistically distinct twins, creating a striking scene.

Yet this scene was only visible to Lu Yang, who followed behind them.

The threat of Ashy Bean was unquestionably terrifying; not even the Four Ancient Immortals were her match. Among all the enemies Lu Yang knew, she was second only to the hidden mastermind.

Lu Yang had intended to summon Eldest Senior Sister, but Fairy Eternity stopped him, saying she wanted to resolve the issue with Ashy Bean herself.

"It’s unexpected, Fairy Eternity, that you would be resurrected in this era. If the Yingtian Immortal, the Qilin Immortal, and the others knew of your existence, I’m sure they would be overjoyed."

Ashy Bean looked at her other self, filled with deep emotion.

"Do you know, Immortal Fairy, I’m truly envious of you. After your death, Little Lotus, Lianyi, Xiao Ling, Ying Tian, the Kirin, Suiyue, Jiuchong... so many cared about you and wanted to bring you back to life."

"They delved into the Power of Incense and Fire, established the Fire Dynasty, formed the Heavenly Court, created the Underworld, and tried all sorts of methods."

"It’s just a pity that, in the end, it was me they resurrected."

"You should know the origin of my identity, right?"

Fairy Eternity stared at Ashy Bean. She had embarked on a path completely different from her own, "The Underworld Reincarnation resurrected me from my Annihilation Dao Fruit Outline phase."

"Indeed, I am another you." Ashy Bean managed a self-mocking smile, filled with an indescribable sense of desolation.

"When Ying Tian and the others resurrected me, they surrounded me all day, telling me your story, saying I would surely abandon the Annihilation Dao Fruit Outline and condense the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, the Eternity Dao Fruit, and become the true you."

"Can you imagine what I felt at that time? One moment, I was on the verge of becoming an Immortal, the first Immortal in history, and the next moment, I found myself a hundred thousand years later, possessing an Immortal Body, and becoming the supreme Heavenly Court Master."

Lu Yang tried to imagine Ashy Bean’s feelings at the time, finding the phrase ’worlds apart and everyone is a stranger’ would be an understatement.

Ashy Bean continued, "Although surrounded by familiar faces, the Yingtian Immortal and the others were entirely different from the ones in my memory; they possessed many memories I didn’t have. Their gaze upon me was not at me, but at you."

"Among all the old acquaintances, only Little Lotus treated me as myself, and not as Fairy Eternity."

"Little Lotus?"

Hearing the familiar name, Fairy Eternity’s expression shifted.

She had the closest bond with Xiao Ling, Lianyi, and Little Lotus. Among them, only Little Lotus’s whereabouts were unknown, even Yingtian Immortal had no clue where she was now.

"Yes, Little Lotus." Mentioning Qinghe, Ashy Bean’s tense face softened into a gentle smile, which quickly returned to her usual expression.

"After my resurrection, it was her who noticed my discomfort with the unknown environment and stayed with me voluntarily, never demanding anything from me, but telling me not to think too much and to learn to go with the flow."

"Although my soul is the you of the past, I’m also derived from the Power of Incense and Fire, influenced by the Incense, I need to become the Immortal Fairy they envision in their minds, the one to overcome the mastermind behind the scenes."

"I also want to condense the Eternity Dao Fruit, but I can’t do it. I can’t abandon the painful past, nor can I let go of the annihilation of our tribe."

At this point, Ashy Bean became emotional, exhibiting a physical gesture for the first time as she spread her arms, as if to declare her sovereignty to Fairy Eternity, "But so what? Even without condensing the Eternity Dao Fruit, I am still you, and I can still defeat the mastermind behind the scenes!"

"You are not me." Fairy Eternity locked eyes with Ashy Bean, her pupils reflecting her own silhouette.

"You are truly the ’adult’ that Little Lotus dearly misses, saying the exact same words she did." Ashy Bean’s lips curled into a mocking smile, unclear who she was ridiculing.

"Where is Little Lotus!" Fairy Eternity suddenly, grabbing Ashy Bean’s collar, sensed something was off.

Ashy Bean’s tone sounded just as if she had just seen Little Lotus!

If she had met Little Lotus, then Little Lotus’s situation was more likely to be grim!

Ashy Bean brushed off Fairy Eternity’s hands, "Little Lotus is fine with me, you don’t need to worry about her!"

"Little Lotus is with you!"

"Yes."

"What have you done to her!"

"I’ve told you, she’s fine with me, you’re superfluous."

Ashy Bean took pleasure in observing Fairy Eternity’s anxious face, finding joy in agitating another version of herself.

Suddenly, both goddesses’ expressions shifted as they looked up, sensing the Immortal Level Formation gradually enveloping Demon City, a Formation capable of isolating external awareness and designating Demon City as a Small World.

Ashy Bean’s expression changed, looking toward the direction of the small shop, revealing an incredulous look.

Why would Zuo Shi Immortal appear here?

She couldn’t dwell on Fairy Eternity’s matter any longer and hastily returned to the small shop.

"We should go too!" Fairy Eternity, pulling Lu Yang along, chased after Ashy Bean.

Chapter 1104: 1103: Zuo Shi Immortal’s Luck

"Zuo Shi Immortal, how can you be here?"

Qinghe was astonished; she had just taken the Complete Culinary Compendium out from the back kitchen to show Mengmeng. How did she come out and run into Zuo Shi Immortal, who had not been seen for 300,000 years?

"Qinghe?" Seeing Qinghe, who had been missing for 300,000 years, Zuo Shi Immortal was equally stunned.

He didn’t understand why Qinghe would appear here.

Over the years, he had been hiding here and there, avoiding the perception of Silent Extermination Immortal. Today, in order to make a move, he had planned for a long time to ensure that Silent Extermination Immortal couldn’t sense him, and only then did he come out from the Small World.

Qinghe was also on the kill list; how could she move around so freely and openly?

Could it be that Silent Extermination Immortal no longer killed people, or that she was already dead?

Zuo Shi Immortal couldn’t understand. Both possibilities were almost zero, but there was no other explanation for why Qinghe could be here.

Damn it, if only he still had the Insight Dao Fruit, then he wouldn’t be so passive everywhere.

"Zuo Shi, how could you dare to show up here? Silent Extermination will kill you!" Although she didn’t know why Zuo Shi Immortal appeared here, Qinghe knew that if Zuo Shi Immortal dared to show his face, he was certain to die.

"Out of old acquaintance, Qinghe, do not meddle in this matter. I will take the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit and leave!"

"Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, where?" One surprise after another prevented Qinghe from reacting promptly, and then she remembered Mengmeng was still here.

"Mengmeng, you have the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit?!"

She had always thought Mengmeng was just an ordinary cultivator she got along with!

If Mengmeng, holding the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, was a semi-immortal, then who exactly are her chief housekeeper and second-in-command?

"I can’t give you this; it took me a lot of effort to cultivate," Mengmeng said, holding onto herself and warily refusing Zuo Shi Immortal’s proposal.

"Well, that’s not up to you now!" Zuo Shi Immortal smirked viciously; he definitely wasn’t here to seek the young girl’s opinion.

"You dare to rob?!" Seeing the situation turning dire, Mengmeng tried to flee, Zuo Shi Immortal’s oppressive aura was on par with Ying Tian Corpse’s. If their strengths were also comparable, then she would surely be no match!

How could Mengmeng possibly escape in front of Zuo Shi Immortal? Putting aside the Dao Fruit Outline, Mengmeng was at the Early Tribulation Stage of her cultivation, but Zuo Shi Immortal was still an immortal, even though he had lost the Insight Dao Fruit.

Early Tribulation Stage and immortal belong to utterly different realms!

Zuo Shi Immortal reached out his large hand and grabbed Mengmeng’s delicate neck; she struggled in pain, ready to fight for the Dao Fruit Outline.

"Zuo Shi Immortal, stop!"

Qinghe raged, forcibly taking away the Dao Fruit Outline would undoubtedly lead to a semi-immortal’s death!

"Shut up!"

Zuo Shi Immortal flung Mengmeng away, knocking down Qinghe, the two piled up on each other, slamming directly into the small shop’s wall!

"Qinghe, for the sake of our old acquaintance, I won’t rob your Longevity Dao Fruit Embryo, but don’t meddle in my affairs!"

The Longevity Dao Fruit Embryo, similar to Master of All Laws’ Dao Fruit Embryo, there were two Upper Dao Fruits, which were Time Dao Fruit and Immortal Dao Fruit.

The holder of the Longevity Dao Fruit Embryo had a lifespan close to infinity; this was how Qinghe managed to stay active from Ancient Times until now.

However, unlike Master of All Laws, Qinghe possessed a nearly consummate Dao Fruit Embryo. If Time Immortal and Fairy Eternity were not around, she could become immortal.

"Eh, you’re a semi-immortal too," Mengmeng said in surprise. Xiao Zhi was right; wherever the second-in-command is, there’s always a semi-immortal.

"Is now really the time for that?" Qinghe felt that Mengmeng really had a huge heart, considering so many things in the face of a life-and-death crisis.

"What’s the deal with this shop?" Zuo Shi Immortal was puzzled. The little shop seemed ordinary, but when he had flung Mengmeng and Qinghe just now, the shop seemed not even to have a crack?

Of course, Zuo Shi Immortal didn’t know that Ashy Bean, in order not to let Qinghe leave the shop’s range, had placed restrictions there. The presence of the restriction ensured that the shop wouldn’t be destroyed.

After all, if the shop were destroyed, there would be no talk of a range of activity.

"You must run!"

Qinghe desperately sought to create an opportunity for Mengmeng to escape, activating the Longevity Dao Fruit Embryo, causing the flow of time to slow down.

But the Immortal’s speed was too fast, too fast; even as time slowed down, Zuo Shi Immortal still moved freely, keeping up with Qinghe’s actions.

"Giving you face and you don’t want it, Qingtian Prison Suppressing Hand!"

A gigantic hand descended from the sky, shattering the restrictions Ashy Bean had left behind, directly smashing the small shop to pieces, with both Yun Mengmeng and Qinghe suppressed by the great hand!

Fortunately, both of their Dao Fruit Outlines possessed extremely strong regenerative abilities. Although they appeared somewhat bedraggled, they were actually not much injured.

"What happened?"

Demon City was shocked. The competition of the Eighth Star Spirit Chef had not been over for long, how could another incident have occurred?

And this commotion was obviously bigger than what had happened during the competition of the Eighth Star Spirit Chef!

"Daring to make a move in Demon City, do you think this Emperor is dead?" Zhu Tian, seated in the Imperial Palace, was furious, flipping the table over, and Spirit Fruits scattered all over the floor.

The opponent was acting utterly lawless, not putting him, the Demon Emperor, in their eyes at all!

Yun Mengmeng immediately ran; this was not the time for everyone to face the enemy together. If she escaped, Qinghe would be safe, as Zuo Shi Immortal’s target was her.

Qinghe bit her tongue, determined to save Yun Mengmeng at all costs.

"Great Dao breeds the Chaos Green Lotus!"

A soft and enchanting voice arose, signifying the boundlessness of the Dao, as the Chaos Green Lotus was born from the Great Dao above Qinghe’s head, blooming to its utmost brilliance and beauty.

"Go!"

"I’ve said it, you can’t escape."

Zuo Shi Immortal spoke indifferently, with an unmistakable tone of certainty. After so many tens of thousands of years, he could finally make his move freely, instead of hiding in the Jade Pendant, living in constant fear, scheming this and that.

The Chaos Green Lotus slammed towards Zuo Shi Immortal, who flicked his sleeve and extended two Order Chains, piercing through the Chaos Green Lotus and binding both Qinghe and the distant Yun Mengmeng, pulling them back.

The two struggled but to no avail; the chains were crafted from Immortal Gold and engraved with countless order laws, not something they could break free from.

Daoist Ming Yuan saw the imposing figure of Zuo Shi Immortal’s dominance and couldn’t help but smile.

The struggle of the Great Era indeed required backing from an Immortal.

Snap.

Accompanying a crisp snapping of fingers, both Order Chains turned into nothingness.

Zuo Shi Immortal’s pupils contracted suddenly, this kind of unreasonable, bone-chilling tactic... was the Annihilation Dao Fruit!

"Are you alright?" A breathtaking silhouette escorted Qinghe back to the ground and asked with concern.

"I’m fine," Qinghe shook her head.

"Zuo Shi Immortal, you have quite the nerve, even daring to lay a hand on my people!"

The breathtaking silhouette comforted Qinghe, appearing before Zuo Shi Immortal. Beneath her calm eyes was an endless fury. She herself couldn’t bear to treat Qinghe like that, so where did Zuo Shi Immortal find the audacity to do so?

"Si-, Silent Extermination Immortal!" Zuo Shi Immortal saw the face that finally made him panic, his hands and feet turned cold, and he stuttered as he spoke.

"So, you are Zuo Shi Immortal, daring to lay hands on my Immortal Lineage’s people!"

Another beautiful silhouette appeared, stepping towards Zuo Shi Immortal, her words not hiding her murderous intent.

"An-, Another Silent Extermination Immortal, no, you are Immortal Fairy!"

Zuo Shi Immortal saw the Immortal Fairy, thinking she was a clone of the Silent Extermination Immortal, but as soon as he recalled the "Immortal Lineage" mentioned just now, he immediately realized her identity!

The first among the Five Ancient Immortals - Immortal Fairy!

What kind of luck was this? To avoid the Silent Extermination Immortal, he had schemed for so long, barely making a move, only to provoke both the Silent Extermination Immortal and the Immortal Fairy.

Either of these two was an imposing figure, unmatched across the world in Ancient Times!

Zuo Shi Immortal’s face squeezed out a smile uglier than crying: "What if I said I was just playing with them, would you believe me?"

Chapter 1105: 114 Little Lotus, long time no see.

Dark clouds gathered above Demon City, lightning flashing and thunder rolling, as the celestial pressure of the immortal’s wrath descended upon the city.

Zuo Shi Immortal raised his head to the suddenly changing weather above, swallowing hard. When an Immortal angers, heaven and earth change color.

He had made complete preparations and had already set up an Immortal-level Array. How had Silent Extermination Immortal sensed him?

Could it be that Silent Extermination Immortal was also in Demon City, and he had trapped himself together with Silent Extermination Immortal?!

Why, exactly, had nothing happened to Qinghe?

Did Silent Extermination Immortal just call Qinghe "my person"? Could that be the reason why Qinghe was unscathed?

"Wait, Silent Extermination Immortal, there might be some misunderstanding between us. I can surrender like Qinghe..."

Boom—

Ashy Bean’s beautiful eyes widened, leaving an afterimage in place, her true form turning into lightning, and with one kick she sent Zuo Shi Immortal flying to the boundary of the Array. Zuo Shi Immortal was kicked to a point where his guts were in disarray and his internal organs shifted.

"Master!"

The moment Qinghe saw Fairy Eternity, passionate blood surged forth. She could barely believe her eyes and the word "Master" trembled violently as she shouted it out. Memories, both familiar and vague, flooded her heart. Tears fell in large drops, impossible to wipe away.

After a separation of four hundred thousand years, the longed-for Master had finally resurrected.

It was like a dream.

"Little Lotus, I knew you were alright." Fairy Eternity turned to see Qinghe safe and sound, and couldn’t help but chuckle with joy. She had been worried that Ashy Bean would harm Qinghe.

"Little Lotus, just wait. Wait for me to deal with this Zuo Shi Immortal!"

Having just regained his senses after Ashy Bean’s kick, Zuo Shi Immortal felt the impact of an Immortal Fist Technique in his abdomen. His stomach churned as though a mighty river had overturned, and he vomited out the bitter contents.

Such a dreadful punch—that was Fairy Eternity’s fist.

Zuo Shi Immortal, having ascended to Immortalhood during the Fire Dynasty era, had not personally experienced the rule of the Five Ancient Immortals but had heard stories of Fairy Eternity from people like Ying Tian Immortal, and he knew she was a formidable presence.

Even though she had not yet fully resurrected, merely a Soul Body, he was no match for her!

Provoking these two, let alone him, even if the Four Ancient Immortals gathered together, it would be a dead end!

He must escape. He had to escape!

Ashy Bean and Fairy Eternity simultaneously attacked Zuo Shi Immortal, kicking him about like a shuttlecock, sending him flying back and forth as they vented their emotions.

At last, enduring the severe pain, Zuo Shi Immortal seized the chance to fly out of Demon City.

But only when he reached the boundary of Demon City did he remember the Immortal-level Array locked the spatial planes of the city. He had designed the Array, and it would take but three breaths to break it open.

Yet, in front of Ashy Bean and Fairy Eternity, let alone three breaths, he didn’t even have a moment to catch his breath!

Damn it, the Array he had designed with countless efforts had trapped him!

Seeing Ashy Bean and Fairy Eternity appear, Demon Emperor Zhu Tian, who was preparing to travel on eight Dragon Colts laying out a golden road, quietly tied up the Dragon Colts, squatted on the ground, and began picking up Spirit Fruits he had overturned back into the fruit tray.

Heavens, in the battle of the three Immortals, it was fortunate he hadn’t gone out. If he had, what would have become of him?

Although he did not recognize Zuo Shi Immortal, anyone worth Fairy Eternity’s effort must also be an Immortal.

Whoosh whoosh—

Heavy rain poured down, cleansing Demon City. The majestic deluge fell upon Zuo Shi Immortal, instantly evaporating with sizzling noises and the steam enshrouded him.

Zuo Shi Immortal panted heavily, his injuries visibly healing at a speed noticeable to the naked eye.

He was grateful that he had stolen the Rejuvenation Dao Fruit Embryo from Muyin Shangren, otherwise he would have been beaten to a pulp and left gasping for breath long ago.

Ashy Bean raised her eyebrows. An Immortal’s Body inherently possessed the ability to recover from injuries, but her Power of Silence had the ability to suppress healing. Even with suppression, Zuo Shi Immortal was still able to recover quickly, which indicated he had other means of healing.

But it didn’t matter, none of the methods would work in front of her.

Different from Zuo Shi Immortal who single-handedly suppressed Qinghe and Yun Mengmeng with world-shaking force, the two women’s confrontation with Zuo Shi Immortal consisted of simple punches and kicks. Their movements were plain and unadorned, embodying the principle that the Great Dao is simple, with each and every move containing the ultimate truth of the Dao.

However, the battle among the three Immortals brought an even greater sense of oppression to Demon City, weighing on everyone like a mountain. Those with insufficient Cultivation Level didn’t even have the right to watch!

"Qingtian Prison Suppressing Hand!"

Zuo Shi Immortal, risking self-mutilation and severe injury, used the Qingtian Prison Suppressing Hand. A green giant hand descended from the sky, like the hand of the heavens, its palm filled with runes representing rules and order.

"Extinguish."

Ashy Bean uttered the word "extinguish" in a chilly tone. Starting from the fingertips, the heavenly green hand disintegrated into the most primitive of runes, which then dissipated and vanished completely.

Zuo Shi Immortal took out a Talisman, ones he had prepared in advance, each Immortal-level Talisman powerful enough to kill a Semi-Immortal.

"Lotus Fire!"

A crimson fire lotus emerged, burning all sins of the world. As Ashy Bean, who had destroyed the Fire Dynasty, encountered the Lotus Fire, she would be engulfed in the karma fire, burnt until dry and withered!

"Void Illusion Realm!"

A massive illusion opened up, plunging Ashy Bean and Immortal Fairy into an illusory realm. If there was even a moment of distraction, it would buy him some time.

"Netherworld Ghost Shadow!"

Countless shadows appeared—these were forces he had accumulated in the Underworld of the Fire Dynasty. The Yin Energy carried by the million ghost soldiers threatened to shroud an Immortal in misfortune, hindering them from focusing on anything else!

"Extinguish!"

Ashy Bean was entirely unaffected by the illusion, still uttering just one word, "extinguish." The word represented the Power of Silence. In the field of Immortal Dao, it ranked at the top of the list for destruction methods. Even the Four Ancient Immortals were helpless against the Power of Silence, let alone Zuo Shi Immortal who did not possess a Dao Fruit!

Under the Power of Silence, the Lotus Fire dispersed instantly, and the million ghost soldiers were directly reduced to ashes. Zuo Shi Immortal seized the opportunity to open his Heavenly Eye. Although he had lost the Insight Dao Fruit, he still knew some techniques for probing and divining his own fate—a specialty of the Imperial Shi Clan that he had brought to new heights in his generation.

Through the Heavenly Eye, he saw himself wrapped in grey fog, a harbinger of great misfortune. It meant that in this battle, his fate would be either imprisonment or death and the utter disappearance of his Dao, with no third possibility.

He made a resolute decision, bit his tongue, and sprayed out a mouthful of Essence Blood while performing the Causality Supernatural Power, "Defying Destiny!"

This was a technique powerful enough to change his own fate, capable of turning his predestined grey future into white and snatching a slither of chance for survival!

"Fixed and Unchanging!"

Immortal Fairy used the Eternity Dao Fruit to lock Zuo Shi Immortal’s fate, rendering whatever Great Divine Skill he used incapable of changing his fate.

The Eternity Dao Fruit not only allowed Immortal Fairy to maintain her own state but could also apply the same to others!

Zuo Shi Immortal’s face fell in shock; he had never expected this move, his Defying Destiny utterly ineffective. Was this the capability of the leader of the Five Ancient Immortals!?

Ashy Bean walked out from the ashes of the million ghost soldiers, her eyes cold, looking at Zuo Shi Immortal as though he were a corpse!

Her voice, cold and commanding, with an air of undeniable authority.

"Kneel before Little Lotus."

With one step, she moved in front of Zuo Shi Immortal, grabbed his head, and pushed down forcefully, pressing him from the sky to the ground, making him kneel before Qinghe.

Immortal Fairy descended from the air, greeting with a cheerful smile, "Little Lotus, long time no see."

Chapter 1106: 1105: Founder of the Eternity Sect

"My lord, is it really you? Have you truly come back to life?" Qinghe covered her mouth with both hands, still unable to believe that her lord was truly alive.

She couldn’t help herself and stepped forward to embrace him, to confirm she wasn’t dreaming.

"It’s me, I’ve come back to life," Fairy Eternity said with a smile as she patted Qinghe’s back, gently comforting her, just as she had when she had risked her life to save Qinghe during a crisis in Ancient Times.

"My lord, I’ve waited for you for so long, I thought I’d never see you again..."

Qinghe’s speech was filled with sobs, and realizing she had overstepped, she hurriedly let go of Fairy Eternity. However, Fairy Eternity stepped forward and embraced her again, whispering softly in her ear, "Little Lotus, I’m glad to see you unhurt."

When she released Qinghe, Qinghe’s eyes were already red from crying.

Ashy Bean looked at Zuo Shi Immortal, who was kneeling on the ground, and suddenly felt very bored.

The battle had lasted only a very short time. Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng looked up to watch the battle, but before they had time to mourn for the unlucky Zuo Shi Immortal, he was beaten black and blue, kneeling on the ground begging for mercy.

While watching the battle, Yun Mengmeng and Qinghe had also conveniently captured Daoist Ming Yuan, who was trying to escape.

Lu Yang felt he had fulfilled his duty towards the Clan Leader Jin Tan for helping him forge the identity of a Five Star Spirit Chef. Although he had not followed Jin Tan’s arrangement to ask for legendary ingredients, he had helped capture Daoist Ming Yuan, which, rounding up, could be considered fulfilling Jin Tan’s wish to become the leader of the Spirit Chef Alliance.

"Chief Housekeeper, I knew you would come to save me!" Yun Mengmeng shouted as she hopped over to Fairy Eternity. She wasn’t too panicked when facing Zuo Shi Immortal earlier because she believed the Chief Housekeeper would come in time.

"Wait, is the Chief Housekeeper the ’my lord’ you spoke of?!" Qinghe asked in shock, as though she had heard a ghost story.

She should have guessed when she learned that Yun Mengmeng possessed the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, but at that time, she was too busy with rescuing others to think about it.

"So, the Second-in-command you’ve always talked about is..."

"This gentleman here, he’s our Immortal Lineage Second Boss!" said Yun Mengmeng, seriously presenting Lu Yang.

"Senior Qinghe, hello, I often hear the Fairy mention you," Lu Yang said with a sheepish grin, always feeling that Senior Qinghe’s gaze on him was somewhat dissatisfied.

Of course, Qinghe wouldn’t give Lu Yang a warm welcome. According to Yun Mengmeng’s description, Lu Yang, having the favor of their lord, would pester her all day and was full of lies.

Fortunately, their lord had a pure heart and hadn’t been tainted.

"Divinity Transformation Stage?" Qinghe raised an eyebrow, wondering how someone at the Divinity Transformation Stage had become the Second-in-command of the Immortal Lineage.

She herself was untalented and had repeatedly tried and failed to condense the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, so she had no choice but to settle for the Longevity Dao Fruit Embryo, losing the qualification to become a member of the Immortal Lineage.

How had this youngster earned such favor from their lord?

"Don’t belittle the Second-in-command just because his cultivation level is low. He had an Incense-converged Immortal Body in Buddha Country, and he was very strong. Even Si Chen God Lord and some Emperor Avatar were no match for him!" Yun Mengmeng defended Lu Yang’s honor, signaling that the Second-in-command was quite formidable.

"Immortal Body from Buddha Country, Si Chen God Lord? Are you the one who took action at Western Paradise Temple?" Qinghe was shocked, as she had assumed that battle was the work of Yun Mengmeng.

The battle at the Western Paradise Temple, that Mysterious Immortal who had the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit—did it mean he could use the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit?

"Yeah, isn’t Little Yang impressive?" Fairy Eternity said to Qinghe with delight. Qinghe saw herself as subordinated to Fairy Eternity, while Fairy Eternity considered them close friends with whom she could share anything.

"Speaking of my resurrection, it’s largely thanks to Little Yang. He joined the Eternity Sect and learned the method to revive me and unintentionally called out my name."

Qinghe’s expression became serious, realizing it was Lu Yang who had revived their lord; her attitude just now seemed somewhat disrespectful.

However, even if it was Lu Yang who had revived their lord, that didn’t justify him deceiving the lord every day with flattery.

Ashy Bean glared fiercely at Lu Yang, "It’s all because of you. If you hadn’t meddled and revived Immortal Eternity, would there be all this trouble now?"

At that thought, the grip of Ashy Bean’s hand on top of Zuo Shi Immortal’s head tightened, causing him so much pain he thought Ashy Bean might seize the opportunity to kill him.

"Don’t kill me, please don’t kill me. If that’s so, I also contributed to the resurrection of Immortal Eternity!"

"You?"

Everyone turned their heads in unison, looking at Zuo Shi Immortal with some surprise.

"It was I who created the Eternity Sect."

Zuo Shi Immortal had always been searching for a Dao Fruit Outline that could allow him to survive the hands of Silent Extermination Immortal. Even though he possessed the Rejuvenation Dao Fruit Outline, which could increase vitality, the most suitable for preserving life was undoubtedly the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit.

But the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit was not as easily obtainable as the Rejuvenation Dao Fruit Outline. The Fire Dynasty, Great Qian, and Great Yu combined over the course of three hundred thousand years could not produce the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit.

That’s why he established the Eternity Sect, hoping to condense the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit through the power of incense and fire.

Then he encountered Yun Zhi in Great Xia. After clashing with her, he fled in a sorry state, no longer daring to visit Great Xia, instead sending a clone there.

Subsequently, the Eternity Sect was destroyed, and his clone was also eliminated; he gained nothing.

But all these matters weren’t worth mentioning. It was more important to claim credit and save his own life.

"In order to resurrect Immortal Eternity, I followed the example of the Fire Dynasty and established the Eternity Sect!"

"Who are you trying to fool? If you really wanted to resurrect the Fairy, would you even mess up the Immortal’s name?" Lu Yang exposed Zuo Shi Immortal’s lie right away.

To resurrect the Fairy, one must chant the correct Immortal’s title. The immortal worshiped by the Eternity Sect was called Immortal Eternity. That wouldn’t resurrect anyone.

"You clearly established the Eternity Sect to secure the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, using the faith of incense as leverage!"

Seeing the others turning increasingly hostile toward him and his life at imminent risk, Zuo Shi Immortal, desperate to prove his worth, blurted out a secret that lay heavy on his heart.

"I know who the mastermind behind the scenes is!"

"!"

Once he said this, everyone was shocked, including Ashy Bean.

The root cause of everything was the mastermind who killed Fairy Eternity!

And until now, no one knew the real identity of the mastermind and why they wanted Fairy Eternity dead.

"My Insight Dao Fruit allows me to see clearly in all ten directions, above and below the heavens, the past, and the future."

"When I had condensed my Insight Dao Fruit, the Yingtian Immortal and his cohorts asked me to see who exactly killed Immortal Eternity."

Lu Yang remembered Ao Ling mentioning this incident, saying that despite Zuo Shi Immortal exerting himself to the point of profuse sweating with his Dao Fruit, he couldn’t discern anything significant.

Zuo Shi Immortal, trembling, said, "Actually, I did see the person behind the scenes then—it was the four of them, Yingtian Immortal and his group!"

"The four of them wanted to obtain the Immortal Dao Fruit, but Immortal Eternity was in their way. They hatched a plan to kill Immortal Eternity, to leave the Immortal’s position vacant!"

Four instigators eyed him greedily, surrounding him and questioning who the murderer was. He would have to be tired of living to dare tell the truth.

Chapter 1107: 1106: The False Past

"The mastermind behind this is the Four Ancient Immortals?" Lu Yang was initially stunned, then his expression turned queer.

So, did Zuo Shi Immortal imply that the mastermind the Fairy had been seeking was actually fabricated by the Four Ancient Immortals, and the true masterminds were those four?

Qinghe also hadn’t expected that when they had Zuo Shi Immortal inspect the murder process, the outcome he’d see was this?

"I am telling the truth!" Seeing that everyone looked different, and that they more or less didn’t believe him, Zuo Shi Immortal panicked.

Every time he recalled this incident, he broke out in a cold sweat. The Four Ancient Immortals were amiable when they questioned him and were always accommodating to his requests.

At first, he thought this was the Four Ancient Immortals showing their true feelings, but when he saw the truth of the past and reconsidered the Four Ancient Immortals, he only felt that their smiles betrayed a sense of spookiness and murderous intent.

Had it not been for his quick thinking to change his statement to not having seen anything, he would have died on the spot!

"Have you never considered that the past you saw could be false?" Lu Yang couldn’t help asking. It didn’t make sense, not to mention, were Ying Tian Immortal and the others actually conspiring against Immortal Fairy for the Eternity Dao Fruit, what were they truly after?

In Immortal Fairy’s words, if one becomes immortal, who would still pursue power? How tiring.

"I even confirmed whether what I saw was true or false by checking all the past events of the four, Ying Tian Immortal and the others!" Zuo Shi Immortal exclaimed passionately, having kept this secret for nearly four hundred thousand years, today he decided to let it all out.

"I saw the broken Firmament Tree birth the consciousness that became Time Immortal, saw Ying Tian Immortal buried as a sacrifice in a tomb, saw Qilin Immortal born from the essence of heaven and earth, saw Immortal Jiuchong born in the Human race’s great Lianshan Clan."

The origins of Time Immortal, Qilin Immortal, and Immortal Jiuchong were not secrets, only Ying Tian Immortal, whose background did not match his persona, was seldom publicized. This indicated that what Zuo Shi Immortal saw was true.

"I also saw them, despite appearing righteous and commanding, were actually full of schemes behind their backs. Different faces in private, driven solely by interests!"

"They approached you merely because they valued your talent. You alone developed the concepts of ’Dao Fruit Outline’ and ’Dao Fruit,’ probing into the ’Immortal’ realm. This talent is unrivaled in history!"

"They even feared you becoming an immortal first. Time Immortal thus persuaded you to give up the Annihilation Dao Fruit and switch to the Eternity Dao Fruit, intending to trap you in this event, preventing you from becoming immortal. Unexpectedly, you let go of your obsession and formed the Eternity Dao Fruit, making them envious and murderous towards you. That’s why they cruelly attacked you!"

Lu Yang frowned, what was going on here? Zuo Shi Immortal’s accounts sounded like conspiracy theories about the Four Ancient Immortals.

"Wait, did you just say ’Dao Fruit’ was something Fairy figured out on her own?"

Lu Yang turned to ask Immortal Fairy, "Fairy, I remember you mentioned that ’Dao Fruit’ was a result deduced by the five of you?"

Both Ashy Bean and Qinghe remembered this. The past Zuo Shi Immortal saw had issues!

"That’s impossible!" Zuo Shi Immortal was dismayed. He would definitely not forget such earth-shattering deductions as the ’Dao Fruit.’

Lu Yang spoke solemnly, "If it truly was the Four Immortals who acted, how did they make the whole world forget about the Fairy?"

"They certainly have methods I’m unaware of!" Zuo Shi Immortal was unwilling to admit his mistake.

Facing Zuo Shi Immortal’s non-answer, Lu Yang had a speculation, "The past you saw is not the true past but a false past manipulated by the real mastermind!"

However, Lu Yang didn’t understand, why would the real mastermind fabricate a past with such a significant loophole as ’Dao Fruit was realized by the Fairy alone’? Was there some unavoidable reason?

Could it be...

"Fairy, if Ying Tian Immortal and the Four Immortals truly had the character Zuo Shi Immortal described, what would you do?"

"What would I do? Of course, stay away from them." Immortal Fairy stated matter-of-factly. She could distinguish who was good and who was bad.

"Then, if it came to deducing the Dao Fruit, would you still invite those four to participate?"

Immortal Fairy pondered what she would do, then answered very affirmatively.

"No, I would deduce it alone. Even if it’s just me, I should be able to deduce the outcome."

"That makes sense then. The past that Zuo Shi Immortal saw was based on the assumption of the personality shift of the Ying Tian Four!" Lu Yang asserted confidently.

However, Lu Yang still hadn’t figured out how the mastermind managed this; only a Dao Fruit could influence the outcomes observed by the Insight Dao Fruit.

"Could this be something that the Cause and Effect Dao Fruit can achieve?"

"How can this be possible? Is there really a mastermind?" At this moment, Zuo Shi Immortal felt his worldview being overturned—the nature of the Ying Tian Four was not as malicious as he thought; were they genuinely without ill intentions?

"So the person who threatened me back then was not the Ying Tian Four but the mastermind?"

"Threatened you?"

"The very night after this incident ended, someone waited for me in my palace when I returned. He demanded I destroy my Dao Fruit. If I refused, he said he would kill me!"

Thinking back to that figure, uncontrollable fear spread in Zuo Shi Immortal’s heart: "The aura he emitted, he was definitely capable of killing me!"

"Who was that person?"

"I don’t know; he did not show his face. I always thought he was one of the Four Immortals. But seeing as the past I saw was fabricated, if there really was a mastermind, then it was the person who threatened me!"

"I was coerced by him, forced to give up on obtaining insight from the Insight Dao Fruit and ultimately destroying the Insight Dao Fruit."

"You don’t have the Insight Dao Fruit anymore?"

"Yes."

Zuo Shi Immortal gave a bitter smile. With the Insight Dao Fruit, which illuminates the ten directions, had he still possessed it, he would have been able to locate Silent Extermination Immortal and wouldn’t have had the misfortune of acting in Demon City.

"Then, what exactly was the Great Era conflict you predicted?" Qinghe asked, referring to the term ’Great Era conflict’ which originated from Zuo Shi Immortal. He had spoken of a Great Era conflict occurring after three hundred and ninety thousand years.

Zuo Shi Immortal sighed, "Although I lost the Insight Dao Fruit, I am inherently skilled at divination. Though it’s not as effective as the Insight Dao Fruit, the divination results are still very good."

"I divined that three hundred and ninety thousand years later, an opportunity to become immortal would arise. Various eras of Semi-Immortals and Immortals emerging to battle, chaos between heaven and earth, with some becoming immortals and others perishing."

"However, my divination results are not entirely accurate—for instance, according to my divination, the Great Era conflict should have already started. Various forces should’ve started clashing by now; the world shouldn’t be this peaceful."

"Another instance is when I divined that by founding Eternity Sect, I would obtain the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit. As you all saw, I wasn’t successful."

Lu Yang’s expression grew increasingly strange. It seemed, had he not found the headquarters of Eternity Sect back then, Zuo Shi Immortal might have succeeded.

Chapter 1108: 1107: Immortal Fairy, The Battle in the Heavens!

"Heh heh, so there really is a puppet master behind the scenes..."

Zuo Shi Immortal’s shoulders shook, and he laughed in fits and starts, tilting his head to let the rain wash over his face.

Ever since he became an Immortal nearly four hundred thousand years ago, he had considered the Four Ancient Immortals as his hypothetical enemies. When the Fire Dynasty fell, there was an unspeakable satisfaction in his heart. They wanted to establish the Fire Dynasty, to create the Eternity Dao Fruit through the Power of Faith, but instead, they created a monster like the Silent Extermination Immortal, causing the Time Immortal to fall and the other three Immortals to flee in disarray. It was really karma.

Therefore, when he divined the future appearance of the Silent Extermination Immortal, he did not inform the Four Ancient Immortals, but rather looked forward to the drama with a sense of anticipation.

Although he was also on the run from being hunted, whenever he thought of the Four Immortals receiving the same treatment as himself, his mood considerably improved.

Unexpectedly, from the very beginning, the puppet master had given him a false past, turning him into someone distrustful of gods and ghosts, trusting no one.

"Truly worthy of being the puppet master..."

If he had informed the Four Ancient Immortals of the divination results back then, would the current situation be any different?

After all his scheming, what had he gained?

"Are you done talking?"

Ashy Bean asked coldly, not giving anyone a chance to react. A grey flame symbolizing death and withering appeared in the palm of her hand, burning only within her palm, causing the space to begin to collapse.

The grey flame instantly enveloped Zuo Shi Immortal. Smoke issued from his seven orifices as he screamed in agony.

"Stop!" Fairy Eternity shouted, but it was already too late. The grey flame turned Zuo Shi Immortal into ash, just like the Ying Tian Corpse had been.

"What are you doing!"

Ashy Bean looked coldly at her other self, "To cut off causality, to approach transcendence, to confront the puppet master—Yingtian Immortal and the others resurrected me for this very purpose, did they not?"

"To handle the puppet master, I alone am sufficient; your existence is superfluous."

"For that, you destroyed the entire Fire Dynasty?" Fairy Eternity demanded, shaking with rage, unable to contain her fury.

"It was a necessary sacrifice."

"It was an unnecessary sacrifice!" Fairy Eternity cried, her eyes welling with tears, as she thought of the dynasty’s destruction, the historical gaps, and the erasure of a hundred thousand years of history. Just how many had to die to reach such an extent?

Everything had started because of her. Would it be alright if she stopped resurrecting and seeking revenge?

I’m not that important!

"Oh right, Yingtian Immortal and the others resurrected me, and they also wanted me to become you." Ashy Bean shook her head and revealed a mocking smile, mocking the circumstances of her own origin. How ridiculous it was.

"You are not me."

"Yes, you’re so lucky, with the Eternity Dao Fruit, fearless of any danger, living carefree, with so many people liking you, worrying about you, and going out of their way to resurrect you. I’m just a nobody without even a name—how could I become you?"

"But if I don’t become you, why would I appear in this world, and how would Little Lotus truly acknowledge me?"

Ashy Bean’s expression became stern, staring at the real self, no longer hiding her killing intent, speaking through clenched teeth, "If I don’t kill you, how can I become you?"

"I’ve said it before, I will definitely kill you, to prove that I’m the real you!"

"Don’t..." Qinghe rushed forward in desperation, but Ashy Bean dismissed her with a Restriction, freezing her in place.

Ashy Bean took a deep look at Qinghe, no longer hiding her presence. Blood boiled in her veins, and from head to toe, she radiated an ominous glow. With a powerful leap, she dispelled the rainclouds.

The torrential downpour had started because of the two of them, and now it was dispersed by them.

If it hadn’t been for the interference of Zuo Shi Immortal, Ashy Bean would have challenged Fairy Eternity after their stroll and heartfelt talk through the streets!

Today, she and Fairy Eternity were destined to fight to the death!

"Immortal Fairy, to the heavens for a battle!"

"Come!"

Fairy Eternity, too, harbored a murderous intent toward her. Her soul shone brightly, like an eternal brilliance that would never extinguish!

...

"Has the Battle of Three Immortals ended?" Zhu Tian asked tremulously, not expecting an answer but talking to himself out of fear, finding solace in his own words to calm his nerves.

Boom!

A dull colliding sound echoed, the entire Demon City shook, and the Array Method that enveloped the Demon City shattered into pieces in an instant during the collision.

The noise reminded Zhu Tian of a scene from the Ancient Times when the Four Ancient Immortals sparred, meteors poured down like rain, and the stars in the sky shattered. No, this incident was even grander than the sparring of Immortals.

Zhu Tian was terrified, comparable to the ultimate battle between the Four Ancient Immortals and some unknown beings!

What on earth has happened, that battle was supposed to be the highest-level one ever!

Zhu Tian stepped out of the Imperial Palace, looked up at the sky, and saw two figures with identical faces but utterly different auras clashing, each collision shaking the earth itself!

These two fighting were causing a far greater disturbance than the earlier Battle of Three Immortals!

Zhu Tian’s mouth twitched, it’s one thing that the Great Era conflict started because of me, but does it have to happen on my turf?

This is really beyond my control.

"Uncle Zhu Tian, what’s happening?"

Jin Caiwei hurried from the Qiongqi Clan to ask, as she had been awoken by the commotion from her deep sleep.

Zhu Tian was from the same generation as the old Qiongqi, so by generation, Jin Caiwei had to call him uncle.

"Look at the sky."

When Jin Caiwei saw the two Fairy Eternities, she was equally astonished, wondering what had happened during the time she was unconscious.

"This can’t be good, Demon City is in danger!" Jin Caiwei and Zhu Tian both made the same judgment.

Fairy Eternity and Ashy Bean had no intention to hurt the innocent and tried to take their fight to the skies as much as possible, but a battle of this scale could cause Demon City to fall with just one stray attack, and no Array Method could stop it.

Zhu Tian was powerless, this was akin to an apocalypse.

"I have a way," Jin Caiwei shouted, flying at a low altitude.

The battle above was far from her, but the spatial tremors created by the fight were even affecting her, shaking her flying!

"Compress it!"

She used the Plane Dao Fruit Outline to flatten the entire Demon City into a plane, then adjusted its size so that its length and width were both compressed to an inch long, and the vast Demon City lay on Jin Caiwei’s palm.

"Uncle Zhu Tian, pacify the citizens, I’ll go see what’s happening!"

Zhu Tian’s Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Outline could keep the Demon City’s citizens in order, preventing chaos that could lead to casualties and property damage.

When Jin Caiwei flattened the Demon City, there were a few individuals unaffected.

Lu Yang and the others were easy to find, as the original site of the Demon City was left with a square, deep pit, and only three people were inside it.

"Greetings, Senior Jin."

"Young Sect Master, what is going on?"

Sure enough, the Young Sect Master was unaffected.

"It’s not something I can explain in a few words," Lu Yang sighed.

"Qinghe sister, why are you here too?" Jin Caiwei was especially surprised to see Qinghe, who had looked after her a lot when she was little.

Qinghe looked up at the sky, silent.

"Second-in-command, hurry up and call Xiao Zhi over!" Yun Mengmeng said anxiously, she still did not understand what had happened and how the fight had started.

What does Fire Dynasty mean, and why are there two Chief Housekeepers?

Lu Yang shook his head: "Just now the Fairy conveyed a message to me, saying that since the incident started because of her, it should end with her, and no one else should interfere."

Chapter 1109: 1108: Little Yang, I Really Can’t Bear to Leave You

Boom!

The battle completely erupted, with two supreme Immortals clashing; the Power of Silence colliding with the Immortal Power, obliterating time and space, distorting reality.

Ashy Bean bathed in gray flames, with space collapsing and the void shattering around her. This was the World-Ending Fire, burning both the Immortal Body and Immortal Soul into exhaustion, never to be extinguished. Should she wish it, even the vast East Sea could be scorched dry by the destructive flames.

To battle her, the most feared thing was to be tainted by the Destructive Flames, which clung to one’s being like incurable gangrene. Once an Immortal Body was contaminated, it demanded a bloody price to cleanse.

Yingtian Immortal lost his physical body, largely because he encountered this flame while fighting Ashy Bean!

Yet, Fairy Eternity faced her without fear, choosing to confront Ashy Bean head-on. She landed a punch on Ashy Bean’s face, but was also smeared with the World-Ending Fire.

Fairy Eternity, with her undying Immortal Soul, allowed the World-Ending Fire to burn; it could not harm her in the slightest!

"Not enough strength, Immortal," Ashy Bean, with her skewed face resisting Fairy Eternity’s punch, slowly straightened, finding the blow barely an itch.

"Fearing not the World-Ending Fire, truly worthy of the Eternity Dao Fruit."

Every movement of the two Immortals changed the heavens and the earth; chaos surged, Immortal light burst forth, their attacks like tempests, striking from the ground up to the heavens, and then throughout the universe. The illusory stars trembled ceaselessly, on the verge of shattering!

...

"This tremor..."

In the Imperial Palace Forbidden Land of Great Xia, Meng Junzi and Jiang Pingan both stood up, displaying expressions of disbelief.

"Is this direction the Demon Territory?!"

Exchanging a look, both were shocked. The Demon Territory was so far from the Great Xia Palace, yet they could sense the fluctuations of the battle. Who was it that was fighting?

When Meng Junzi fought Ashy Bean in Buddha Country, even Jiang Pingan who was stationed in Great Xia didn’t catch wind of the movement; it was only discovered afterward!

"So, it has finally come to a head?" Yun Zhi, while picking leaves from the Enlightenment Tree in the medicinal garden, felt the distant fluctuations of the battle. Without needing to check, she could guess it was the battle between two senior Fairies.

After picking the leaves, she leisurely returned to Sky Gate Mountain to brew and savor her tea.

"Such a battle for the ages is a pity to miss. Grey Immortal carries the fate of her senior precursor, blessed with the ability to turn danger into safety. Only when she faces her precursor in battle, with their destinies clashing, can she be oblivious to whether the outcome of this battle is victory or defeat."

"Junior Brother hasn’t called for me either, probably because my senior predecessor doesn’t wish for me to interfere with this matter."

After an engagement in the East Sea and several subsequent trials, Yun Zhi understood that to resolve the issue with Grey Immortal, it required the senior Fairy to act.

"Little Yun, has there been an Immortal Battle?"

Vast Sea Daoist Lord also sensed the battle above Demon City, rushing to Yun Zhi to inquire about the situation, yet he couldn’t discern how many Immortals were involved.

"Yes."

"Shall we go take a look?" Vast Sea Daoist Lord was itching with curiosity—such Immortal Battles were a rare sight in several lifetimes. He had missed the battle in Imperial City, and this time would not let it pass by again.

He roughly calculated the coordinates of the battlefield and opened a Spatial Gate, only to find that the gate had barely formed when it dissipated.

"What is this..." Vast Sea Daoist Lord’s pupils shrunk suddenly; he had never encountered such a phenomenon before.

Yun Zhi finished a cup of tea, poured another, and kindly invited Vast Sea Ancestor to sit down for tea, suggesting, "My advice is not to go there. The space at the battle site is completely chaotic, making it impossible to teleport."

...

"Self-Detonation!"

Continual massive explosions occurred in the cosmos, with fire shooting up into the sky; it was already night, but it was as bright as day.

Ashy Bean suffered a heavy blow, staining the sky with blood, but her injuries were rapidly healing!

"Having a soul without a body, how much of your true strength can you even wield!" Ashy Bean arrogantly proclaimed, striding forward. She could quickly recover from injuries solely because her physical body was excessively strong, naturally possessing self-healing strength, not needing any Dao Fruit at all.

She faced the power of the self-detonation and walked straight into the explosion’s center, seizing the moment Fairy Eternity was restoring her physical body, to throw a punch.

"Immortal Killing Fist!"

This was a fighting technique uniquely created by Ashy Bean, stemming from the Annihilation Dao Fruit, unrivaled in its execution, capable of causing cosmic upheavals. The Power of Silence contained within it was boundless and could obliterate all traces of life. Even one’s lifespan would rapidly deplete, resulting in the Heavenly Person Five Decays, leading ultimately to death from old age!

Although Immortal Fairy was not lacking in vitality, and the effect of obliterating vitality was futile on her, setting that effect aside, the punch still carried enough force to shatter worlds, sending her flying directly!

Ashy Bean’s attacks were relentless, giving Immortal Fairy no chance to breathe, blasting her from the cosmos down to the sixth continent—the Wuling Continent!

This continent seemed like a replica of the Central Continent, with mountains and rivers, lakes and seas, lush and verdant, yet without Spiritual Energy or living beings, an utterly primitive and pure place.

"All things wither away!" Ashy Bean emitted a thick scent of death, causing plants to wither wherever she passed, mountains to collapse into flatland, rivers to dry up, deserts to expand and then all to turn to ash and vanish into nothingness.

The Power of Silence, reducing all to nothingness.

"Permanence, unchanging!" Immortal Fairy shouted, locking down this area, plunging herself and this place into an "eternal" state where no external force could induce change.

"It really is a troublesome ability. No wonder you gave up the Annihilation Dao Fruit for the Eternity Dao Fruit when you were a Semi-Immortal," Ashy Bean said coldly. Among all her opponents, the Eternity Dao Fruit was the most difficult to deal with, or rather, the Eternity Dao Fruit was an existence entirely opposite to her own.

"If this is all you are capable of, you might as well die!"

...

"What’s the situation with the battle?"

Lu Yang and the others anxiously awaited the outcome of the fight. After Immortal Fairy and Ashy Bean took the battle to the cosmos, it was unclear who held the advantage. By the time they fought down to the Wuling Continent, their traces of battle were completely lost.

Boom!

A figure was struck down, plummeting straight to the ground, creating a crater in the earth as vast as Demon City.

"Fairy!"

Lu Yang cried out excitedly. The person who was struck down was actually the Fairy!

"Don’t come over!" The Immortal Fairy yelled, now entwined with the Power of Silence. Even a slight touch by Little Yang would be fatal!

Ashy Bean landed and stepped closer to Immortal Fairy, who was lying on the ground.

"I could spare your life for Little Lotus’s sake, but all others, except you, must die."

Ashy Bean turned her head towards Lu Yang, her eyes filled with a murder intent so thick it could not be dissolved, "Especially you, aware of my existence, using me to handle the Ying Tian Corpse."

Immortal Fairy staggered to her feet, blocking Ashy Bean’s path, her eyes fierce: "Little Yang is under my protection; nobody is allowed to touch him!"

She turned her head to look at Lu Yang, offering a tragic smile.

"Little Yang, I truly can’t bear to leave you."

"What are you going to do, Fairy!" Lu Yang, horrified, wanted desperately to rush forward and intervene.

But it was too late. Immortal Fairy seemed to have made a certain determination; her soul began to shine, previously dim, now surging with vitality.

No, it wasn’t just surging—her soul had completely transformed into a physical body!

Ashy Bean watched, stunned, as Immortal Fairy created a fleshly body out of the Eternity Dao Fruit?!

Immortal Fairy stared intently into Ashy Bean’s shocked eyes, enunciating every word, "I didn’t want to come to this point. You forced my hand!"

Chapter 1110: 109 Name

Fairy Eternity obtained a physical body in that moment, and Ashy Bean felt as though facing a formidable enemy.

Could the opponent actually have obtained the ability to gain a physical body!?

But that was also fine, dealing with an Immortal Fairy who only had a soul, even if one won, there was no sense of achievement.

"Immortal Killing Fist!"

"Immortal Fist Technique!"

Both figures vanished from their spots at the same time. Ashy Bean was fast with her punches, but Fairy Eternity was even faster; a punch landed on Ashy Bean’s abdomen, causing such pain that Ashy Bean spat out a mouthful of Immortal Blood.

The Immortal Blood corroded the earth, sizzling as it smoked, penetrating from the surface to the depths below, only stopping when it encountered the barrier set up by the Four Immortals.

In the blink of an eye, they exchanged thousands of blows, and even Qinghe couldn’t see the course of their fight, only able to determine their location by the direction of the shattered space.

Even so, Qinghe couldn’t keep up with the rhythm and was always one step behind.

If even Qinghe was like this, not to mention the others.

Ashy Bean was beaten into a continuous retreat, her arms numbing from the vibrations; Fairy Eternity’s strength was astonishingly great, to the point that Ashy Bean was slightly overpowered in terms of strength!

In the past, when Fairy Eternity fought with Ashy Bean, she rarely threw punches, knowing that in terms of strength, her soul alone was no match for Ashy Bean.

But now that she had regained a physical body, she no longer had this concern.

"So this is your true strength, Immortal!" exclaimed Ashy Bean, who had never tasted defeat since her appearance, and had never even been at a disadvantage; this was the first time she encountered a being who transcended her own physical form!

"This is more like it, only then will I feel a sense of achievement when I win!" Faced with the Immortal Fairy who obtained a physical body, Ashy Bean was not afraid at all. She and the Immortal Fairy were originally one being, having condensed the Invincible Elixir, unbeatable in life, with absolute confidence in her own strength!

"Arhat Fruit Fist, Gancao Fist, Goji Fist, Angelica Fist..." Fairy Eternity threw punches in succession, each one different from the last, diverse and unpredictable.

The Immortal Fist Technique could transform into medicine, morph into myriad herbs, brimming with continuous life force, rejuvenating this region, resisting the Power of Silence.

Similarly, it could also inject the power of medicinal herbs into the opponent’s body, interfering with the flow of their meridians.

"Five Elements Great Style!"

This was an Escape Skill, as well as a Rapid Method. Ashy Bean danced like the Dragon and Phoenix, her speed nearing that of light itself, even faster than Meng Junzi who had borrowed the speed of an unformed Dao Fruit Outline!
