Lu Yang consumed two chess pieces and began his retreat.

In terms of sword techniques, Lu Yang’s were unmatched in realms, even formidable practitioners in the Body Integration Realm like the Sword Tower Master were only slightly better than him.

But whether it was One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand or Ten Thousand Swords Returning to Origin, they were pre-existing techniques. He lacked something of his own.

He morphed into a Swordmanship furnace, with basic moves, One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand, and Ten Thousand Swords Returning to Origin flashing through his mind, along with all formidable enemies he had faced so far.

As Lu Yang secluded himself in his abode to delve into his studies, the noise from Ao Ling and others playing chess quieted, fearing they might disturb his thoughts.

In reality, they worried too much; at that moment, Lu Yang was completely focused on Swordmanship, undistracted by the outside world.

He was like swimming in the ocean of Swordmanship, searching for a Sword Technique unique to himself.

Suddenly, an inspiration struck, and he grasped it, immediately finding a direction.

This spark of inspiration, like a seed, took root and sprouted in Lu Yang’s heart; he nourished it with all his knowledge of Swordmanship, allowing it to grow strong.

Time was untraceable; when Lu Yang opened his eyes, they seemed to hold two Immortal Swords, too dazzling to behold, "It’s done!"

"Old Meng, thinking you’re the only one who can create techniques?"

"Fairy, what do you think I should name this set of sword techniques?" Lu Yang wasn’t good at naming and sought Fairy Eternity’s opinion.

"Um~ Since this is the dynasty’s first self-created Sword Technique, call it Soy Sword Techniques!"

"Alright, I’ll listen to the Fairy. Let’s name it Immortal Sword Technique!" Though he wasn’t adept at naming, any name that came after Fairy Eternity’s suggestion seemed perfect.

Chapter 1120: 1119: Soy Sword Techniques

"Fairy Eternity was fuming, saying ’I was talking about the Soy Sword Techniques!’" Her essence, qi, and spirit all seemed much improved.

Lu Yang chose to ignore Fairy Eternity, stretched lazily, and felt that this period of closed-door cultivation had lasted quite a while. He was mentally exhausted, but finally having created his own sword technique was still worth celebrating.

He just didn’t know how much time had passed.

When he pushed open the door of his abode, he found Old Ma still playing Go with Dragon and Phoenix, Yun Mengmeng was still baking little biscuits, and Jin Caiwei was still studying the Recording Orb.

"I must have been in closed-door cultivation for a long time... right?"

There was no change outside the abode from before he started to after he finished, making Lu Yang doubt he had been in closed-door cultivation at all.

"Forget it, it’s not important. I need to find Old Meng!"

Meng Jingzhou had shown off to him before; now it was time to show off in return.

Lu Yang grabbed a handful of freshly baked biscuits and happily went to find Old Meng.

Only his spiritual space was filled with Fairy Eternity muttering "Soy Sword Techniques," which was somewhat distracting for Lu Yang.

At the entrance to Meng Jingzhou’s abode, Old Ma was balancing on two legs, walking upright, setting up a drying rack, and placing bundles of quality hay on it.

Although he could use True Fire to quickly dry the hay, that method produced hay with a slightly off taste, lacking the scent of nature, and couldn’t compare to natural hay dried by the sun and moon.

"Is Old Meng still in closed-door cultivation?" Lu Yang saw the door to Meng Jingzhou’s abode was tightly shut, with only Old Ma drying the hay. From a distance, it looked somewhat scary.

"Let me help you." Lu Yang brushed the biscuit crumbs from his hands and helped Old Ma set up the drying rack.

When Meng Jingzhou came out of closed-door cultivation, he found almost no place to stand at the entrance of his abode; it was all drying racks.

"Aha, finally out," said Lu Yang, swaying the Green Peak Sword, timing it perfectly just as he finished helping Old Ma with the hay.

"You came to find me proactively, so you must have created a technique too?" Meng Jingzhou didn’t need Lu Yang to say anything, just by looking at Lu Yang’s smug expression, he guessed the outcome.

"Just so happens my Supreme Fist Technique is also crafted to the second level—the Nine Earth Realm!"

Meng Jingzhou thought the Digital Immortal Skill of Immortal Jiuchong was really useful. He was able to find both the name of his fist technique and the name of the realm within it, and could just take it and use it directly.

"Let’s fight!"

"Let’s!"

The two, with good intentions, chose not to fight at the entrance of the abode to avoid damaging Old Ma’s drying hay, and went to a place a little further away.

Meng Jingzhou took two deep breaths, his mind state calm and undisturbed, as he looked down on Lu Yang, appearing utterly confident.

"Supreme Fist alone stands above all!"

His fist turned golden again, rampaging in all directions. Even the geniuses of history were no match for him, and even Lu Yang felt the pressure.

Lu Yang turned the pressure into motivation, displaying sword skills that had never been seen before in the world. His Sword Qi was like a rainbow, dazzling and bright, indestructible. The sword light was interwoven, forming a terrifying net in heaven and earth, impenetrable, and it enveloped Meng Jingzhou.

"Good!" Meng Jingzhou’s muscles bulked up, with beads of sweat the size of beans oozing out and rapidly evaporating into mist. His footsteps, of unknown origin, pressured with each step. His deadly fists became Fist Seals, and he clashed with Lu Yang’s net in a do-or-die battle.

He swung his fists vigorously, shattering thousands of sword lights.

"What a powerful sword technique, what’s this move called?"

Without thinking, Lu Yang blurted out, "Soy Sword Techniques!"

As soon as he said it, he realized something was amiss. Fairy Eternity had been whispering ’Soy Sword Techniques’ in his ear since he left closed-door cultivation. Although he tried hard not to think about those four words, when someone asked, he would subconsciously respond in that way.

In his spiritual space, Fairy Eternity cheered; Little Yang really thought the name she picked was better.

As the historian, Qinghe diligently recorded everything.

According to the "Dou Book·Emperor Chronicles": When Emperor Lu Yang first created his sword technique, Emperor Dou bestowed the name ’Soy Sword Techniques.’ The Emperor harbored doubts and did not dare to accept the decree at first. Over time, after deep contemplation of the Emperor’s intent, he finally grasped the essence of the sword technique, which truly matched the bestowed name. He then realized the intention and changed his previous thoughts, honoring the decree and calling it Soy Sword Techniques.

"No, this move is called the Immortal Sword..."

"Great, the Soy Sword Techniques! Take this!" Meng Jingzhou didn’t give Lu Yang a chance to explain, seizing the opportunity to push his fist technique to the Nine Layer Realms.

Sword light surged simultaneously, slashing towards Meng Jingzhou, creating chaos in heaven and earth. Meng Jingzhou swung his fists like rain, crushing the sword light with overwhelming force.

"Cough cough—" Facing the Nine Layer Realms Fist, Lu Yang obviously struggled, with his newly comprehended Immortal Sword Technique appearing somewhat immature compared to the well-hammered and refined Supreme Fist.

"Don’t think you’ve won just yet. My Swordsmanship has a total of twelve styles. I’ve only used the first one so far!"

"Then use the next eleven Sword Skills if you can!"

"Then use the Fist of the nine realms if you can!"

The two glared at each other, the subsequent moves being bluffs just for show, impossible to come up with on the spot.

"Bring it on!"

Meng Jingzhou’s eyes flashed like lightning, facing the overwhelming Swordsmanship with an unmoved expression, his every punch able to shatter the sword light, even blasting away the Green Peak Sword itself!

Terrifying fluctuations swept across heaven and earth like a tsunami, rushing in all directions.

The Immortal Sword Technique was too immature, and Lu Yang quickly found himself on the defensive, becoming the one who blocked!

Lu Yang calmly reflected on the flaws of the Immortal Sword Technique. The Immortal Sword Technique was a peerless Swordsmanship he had forged with all his Swordsmanship insights, but was this truly the best move he could comprehend?

No, that wasn’t right, he shouldn’t limit himself to Swordsmanship alone; he hadn’t yet extrapolated his adept magic skills. This wasn’t his best move yet.

With this realization, Lu Yang took a big leap back, aided by the force of Meng Jingzhou’s fists, throwing the Green Peak Sword aside, opting instead to face Meng Jingzhou barehanded.

Meng Jingzhou laughed, "Old Lu, are you too confident in the strength of your body? Although your physique can rival that of the Body Integration Realm, it’s still not on par with me!"

Facing Lu Yang, Meng Jingzhou didn’t need to hold back. His Supreme Fist became even more ferocious.

As soon as they clashed, Meng Jingzhou noticed something unusual about Lu Yang. His body was wrapped in Sword Qi, stinging like needles upon impact.

You think this will fend off this young master?

Meng Jingzhou’s golden fists shone even brighter, like they were cast from gold, surrounded by runes, capable of toppling mountains and splitting the seas!

Lu Yang’s movements became increasingly strange, as if he was integrating Swordsmanship completely. Meng Jingzhou felt as if he was not battling Lu Yang but a sword.

"Not right!" Meng Jingzhou suddenly realized what Lu Yang was trying to do, but he was powerless to stop it.

"Green Peak Sword Imitation Fist!" Lu Yang executed the Imitation Fist, transforming into the likeness of the Green Peak Sword!

Although he could wield the Green Peak Sword as if it were an extension of his arm, after all, it was an external object and not truly a part of his body.

Now that Lu Yang had become the Green Peak Sword, he fully unleashed the entire power of the Immortal Sword Technique!

"Sword Being!" Meng Jingzhou came up with a name for the technique.

"Bullshit, this is called Body as Sword!" Lu Yang erupted in anger, charging at Meng Jingzhou with his head.

Confronted with Body as Sword Lu Yang, Meng Jingzhou felt immense pressure. This technique was indeed terrifying, the power of the Swordsmanship increased by a notch, forcing him back on the defensive once more.

Although the offensive and defensive roles switched, Meng Jingzhou was not flustered but instead reflected on where the problem lay. If Old Lu could temporarily enhance the power of his Swordsmanship, then there was no reason he couldn’t do the same!

He had an inkling, a feeling that he hadn’t fully unleashed the Supreme Fist’s entire power.

Indeed, his Mind State wasn’t quite at the level of Supreme, always falling short by a bit.

It was this very shortfall that made his Fist lack dominance.

Realizing this, Meng Jingzhou took a deep breath twice, his demeanor completely transforming, as if a deity had descended, regarding all beings as mere ants, uttering the forbidden words passed down from Ancient Times.

"I am born in accordance with heaven. Those who defy this Immortal shall be slain!"

Clang!

Suddenly, Meng Jingzhou’s Fist burst forth with an indescribable dominance, repelling Lu Yang.

Meng Jingzhou’s heart leaped with joy, for indeed, quoting from the "Immortal’s Quotations" worked!

Reciting the Immortal’s Quotations changed his entire aura!

Body as Sword Lu Yang once again clashed with Meng Jingzhou.

Not far away, Old Ma leaned against a tree, chewing on straw, watching the inhuman Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou uttering nonsensical phrases, thinking that their fighting methods were perhaps a little perverse?

Chapter 1121: 1120: Mutual Progress, Joint Breakthrough

"One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand!"

Lu Yang became the sword; with a flicker of his body, he produced countless Green Peak Swords, indistinguishable between real and fake, forming a Sword Cage that imprisoned Meng Jingzhou within.

"Mere mortals dare to touch me, death!" Meng Jingzhou responded domineeringly, his black hair flying wildly, exuding an aura that surpassed this realm, his fist sending a large swath of Green Peak Swords flying.

The Green Peak Swords were unending, inexhaustible; no matter how many Meng Jingzhou shattered, new ones would replenish and launch fresh assaults on him.

What Meng Jingzhou failed to notice was that the Green Peak Sword Lu Yang had become was stealthily mixed in a spot extremely close to him, not attacking, but drawing circles around him.

"Draw a Prison!"

Meng Jingzhou secretly muttered trouble; he had fallen for that grandson Lu Yang’s ploy and suddenly felt his body sink, trapped in an underground cell surrounded by pitch-blackness, unable to discern up from down or left from right.

Boom, boom, boom—

The impregnable prison’s interior was suddenly marked by countless Fist Seals and then exploded, Meng Jingzhou bursting forth from the ground!

But what greeted Meng Jingzhou was not light, but a slash from Lu Yang!

Lu Yang struck head-on, and Meng Jingzhou clasped his hands together, firmly catching the tip of the sword, effectively seizing Lu Yang’s head.

Lu Yang twisted his body; if Meng Jingzhou didn’t let go, his hands would be cut by the sharp sword edge!

Meng Jingzhou immediately realized this and, instead of releasing, chose to spin in sync with Lu Yang, man and sword revolving rapidly in the air, spinning countless times before touching down to a halt.

In the end, because the Immortal Sword Technique was too immature, Lu Yang was one step behind, narrowly defeated by Meng Jingzhou.

"Ha ha, how about that, Old Lu, I won, didn’t I?" Finally beating that grandson Lu Yang for once, Meng Jingzhou was in high spirits. Had he been in Imperial City, he would have thrown a three-day feast, inviting guests from all directions.

"Just wait, when I figure out the second move, I’ll come to find you!" Lu Yang retorted and went back to shut himself away for intense cultivation.

...

"Slash!"

Lu Yang had grasped the second move of the Immortal Sword Technique and immediately sought out Meng Jingzhou to settle the score. After an intense battle, Lu Yang’s final slash broke through Meng Jingzhou’s Barrier Door, causing Meng Jingzhou to falter and be defeated.

"Hey, Old Meng, how could you lose after just a few rounds? Your fist technique is lacking." With the upper hand regained, Lu Yang felt relieved. Were he in Buddha Country, he would invite Old Meng to eat for three days straight.

"Pah, just you wait, I’m about to elevate my fist technique to the Eight Desolate Realm!"

Meng Jingzhou immediately returned to his closed-door cultivation.

...

"My fists alone are supreme!"

"Old Lu, not to speak ill of you, but your swordsmanship isn’t cutting it; it can’t even withstand my fist."

"You just wait, next time you’re definitely going to lose; I’ve already found a flaw in your sword technique, just watch as my twelve moves of swordsmanship scare you to death!"

...

"You just wait, you’ve only won this once; I already know how to fix the flaws in my fist technique, next time my fists alone will hammer you senseless, you won’t be able to tell north from south, east from west!"

...

"Old Meng, you just wait!"

...

"Don’t get too happy too early, Old Lu!"

...

"Don’t get cocky, Old Meng!"

The two became fiercely competitive, determined to outdo each other with their original moves, proving who was the strongest. Their initially crude techniques rapidly improved amidst their fights, addressing weaknesses and becoming increasingly refined.

"It’s still me who won, right?" Meng Jingzhou recalled the recent battle and was so delighted he couldn’t settle down for cultivation.

"He aims to continue refining his fist technique in one go, ensuring Old Lu won’t win next time!"

Meng Jingzhou happily entered his closed-door cultivation, taking an Oblivion Pill to reach a state of sudden enlightenment, with his fist technique flashing rapidly through his mind. In the previous confrontation, what appeared flawless to others seemed glaringly obvious.

"Hmm, I need to change the force-applying motion of this move; otherwise, it’ll be easy for Lu Yang to interrupt."

"Footwork needs to be adjusted too; it’s too rigid, not fluid enough."

Cultivation knows no time, Meng Jingzhou didn’t realize how long he had been immersed in his fist techniques. As his fists dramatically improved, his realm silently reached the Great Perfection of the Divinity Transformation Stage, just a single thought away from a breakthrough.

He now felt like a full water bottle, a gentle shake, and water would overflow, achieving Great Perfection.

"Success!"

This time, Meng Jingzhou’s seclusion brought significant gains, but upon rising, he found his body somewhat unstable.

"Am I about to break through to the Void Refinement Stage?"

Meng Jingzhou rejoiced. Indeed, it was a stroke of double luck with both his fist technique and realm breaking through.

"Time to leave seclusion and face the Tribulation!"

Now he could recite the Immortal’s Quotations backwards easily and with the backing of the Yingtian Immortal, passing the tribulation would be a piece of cake.

"It seems Old Lu, you’re one step behind. I’ll reach the Void Refinement Stage first!"

Meng Jingzhou pushed open the stone door and even the gloomy sky seemed remarkably beautiful.

He lazily stretched, ready to welcome the Heavenly Tribulation, only to see his defeated underling Lu Yang running over excitedly.

Lu Yang was holding a new type of bun invented by Yun Mengmeng, with the filling baked on the outside.

"Old Meng, let me show you a new sword technique I’ve just comprehended."

Meng Jingzhou smiled inscrutably, "Old Lu, you and I are no longer in the same realm of cultivators."

Lu Yang’s once joyful smile froze on his face.

"Are you about to break through?"

"Indeed," Meng Jingzhou said, pointing to the rapidly gathering dark clouds above.

Lu Yang’s complexion changed drastically, and he took off running. He was always on the edge of a breakthrough, suppressing his realm because he was afraid of facing the tribulation. He had thought to delay it by a few days if possible.

But who would have thought Old Meng would bring the Thunder Tribulation down on them so soon?

Damn it, the spiritual essence within is unstable and about to burst out!

Under the gathering power of the Heavenly Tribulation, the Power of the Heavenly Tribulation drew Lu Yang out, helping him puncture through the bottleneck of his realm!

"Eh, Old Lu, you’re breaking through too?" Meng Jingzhou noticed Lu Yang’s anomaly; he was in a similar state to himself.

The originally dark clouds grew denser, and new clouds joined the brewing storm above.

"Well, then, let’s face the Tribulation together."

Thunder rumbled, flashing behind the dark clouds, filled with the same excitement as Meng Jingzhou.

"I think it’s better if we face the Tribulation separately," Lu Yang whispered as he quietly distanced himself from Meng Jingzhou. Facing the Tribulation together was definitely not a good idea.

"Come on, let’s do it together."

Meng Jingzhou wouldn’t let Lu Yang escape easily; with Lu Yang there, the difficulty of passing the tribulation would surely decrease.

With a thundering roar, the tribulation descended, swift and fierce, enveloping them both.

...

Laurel Fairy Palace, the Black Ancient Ring.

The Venerable Ying Long soaring through the sky took human form, his face unable to hide his smile.

This puzzled his disciple Startling Swan, who always thought of her master as a dignified individual, "Master, is there something that makes you happy?"

Yingtian Immortal stroked his good disciple’s head with a smile, "Yes, something good has happened. Your master will make a trip."

Yingtian Immortal left the Black Ancient Ring, and no one in the Fairy Palace noticed his presence.

He activated the Tribulation Dao Fruit, linking his intent to the Heavenly Tribulation gathering above the Seek Dao Sect.

"Heh, both are present, perfect. Let’s start with the Qiantian Divine Thunder, ranked number one in the Divinity Transformation Stage, to warm you two up."

Chapter 1122: 1121: The Top-Ranked Thunder Tribulation is Constantly Refreshing

"Is someone about to make a breakthrough?"

The members of the Seek Dao Sect all looked up, the sky filled with dense, oppressive clouds, a clear sign of the Heavenly Tribulation brought about by a significant advancement in realm.

"That direction is Junior Brother Meng’s abode! He’s going to break through to the Void Refinement Stage!"

"Why hasn’t the second-in-command returned yet?" Yun Mengmeng fidgeted with a strand of her hanging hair, recalling how the second-in-command had run off with a freshly baked bun just now without leaving any feedback or suggestions for improvement.

"Xiao Zhi, what do you think about this roasted bun?" Yun Mengmeng asked while sitting next to Yun Zhi, who had been forced to eat the buns.

"It’s pretty good."

"Is that all?"

"What else do you want?"

Yun Mengmeng put on a disappointed look; the second-in-command was better, knowing how to give feedback, while Xiao Zhi would just naively say it’s tasty.

Yun Zhi ate the last piece of the bun’s crust, and although not telepathic, she felt her best friend was bad-mouthing her.

"Why is there thunder? Is someone going to Pass Tribulation?" Yun Mengmeng followed the sound of thunder, "Wasn’t that the direction the second-in-command went in?"

"Did Elder Brother Lu Yang go somewhere else to Pass his Tribulation?" Ao Ling popped her head out from the pool, which she had dug at Heaven’s Gate Peak Mountain and filled with seawater to enjoy a comfortable soak.

"Why isn’t he Passing his Tribulation here?" Jiang Lianyi, in her original form, lay on the ground while Jin Caiwei groomed her feathers.

"The second-in-command was probably afraid of ruining my oven."

"And my pool, too."

Yun Zhi looked over the damaged landscape, now under the control of the Immortal Force, and thought it wouldn’t be too bad if the junior brother Passed his Tribulation here.

...

"Cough cough, Old Lu, does the power of this Thunder Tribulation seem off?" Both were caught off guard by the sudden Tribulation, almost leaving Meng Jingzhou stunned.

Wouldn’t a typical person at the Divinity Transformation Great Perfection stage have their soul ripped out?

"Really? I don’t feel much power," Lu Yang said nonchalantly, enduring the pain and moving around like nothing was wrong.

Meng Jingzhou laughed heartily, unconcerned: "Hey, just messing with you. You didn’t fall for it. What could possibly go wrong with such a minor Thunder Tribulation?"

"It’s good then. I thought you overdid it with your practice and wore yourself out."

"How could that be?"

The two exchanged smiles, not finding the Thunder Tribulation challenging at all.

Unbeknownst to them, the dark clouds above had vanished, replaced by a vast sea of purple lightning that was dazzling to behold. Those with insufficient cultivation levels, even from a distance, would find their eyes stinging and uncontrollably shivering.

Dai Bufan watched the Thunder Tribulation in shock: "Isn’t this the top-ranked Qian Heaven Thunder Tribulation? How come it’s this kind for the first round?"

"Isn’t Elder Brother Lu Yang in danger?" a disciple who had just joined the Seek Dao Sect asked, worry filling his eyes.

"No worries, no worries. Remember when Elder Brother Lu passed his Nascent Soul Stage Tribulation? He encountered the Qian Heaven Thunder Tribulation and still passed it easily without any injuries."

Lightning fell, the Five Lightning Strikes engulfed them, yet they had the leisure to converse.

"Old Lu, does it hurt?"

"Not at all. It’s nothing."

The two grimaced and tried to smile, feeling their bones go numb, yet they refused to cry out in pain.

Purple lightning fell like a river breaking through a dam, wholly unstoppable by human means, and in an instant, the figures of the two men were completely submerged, and they no longer dared to talk tough.

"This is the Qian Heaven Thunder Tribulation, isn’t it? Old Lu, is the connection you found even reliable?" Meng Jingzhou cursed loudly, realizing the origin of the thunder tribulation that looked familiar only after it had struck him clear-headed.

The first round was the Qian Heaven Thunder Tribulation; what would follow could only be some kind of demons or monsters!

"Heavenly Reversal!"

When Lu Yang was passing his Nascent Soul Perfection tribulation, he had relied on this trick to fool the Qian Heaven Thunder Tribulation, hoping to repeat the trick.

But whether he became Lu Yin or remained Lu Yang, the thunder tribulation struck unerringly, impossible to deceive!

Lu Yang realized that last time he had succeeded because the thunder tribulation was uncontrolled; this time, with the Yingtian Immortal watching, fooling it would be preposterous!

The ruthless purple glow thrashed them, bathing them in the thunder tribulation. They were slashed open, skin flayed, and one could smell the charred scent of their singed hair and blackened bodies resembling lumps of charcoal.

"We finally got through it." Meng Jingzhou spoke, exhaling black smoke, his voice hoarse, still terrified, thinking he was about to die.

Fortunately, they had made it through; their physical bodies were extraordinary, and despite being mutilated, they were still automatically recovering their strength and physique.

However, before they could even grasp the situation, the second round of the thunder tribulation followed, giving them no chance to catch their breath!

"It’s coming again?!" Both men looked shocked, abandoning all pretense and greedily gulping down elixirs to restore their condition.

A thunder tribulation of myriad colors descended, like someone had kicked over a bucket of paint, turning the colors of the lightning bizarre and wondrous, omnipotent, annihilating everything.

"How do I feel this thunder tribulation is even more terrifying than the Qian Heaven Thunder Tribulation?" A newly admitted disciple, although unfamiliar with the classifications of thunder tribulations, was unusually sensitive to danger.

Dai Bufan had a strange expression, racking his brains but unable to match any known thunder tribulations with this second round: "I’ve never seen this kind of thunder tribulation. Could there be new types cropping up even after three hundred thousand years?"

"And its power is clearly stronger than that of the Qian Heaven Thunder Tribulation. Has the Qian Heaven Thunder Tribulation evolved to be the second thunder tribulation for the Divinity Transformation Stage?"

Why does everything involving Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou always turn out so odd?

Looking at them attempting to pass the tribulation was like witnessing history.

The lightning was splendid, shaking heaven and earth; the sky was painted in dazzling, variegated colors as if a great catastrophe was descending.

Suddenly, silence fell, the thunder tribulation still present but no longer making any sound.

Great sound is often silent!

The quiet lasted only a moment before the world’s sounds returned, and the thunder tribulation surged even more fiercely, fiercely raining down on Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

Lu Yang wielded his sword, Meng Jingzhou his fist, attempting to destroy the thunder tribulation before it could fully descend.

Unfortunately, against the thunder tribulation meticulously prepared for them by the Yingtian Immortal, all resistance was futile.

The thunder tribulation pierced through Lu Yang along the Green Peak Sword, and simultaneously, a fist seal struck Meng Jingzhou.

After the second round of the thunder tribulation, both men felt their marrow and blood imbued with electricity. They were in such a sorry state that it was difficult to even discern their identities, let alone distinguish whether they were male or female by outsiders.

Faced with the imminent and unknown third round of thunder tribulation, which intuitively seemed even more terrifying than the second, they knew it absolutely must not descend!

Meng Jingzhou seemed to recall something and hurriedly shouted to the heavens to clarify his position:

"We’re your own people, ah! I can recite the ’Immortal’s Quotations’ backwards!"

The impending third round of thunder tribulation suddenly froze; just when Meng Jingzhou thought he had succeeded, he saw the thunder tribulation transform again, becoming even denser based on what had occurred before, as if someone had increased its power at the last moment, making it feel even more dangerous.

The dense, almost white thunder tribulation descended, reflecting the despair on their faces.

Chapter 1123: 1122: My Fate Is In My Own Hands, Not Dictated By Heaven!

Lu Yang’s face was scorched black, and upon hearing Meng Jingzhou shout about being able to recite the Immortal’s Quotations backwards, he instantly turned pale as a ghost.

This intensified third round of Thunder Tribulation looked like it was set to annihilate someone.

He had originally intended to set Old Meng up, but instead, he had trapped himself miserably.

"Damn it, Old Lu, you set me up!" Recalling the peculiarities of the third round of Thunder Tribulation, Meng Jingzhou instantly realized he had been played.

So much for the Yingtian Immortal Device favoring Old Lu—it was clearly Old Lu who had somehow provoked the Yingtian Immortal, resulting in himself getting dragged into the mess!

And the likely reason was probably found in this "Immortal’s Quotations"!

"It was you who told me to sweet-talk in front of the Yingtian Immortal, to give you a hand!"

"I didn’t tell you to drag me down with you!"

"How can it be called dragging down, this is a great opportunity, I only pulled you in because we’re good brothers. Don’t you know? Ancient Immortals have said that Thunder Tribulation is a test from the heavens, the more powerful the Tribulation you face during your ascension, the more benefits you’ll get after succeeding. Alright, I can’t make up any more..."

Their voices were quickly drowned out by the thunderous roaring.

The new round of Thunder Tribulation was incredibly fierce, causing the entire Seek Dao Sect to tremble. The range of the Tribulation expanded, and areas that were once safe were now within the radius of the Thunder Tribulation, with Disciples of Dao Seeking Sect who had been watching from a close distance turning to run while still looking back at the two passing their Tribulation.

"Truly worthy of being Senior Brother Lu and Senior Brother Meng, still in the mood to dance amidst such a fierce Thunder Tribulation."

"Yeah, yeah, I really don’t know if we can be as calm as them when it’s our turn."

"Let’s all do our best."

Seeing Lu Yang and the other dancing provocatively under Thunder Tribulation, countless Disciples of Dao Seeking Sect were greatly inspired, determined to become a Great Cultivator who could stand tall just like Lu Yang!

"Since the second and third rounds of Thunder Tribulation have no names, why not name them after the two Junior Brothers?"

"That makes sense, the second round of heavenly thunder could be called Jingzhou Thunder Tribulation, and the third round, Lu Yang Thunder Tribulation!"

"Great names, great names. To leave one’s mark in history with the name of heavenly thunder, surely the two Junior Brothers would be pleased."

"Old... Lu... you... wait... for... it... when... I... get... out... I’ll... definitely... make... you... pay..." Right now, Meng Jingzhou could hardly speak, even transmitting via spiritual sense was not smooth, his body convulsed uncontrollably like he was being electrocuted.

"Wait... for... me... to... find... Fairy... to... save... me..."

"You... hurry... go..."

"Fairy, save me!" Left with no choice, Lu Yang ran to the Spiritual Space to report and ask for help.

However, Qinghe stood at the gate of the Soybean Imperial Palace, blocking Minister Lu Yang: "Shh, don’t shout, the master is already asleep. Don’t disturb the master’s rest; we can talk when the master wakes up."

"I have urgent matters to report!"

"Let’s talk when the master wakes up."

Seeing Lu Yang come back to his senses, Meng Jingzhou quickly asked about the situation: "What did the Immortal say?"

Lu Yang’s face was full of grief and resentment, his heart aching: "The Historian rules all, the nation shall not be a nation!"

To think that he, who had devotedly accompanied Fairy Immortality through thick and thin, establishing the Great Bean Dynasty and becoming the first in the line of success, would be ruined by the rise of the Historian!

"Ah?"

The Meng Family was the most prominent influential family and also a family of officials. Meng Jingzhou, well-versed in the intrigues of government, was at a loss trying to connect ’Historian’ with ’autocratic rule’.

Old Lu’s antics with the Immortal seemed quite varied.

"Let’s try the new technique," Lu Yang said gravely. It was originally prepared for Old Meng, but now it seemed it had to be used on Yingtian Immortal first!

In an instant, Lu Yang’s body was enveloped in Sword Qi, bravely confronting the Thunder Tribulation. The pure and spotless white Thunder Tribulation struck Lu Yang, allowing Meng Jingzhou, who stood beside him, to see Lu Yang’s bones.

However, Lu Yang knew the limits of his physical body all too well; if Old Meng couldn’t withstand it, he was even less likely to succeed.

Yingtian Immortal, floating in the sky, watched Lu Yang with confusion, not understanding what the youngster was attempting to do.

Then he watched, eyes wide, as Lu Yang transformed from a person into a sword.

"?"

Yingtian Immortal rubbed his eyes, thinking he was seeing things.

Lu Yang once again became the Green Peak Sword, fiercely unleashing a Sword Qi strike, clashing with the thunder, still unable to overcome the Thunder Tribulation.

Lu Yang’s body became the sword, with the sword tassel on the hilt bound with the Identity Jade.

The Identity Jade gently swayed, and the Green Peak Sword, Inheriting Shadow Sword, Seven Star Sword Set appeared, surrounding Lu Yang like loyal guards.

"Ten Thousand Swords Returning to Origin!"

Lu Yang called out the Sword Skill in his heart, merging with the nine Immortal Swords, significantly boosting his momentum. He fiercely slashed out a Sword Qi, shattering the thunder!

"Liu Ningxuan’s Unity of Man and Sword?" Yingtian Immortal wore a strange expression, immediately thinking of the original owner of the Inheriting Shadow Sword, Liu Ningxuan, who invented the "Unity of Man and Sword."

But Lu Yang’s approach was clearly different from Liu Ningxuan’s; he had created his own unique version of Unity of Man and Sword.

Even though Lu Yang had successfully shattered a strand of Thunder Tribulation, more thunder followed one after another, leaving Lu Yang overwhelmed.

"Supreme Dominance Fist!" Meng Jingzhou turned into a figure with three heads and six arms, shouting the "Immortal’s Quotations" in his heart, exuding an air of unrivaled supremacy.

The disciples watching were inspired by the desperate fighting spirit of Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou. They felt as if they were facing the Thunder Tribulation themselves, their blood boiling, Qi and Blood surging, excitement turning their necks red.

"Our fate is in our own hands, not in heaven’s! Senior Brother Lu, Senior Brother Meng, you’re sure to defy the heavens!"

It was unclear who started it, but what began as just a few voices turned into a roar of encouragement from all around, cheering the two on.

"Our fate is in our own hands, not in heaven’s! Senior Brother Lu, Senior Brother Meng, you’re sure to defy the heavens!"

While still contending with the Thunder Tribulation, Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou stiffly turned their heads, disbelievingly looking at their well-meaning junior brothers and sisters.

Damn it, don’t shout nonsense! We’ve always been generous to you all!

And Elder Brother Dai, what are you so excited about? You’re shouting the loudest!

"Oh ho, quite a loud cheer!" Yingtian Immortal had thought that enough was enough, but when he heard the people around cheering on, he suddenly felt invigorated, rolling up his sleeves to continue manipulating the Thunder Tribulation.

He always enjoyed striking at those who declared their fate was in their own hands, not in heaven’s. Did they really think his Thunder Tribulation was vegetarian?

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou were evenly matched in their struggle against the Thunder Tribulation, but just as they reached a delicate balance, the power of the thunder increased once more. Both were struck so hard by the Thunder Tribulation that they wished for death. Lu Yang was hit back to his original form, with the nine Immortal Swords scattered around him.

The two fell head-first into the ground, looking like newly planted saplings, twitching from the lightning strikes.

Using their palms to prop themselves up, they burst from the ground and lay there, watching the final Thunder Tribulation brewing in the sky, the corners of their eyes twitching.

"Old Meng, I’m glad to have known you in this lifetime. I’ll see you in the next," said Lu Yang.

Meng Jingzhou with a mournful expression replied, "Don’t get to know me next life. Find someone else to trap."

"Old Lu, you know I’ve always respected Yingtian Immortal. How on earth did you manage to provoke him?"

"Oh, you want to know?" Lu Yang, as if seeing the light at the end of the tunnel, suddenly perked up.

Chapter 1124: 1123: Void Refinement Stage

"No, no, no, I don’t want to listen!" Having been deceived by Lu Yang once, Meng Jingzhou was not about to be fooled again. Seeing that Lu Yang still wanted to speak, he immediately covered his mouth.

Seeing that Old Meng was not fooled, Lu Yang had no choice but to prepare for the final Thunder Tribulation.

"Although it’s still theoretical, the opportunity is rare, so it’s worth a try."

Lu Yang lay on the ground, becoming one with the earth, planting the Bodhi Seed. In an instant, the Bodhi Tree blossomed and bore fruit, giving birth to Lu Yang and his Wooden Avatar.

His primordial spirit left his body and merged into the Wooden Avatar. The Wooden Avatar exhibited Imitation Fist and transformed into the shape of the Green Peak Sword. Then, executing Ten Thousand Swords Returning to Origin, the Wooden Avatar and nine swords unified, held by Lu Yang.

"I always feel your moves are several times more complicated than others."

"You’re the one who talks too much. Today, you’ll witness the true power of the Immortal Sword Technique!"

Wielding the Self Sword, Lu Yang confronted the Heavenly Tribulation alone, a scene of tragic heroism.

Lu Yang swung the Immortal Sword, his posture graceful, resembling a transcended Sword Immortal. Every movement carried a unique rhythm. If any Sword Cultivator were present, their understanding of Swordsmanship would have undoubtedly ascended.

The only downside was that Lu Yang’s body was charred black as if he had crawled out of a coal pile.

"Hm?" Meng Jingzhou noticed the change in Lu Yang, surprised, and no longer lay on the ground playing dead. He stood up to see what Lu Yang intended to do.

He sensed that Lu Yang’s sword moved with his heart, achieving Unity of Essence, Qi, and Spirit. This wasn’t surprising, as Lu Yang’s sword was inherently a part of him.

However, he then sensed that Lu Yang’s strength was concentrated in his right arm, which showed signs of being unable to bear his body’s power, his fine blood vessels bursting directly.

Crack—

The final bolt of Heavenly Lightning descended, vast across the sky, plummeting down, but Lu Yang faced it head-on.

"Slash!"

The Self Sword unleashed a dazzling sword light, the strongest Lu Yang had ever produced, so overpowering that even Body Integration Realm Sword Cultivators felt inferior.

Lu Yang, who thrived in battle, found both pressure and motivation in the Heavenly Lightning, helping him complete the last piece of the Immortal Sword Technique.

The sword light split the Heavenly Lightning, seemingly cleaving it in two!

Boom—

To execute this strike, Lu Yang poured all his energy into it, pushing his right arm to the limit of its endurance until it exploded!

Meng Jingzhou was shocked. Was Lu Yang practicing a self-harming sword technique? His arm exploded; it was practically self-detonation, which they wouldn’t expect to recover from quickly even at the Body Integration Realm!

Unexpectedly, Lu Yang released the Self Sword and transformed into a spiritual energy life-form, consuming spiritual energy to compensate for the deficiency. When he regained human form, his right arm was restored to its original state, leaving Meng Jingzhou’s eyes nearly popping out.

"How is that possible?!"

Lu Yang twisted his arm, feeling no discomfort. Indeed, his approach was correct.

The lightning split into two, striking both Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

Finally drained of strength, both collapsed to the ground with a thud.

As the Heavenly Tribulation ended, nectar descended, nourishing their bodies. They floated in the air, their wounds silently healing.

Both officially entered the Void Refinement Stage!

"Finally reached the Void Refinement... How did we get back to the Qi Cultivation Stage?"

Both were astonished by the sudden drop to the lowest realm, their powers returning to the initial state of cultivation, now unable to defeat even a Tiger Demon.

In the Void Refinement Stage, there was erratic leaping from the Qi Cultivation Stage to the Body Integration Realm without any pattern. Cultivators in this realm seldom left their homes to avoid being killed by Foundation Establishment Great Masters.

Although they had learned about the unique manifestations of the Void Refinement Stage beforehand, accepting them still wasn’t easy once they reached this realm.

"Now we’re back in the Nascent Soul Stage."

In an instant, they both leaped to the Nascent Soul Stage, the changes rapid. The swift shifts in realm meant they couldn’t even control their own strength properly—one second they used the strength of the Qi Cultivation Stage to walk, the next they were in the Nascent Soul Stage, potentially covering hundreds of feet in a single step.

"Strange, where did these petals come from?"

Daoist Xiantian was puzzled when suddenly petals drifted above his head.

Everyone looked up to see that after passing the Tribulation, the thunderclouds had not dissipated but instead had split open to reveal sunshine and a flurry of petals. From within the sunlight, the emperor-like, slender figure emerged, overlooking the mortals.

He had seen countless trials; his eyes held the vicissitudes of life. Nothing that appeared before him could sway his decision; he fulfilled all mortal fantasies about immortals.

The emperor-like figure appeared silhouetted against the light, his face indiscernible, signifying that mortals should not gaze upon the countenance of an immortal.

When he spoke, the world hushed, as if his was the only voice in existence, and even the sounds of insects and birds completely vanished.

"Heh, who would have thought that such extraordinary talents would appear in later generations."

The members of the Seek Dao Sect were thrown into chaos, unable to believe that additional changes could occur during a Heavenly Tribulation, something unprecedented from ancient times to the present.

"I don’t know, this situation has never occurred before!"

The emperor-like figure’s voice was not loud, but his forceful tone spread throughout the entire Seek Dao Sect.

"I am known as Ying Tian Immortal, abiding by the heavenly principles, acting on heaven’s behalf. Today, I see two remarkable talents in the mortal world, truly gratifying."

"What, he is the legendary Ying Tian Immortal?!"

Upon learning the identity of Ying Tian Immortal, the members of Seek Dao Sect were no longer calm; such a major figure, who had vanished without a trace for three hundred thousand years, had now appeared?

This could indeed be considered a miraculous immortal event unseen for all eternity!

"That lascivious Ying Tian Immortal?"

"Or the Ancestor of Spirit Chef, the sole Nine Star Spirit Chef!"

"Wasn’t it said that he is a Female Immortal? Then why create the Mother-child River? Turns out he’s a man?"

Ying Tian Immortal’s corner of the eye twitched; what were the minds of these Seek Dao Sect people like, not one reaction was on point; in the past, his appearances were met with endless praises.

"Are the talents of the two Senior Brothers so great that even the legendary immortals are alarmed!"

"No wonder the two Senior Brothers have encountered a Heavenly Tribulation unprecedented in ancient times!"

The Seek Dao Sect was abuzz, extraordinarily excited; today truly was a witness to history, as even an immortal had appeared.

Ying Tian Immortal, dressed in a black-based golden dragon robe, moved as if the golden dragon was coming to life, vivid and lively.

As he descended to the mortal world, the dust naturally avoided him, signifying his transcendence above the mundane, not touching a speck of dust.

Ying Tian Immortal looked towards Lu Yang and the other with a compassionate gaze, "You have passed my test, well done."

"But you must remember, the Void Refinement Stage is just the beginning for cultivators; you must guard against arrogance and impatience and diligently cultivate."

"We will heed the Venerable Immortal’s words!"

Lu Yang and the other spoke in unison, not daring any disrespect.

"Venerable Immortal, I have a question and ask for your enlightenment," Lu Yang asked, raising his head.

"Speak freely."

"Why am I now in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage?"

Logically, being in the Void Refinement Stage, one’s realm should fluctuate between the Qi Cultivation Stage and the Body Integration Realm.

But Lu Yang definitely felt that his realm was at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

He had encountered countless great powers passing Tribulation, and he was not mistaken about the realms.

Ying Tian Immortal: "..."

How come you always have odd questions, young man.

Chapter 1125: 1124 Continuous Visits

"What, has Junior Brother Lu Yang reached the Transcendance Tribulation Stage now?"

"Isn’t the limit of the Void Refinement Stage the Body Integration Realm?"

"I don’t know."

Disciples of the Seek Dao Sect were agitated once again, even Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi displayed extremely surprised expressions, as even during their Void Refinement phase, they had never encountered such a situation.

Yingtian Immortal rarely fell silent for a moment.

Ever since he had met Lu Yang, this lad always stumbled upon unexpected situations, just like an Immortal Fairy whose motives were inscrutable.

Truly worthy of being from the Immortal Lineage.

Yingtian Immortal carefully sensed Lu Yang’s realm and, indeed, he was at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

"Venerable Immortal?" Lu Yang looked at Yingtian Immortal expectantly. He wasn’t picking a quarrel; he genuinely wanted to know what the issue was.

Yingtian Immortal pondered for a moment, his voice calm and unhurried, as if Lu Yang’s situation had been within his expectations all along.

"As the saying goes, every drink and morsel are preordained. You have surpassed your predecessors, passing the triple-layered Heavenly Tribulation deemed impossible, accomplishing an unprecedented feat and thus breaking through the ultimate limit of the Void Refinement Stage. This situation is an opportunity bestowed by me, on behalf of Heaven."

Lu Yang translated for Yingtian Immortal: The force of the Heavenly Tribulation was so strong it brought about a new situation.

This was almost what Lu Yang had guessed since Old Meng had also reached the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, although he slipped back down pretty quickly.

Although it was a new situation, it was a good one. According to ancient legends passed down, the sages who created the realm of Void Refinement did so in the hope that cultivators would get accustomed to subsequent realms in advance, thereby creating a new realm.

Being able to experience the power of the Transcendance Tribulation Stage during the Void Refinement Stage means that once we truly reach the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, cultivation will be twice as efficient with half the effort.

"Did you hear what Ying Tian Immortal Venerable said? The two Junior Brothers have encountered a fortuitous fairy encounter!"

To pass the test of immortals, to have an immortal reveal themselves, to obtain a fairy’s fortune, this would be a glorious tale to be passed down through the ages in any era.

"Is that really so?" Yun Zhi raised an eyebrow.

When she faced the Qian Heaven Thunder Tribulation during her tribulation, she felt that there was still potential to be tapped into within the Void Refinement Stage, but unfortunately, she could not control the thunder, and could not prove her conjecture. The changes in the little junior brother and Junior Brother Meng this time confirmed her thoughts.

"Little Yang, see what Immortal said? The harsher the tribulation, the greater the reward!"

Lu Yang had successfully passed his tribulation and had undergone a new transformation. Immortal Fairy seemed even more overjoyed than Lu Yang himself.

Looking at the exuberant Immortal Fairy and then at Qinghe, who was standing beside her showing no emotion, Lu Yang revealed a peculiar expression.

Fairy, you don’t seem like you’ve just woken up at all.

Considering it, it made sense. With such commotion from his tribulation, even if Fairy was hibernating, she would have woken up. How could she still be asleep?

So, does that mean Qinghe blocking me outside the Imperial Palace was actually on Fairy’s orders?

Lu Yangxin shivered within. As the saying goes, keeping company with the emperor is like accompanying a tiger. Was his Majesty’s action meant to sow discord between himself, a loyal minister, and the treacherous historian, thereby balancing the court relationships? Was this the art of an emperor’s mind?

Noticing that Little Yang’s gaze towards her was somewhat off, as if poking through her little scheme, Immortal Fairy smiled awkwardly.

She knew the principle that the greater the intensity of the tribulation, the greater the reward. Yet, seeing Little Yang struggling pitifully under the Heavenly Tribulation, she couldn’t bear to see him tormented, and worried that Little Yang would come seeking help, which would soften her resolve. Therefore, she had Little Lotus intervene.

Qinghe, whose hands were placed before her, stood silently as though she were a statue, contemplating nothing.

As an immortal descending upon the mortal world, she couldn’t appear for too long, as immortals must always maintain a sense of mystery and limit the time spent interacting with mortals.

Yingtian Immortal, who proposed this theory, naturally implemented his own philosophy.

"Today’s achievement is but a small step on the path of cultivation, do not take your practice lightly."

"I should not intervene too much in the affairs of the mortal world, take good care of yourselves."

"Yes!" Lu Yang and the other called out in unison.

A gentle breeze emerged from beneath, inflating Ying Tian Immortal’s dragon robe. The golden dragons embroidered on the robe blinked, moved about the fabric, then flew out of the garment to greet the wind, rapidly transforming into a divine dragon of immense size, drawing gasps of amazement from the onlookers.

The presence of the divine dragon exerted a subtle pressure, not intentionally so, but rather it was so immense that controlling it completely was difficult, allowing a tiny fraction to seep through.

Even this slight pressure gave everyone a sense of ancientness from a bygone era.

The divine dragon lowered its proud head. There is only one in the world who could make it bow—its master, the Ying Tian Immortal.

Ying Tian Immortal stood upon the dragon’s head, riding it back to the heavens, vanishing into the sky.

As Ying Tian Immortal disappeared, the dark clouds finally cleared, revealing the long-missed sunshine that warmed everyone pleasantly.

"Brother Ying Tian’s entrances and exits are always so distinct," Ao Ling couldn’t help but laugh and cry at the same time. He had never seen this divine dragon before; could it be that it was newly cultivated recently?

"It’s finally over!"

Lu Yang grimaced, passing through a tribulation was truly no easy task, and the pain was indeed real.

Despite the fact that the heavenly dew could heal injuries, both of them had not fully recovered yet, with wounds inside and out causing excruciating pain at the slightest movement. They would need at least a month of rest to recuperate.

"See, I didn’t lie to you, the greater the power of the tribulation, the greater the reward. I did all this for your own good."

As heroes who created history, both were surrounded by Disciples of Dao Seeking Sect and escorted to their respective beds to rest and heal comfortably.

"Little Yang, I need to step away for a moment," Fairy Eternity said.

"Ah, okay," Lu Yang replied without much thought, simply acknowledging her.

...

"Master, have you finished your business here?" Startling Swan found that ever since her master returned, the smile on his face never ceased, even more exaggerated than when he had left.

Since becoming a disciple, Startling Swan had never seen her master smile like this.

News spreads at its own pace, and the affairs of Dao Seeking Sect had not yet reached the Laurel Fairy Palace.

"Now I feel comfortable."

Considering that smiling all the time did not help in maintaining an authoritative image, Ying Tian Immortal turned his back to Startling Swan, enigmatic as ever.

Making an entrance observed by all, announcing to the world his return, and then departing on a dragon amidst the adoring gazes of the crowd—the sequence of events felt like a return to the awe-inspiring days of Ancient Times.

After suppressing it for three hundred thousand years, he finally regained that feeling.

Behind Ying Tian Immortal, the voice of a surprised Startling Swan rang out, "Eh, Immortal Fairy, how come you are here?"

"Young girl, leave us for a moment."

"Oh."

"Ying Tian, who allowed you to strike so fiercely!" Immortal Fairy stormed in, angrily seeking an explanation from Ying Tian Immortal.

Ying Tian Immortal turned around, looking at Immortal Fairy as if he had seen a ghost: "When I said I wanted the strike to be stronger, you agreed!"

"So what if I agreed?"

"Aren’t you being reasonable... hey, hey, hey, don’t self-detonate..."

Boom—

After the intense explosion, Immortal Fairy vanished without a trace, leaving only Ying Tian Immortal with a stunned expression.

Suddenly, space twisted again, and another visitor arrived to see Ying Tian Immortal.

"Come on, Daoist Yun Zhi, you don’t even say hello before you start swinging..."

Chapter 1126: 1125: Is There No Concept of Convalescence in Ancient Times?

"Senior Brother Lu Yang, you will get better."

"Junior Brother, everyone will remember you."

"Junior Brother Lu, you are the pride of our Seek Dao Sect!"

In the abode, lying on the bed with his hands crossed over his chest, Lu Yang was surrounded by fresh flowers and blessings, which was quite touching.

Whether it was senior brothers and sisters or junior brothers and sisters, having witnessed Lu Yang overcome the Heavenly Tribulation with his defying talents and receive blessings from an immortal to break through the limits of the Void Refinement Stage, they were greatly shocked. They then visited Lu Yang one after another to show their respect.

The new disciples, seeing their idol, couldn’t help but burst into tears.

There were too many people visiting Lu Yang, they spoke only a word to him, placed down flowers, and left to give time to those who followed.

"All right, time’s almost up, the second-in-command should rest."

Yun Mengmeng came out to take charge, hanging a ’No Visitation’ sign at the entrance of the abode.

After the guests left, Ao Ling and others, who didn’t know from where, appeared one after another, all coming to help take care of Lu Yang.

Even if they could not take care, having a lively atmosphere was excellent.

Lu Yang really wanted to ask, didn’t you have the concept of convalescence in Ancient Times?

Yun Mengmeng, seeing the flowers piled up several layers around Lu Yang and recalling that weaving flower wreaths was a custom in the Secret Realm to represent blessings, said,

"With so many flowers here, why not weave a big flower wreath to give a huge blessing to the second-in-command?"

Hearing this, Lu Yang quickly tried to divert Yun Mengmeng’s idea, "Sister Mengmeng, why not make flower cakes instead?"

"Did you like eating flower cakes, second-in-command?"

"Yes."

"I’ll go make them right away!"

Yun Mengmeng, holding several bunches of flowers, left.

These flowers were not only beautiful but also had high medicinal value; making them into flower cakes could also be considered a form of dietary therapy.

"It’s surprising, the changes one can undergo in the Void Refinement Stage."

Ao Ling and others circled around Lu Yang, clicking their tongues in wonder. In their time, there were no Heavenly Tribulations in the Void Refinement Stage; only from the Body Integration Realm to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage did such tribulations exist. The present Heavenly Tribulations were invented by the Yingtian Immortal after she became an immortal.

Now they couldn’t return to the Void Refinement Stage; they could only gather around Lu Yang to see the rarity.

"Not to mention, Senior Brother Lu has deep blessings, not only did he revive Immortal Sister, but he also received such great fortunes."

Lu Yang now only hoped that Immortal Fairy would come back soon to divert these people’s attention from studying him.

Eldest Senior Sister also was nowhere to be found.

"Two sisters, Sister Caiwei, it’s time to eat the Spirit Fruit." Qinghe came holding the freshly arranged spirit fruits.

When Immortal Fairy was not in the Spiritual Space, Qinghe used to reincarnate and didn’t stay in the Spiritual Space.

"And Lu Zhuguo, this is yours." Qinghe placed a plate of spirit fruits beside Lu Yang’s bed.

Lu Yang’s eyes twitched; this was the first time he had been addressed that way.

Lu Yang noticed the varieties of spirit fruits on the plate were different, but Qinghe had cut them into uniformly sized round shapes with silly smiling faces.

... These all looked suspiciously like an immortal.

Lu Yang ate a piece of spirit fruit, which was very sweet and made his throat feel much better; this piece must have had a lung-moistening function.

"But why is there no spiritual energy?"

Since they were spirit fruits, why was there no spiritual energy at all?

Qinghe sighed, patiently explaining, "Please understand your condition, Lu Zhuguo. You are currently in the Void Refinement Stage, with an unstable realm—one moment you could be in the Late Tribulation Stage and the next in the Early Stage of Qi Refinement."

"If you were in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, naturally you could consume any level of spirit fruit, but if you were in the Early Stage of Qi Refinement, consuming spirit fruits would be a death wish."

"So everything you eat now can’t contain spiritual essence, to avoid harming your body further."

What she didn’t mention was that before bringing the spirit fruits in, she had advised Yun Mengmeng, who was pondering over making flower cakes, to bake out the spiritual essence from the flowers.

Lu Yang hadn’t expected there to be so much to consider.

"Little Lotus is truly as meticulous as ever," Jiang Lianyi said with a smile, teasing her. "No wonder Immortal Sister loves being looked after by you."

"You flatter me, Sister Lianyi, this is my only strength."

"There you go being modest again." Immortal Fairy triumphantly returned, entering through the grand doors of the abode.

"I’ve always said you’re great in every way; you just lack confidence."

"I still fall short compared to an adult."

"There you go saying that again."

"Is the Fairy back?" Lu Yang greeted from the bed.

"Yes, I just went to catch up with Ying Tian Immortal," said Immortal Fairy with a grin, pride written all over her face.

"Do you know where Eldest Senior Sister went?" Lu Yang had just asked Yun Mengmeng, and even she didn’t know where Eldest Senior Sister had gone.

"Miss Yun? I haven’t seen her." Immortal Fairy furrowed her brow, slightly surprised. Little Yang was injured and in bed, where could Miss Yun possibly be at a time like this?

"Forget it, Miss Yun won’t get into trouble. By the way, Little Yang, since you need to heal, let Little Lotus look after you."

"Cough, cough, cough—" Lu Yang suddenly started coughing violently, nearly coughing up internal injuries. What qualifications did he have to be looked after by a Semi-Immortal?

Lu Yang frantically signaled to Qinghe with his eyes, urging her to quickly reject Immortal Fairy’s absurd idea. Didn’t you always prefer taking care of the Fairy?

However, Qinghe completely ignored Lu Yang’s signals and seriously said, "I will follow the adult’s orders completely."

Lu Yang was dumbfounded; Qinghe, you’re really too stubborn.

"With Little Lotus looking after me, I can rest easy! So tired, so tired, I’m going to sleep."

Immortal Fairy stretched lazily and swiftly lay back on the grand bed in her Spiritual Space.

"Young Sect Master, would you like to see a Dream Mirage?"

Jin Caiwei held out a Recording Orb, which she had tested repeatedly. Finally, she managed to perfectly integrate the Dream Mirage with the Recording Orb, achieving an immersive effect.

No one else had yet seen her research results, and Lu Yang was the first one.

"Let’s see."

Lu Yang was interested, wanting to know if it was as he had imagined.

Jin Caiwei placed the Recording Orb on the ground, channeled a thread of spiritual essence into it, and the orb projected a light screen. It opened up like a realm, enveloping everyone and presenting a grand battle.

It was a Golden Phoenix rebirthing from Nirvana, her opponent a massive creature as red as the blazing sun, with six legs and four wings.

"Is this the fight Elder Lianyi had with Nation Master Zhu?" Lu Yang recognized the combatants instantly—it was the battle that founded the Demon Nation.

Although he had watched it in person, seeing a battle of Semi-Immortals twice definitely had its benefits.

Lu Yang felt as if he were really there, back in the Demon Nation—the effect of the Recording Orb was even better than he had imagined.

"Look at what you are doing; you should have used fire here!"

"And here, you hit too lightly." Ao Ling commented right in front of the persons involved, causing dissatisfaction.

"How dare you critique? You should know that today’s Zhu Tian isn’t the Zhu Tian from Ancient Times. He has countless Tribulation Crossing Demons under him; I’m the only one who can definitely beat him. If it were you, you probably couldn’t!"

"Ridiculous, what does having Tribulation Crossing Demons amount to? Are they going to defy heaven?" Ao Ling scoffed coldly.

Cold sweat broke out on Lu Yang; if this kept up, Elder Ao Ling might just challenge Zhu Tian to a fight.

"Junior Brother, are you okay?" Eldest Senior Sister arrived belatedly.

"Eldest Senior Sister!" Lu Yang felt her timing couldn’t be more perfect; she had saved the Demon Nation.

Eldest Senior Sister gently prodded Lu Yang’s injured spot.

"Does it hurt?"

"It hurts."

"That’s good. I just asked Chasing Moon True Person, and he said we could use the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo to convert your pain into pleasure. Should I ask for his help?"

Chapter 1127: 1126 Lu Yang: I Did My Best

Hearing the proposal from Eldest Senior Sister, Lu Yang was quite tempted.

Back when he was cultivating on Eldest Senior Sister’s Golden Core Star, the Jinwu Qi had refined him using the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo, and the effect was unexpectedly good.

He couldn’t feel any pain at all; it was truly joyful cultivation.

However, he still had concerns.

"Did Old Meng agree?"

Both he and Old Meng were recuperating. If Old Meng stubbornly insisted that it didn’t hurt and he didn’t need help from Chasing Moon True Person, then he couldn’t seek help from Chasing Moon True Person either!

"He agreed."

"I’ll go."

Eldest Senior Sister nodded, then disappeared, and the crowd hurriedly constructed a stretcher, lifting Lu Yang onto it.

"Ouch, it really hurts."

Meng Jingzhou was also surrounded by bouquets and well-wishes, creating a touching scene.

When people were around, Meng Jingzhou endured the pain and said it didn’t hurt, but as soon as everyone left, he started shouting out in pain.

The gains from Passing Tribulation were truly significant, and so was the pain.

"Junior Brother Meng, how are your injuries?" Eldest Senior Sister appeared in Meng Jingzhou’s abode.

Compared to Lu Yang’s noisy surroundings, Meng Jingzhou’s abode was much quieter, fitting for convalescence.

Seeing Eldest Senior Sister visiting him, Meng Jingzhou was quite moved and tried to struggle to his feet, but found his physical condition didn’t allow it, and could only lie in bed, "It’s alright, I need to rest quietly, and emotionally, I also need to avoid major fluctuations."

"I just asked Chasing Moon True Person, she said she could use the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo to turn your and your Junior Brother’s pain into joy. Shall I ask her to help?"

Meng Jingzhou didn’t answer right away but remained silent, seemingly hesitant about something.

"Junior Brother agreed."

"I’ll go!"

On Great Snow Mountain, two groups of people set off for the summit simultaneously.

Old Ma chewed on dried grass in his mouth, with several ropes tied to the saddle, and at the other end of the ropes was Meng Jingzhou lying on a rush mat.

Meng Jingzhou, lying on the mat looking at the sky, heard Lu Yang greet him, "Old Meng, how are you feeling?"

Meng Jingzhou turned his head and nearly burst a blood vessel.

Yun Mengmeng was leading the way in front, while Lu Yang lay on a stretcher, with Qinghe, Ao Ling, Jiang Lianyi, and Jin Caiwei each holding a corner of the stretcher, a rather luxurious assembly.

It’s not even something Emperor Xia could enjoy if he were severely injured—let alone his funeral!

Facing Lu Yang, Meng Jingzhou remained unflustered, "It’s okay; I’ve been researching the Four Ancient Immortals lately and found out that besides Qilin Immortal who got married, the other three Immortals remain unmarried, suggesting that if one wishes to become an Immortal, it’s best not to involve oneself in romantic affairs."

"Gone soft?"

"You’re the one who’s gone soft!" Meng Jingzhou snapped angrily.

The two argued all the way from the foot to the top of the mountain until they saw Chasing Moon True Person and finally quieted down.

Chasing Moon True Person circled around them, "Rare indeed! Even the Yingtian Immortal disappeared for 300,000 years, and you two managed to alarm him with your tribulations."

Lu Yang thought to himself that it was no big deal; if the True Person were to return, perhaps she could also find the Yingtian Immortal at Laurel Fairy Palace.

"Pain turned into joy, is it? Easy."

Chasing Moon True Person activated the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo, and two red halos appeared in her palm, slapping them onto both individuals.

Lu Yang, originally aching all over, suddenly lit up his eyes and felt comfortable, the effect was immediate.

It was Meng Jingzhou’s first time enjoying this treatment, and his reaction was even greater than Lu Yang’s, "So comfortable!"

Although the injury had not changed, the healing process had become quite comfortable!

Suddenly, Meng Jingzhou’s expression froze, something was off.

"Old Meng, what’s wrong?"

Meng Jingzhou’s expression was weird as he transmitted to Lu Yang, "I was always tormented by lustful fire and felt very painful, but now I feel so comfortable."

"?"

Lu Yang worried that one day a good brother would really become a eunuch.

With Chasing Moon True Person’s wonderful rejuvenation, both good brothers returned to their respective abodes with peace of mind.

Lu Yang continued to lie in bed, watching the battle between the two Semi-Immortal Great Powers, Zhu Tian and Jiang Lianyi, with Qinghe feeding him fruit on the left, Yun Mengmeng feeding him small pieces of flower cake on the right, and also listening to Ao Ling, the foremost person below the Immortals, comment on the battle.

"See, your move was too slow, you can’t fight like this."

"This is a combat strategy I developed called static-quelling dynamics," retorted Jiang Lianyi.

Ao Ling smirked, as if saying she knew her too well, "Come on, that excuse might fool Senior Brother Lu, but you just woke up and your body is still sluggish, your reaction was half a beat slow."

Before this battle took place, Jiang Lianyi was using the Heaven-Deceiving Life-Defying Technique to sleep since ancient times and was accidentally found by Lu Yang, then awakened by Immortal Fairy.

"And this move, all flashy but clearly more powerful when in human form, yet you insisted on fighting in a phoenix form for the sake of appearance."

"And more..."

The more Jiang Lianyi listened, the redder her face got, and she had no rebuttal because Ao Ling was right.

But Jiang Lianyi couldn’t show weakness in front of Ao Ling, and with a stiff neck, she said:

"Don’t just talk the talk without walking the walk. See, among the Semi-Immortals, I’m the only one who can beat Zhu Tian, you really can’t, but if you don’t believe it, go and try!"

"How ridiculous, let’s try then!"

Lu Yang broke into a cold sweat listening to this—why had they started arguing again all of a sudden? Where was Eldest Senior Sister to save the day, and Senior Caiwei? Stop showing this battle, is there nothing else in the Recording Orb? Change it.

"A moment, esteemed predecessors, may I make a suggestion?" Lu Yang was genuinely worried that these two would end up seeking out Zhu Tian, bringing misery upon him.

Eldest Senior Sister was nowhere in sight, and Jin Caiwei didn’t change the content, leaving Lu Yang no choice but to step in and mediate.

Lu Yang held significant respect in the hearts of the two women; once he spoke up, they stopped arguing.

"This Demon Nation is, after all, part of our Great Bean Dynasty, and Nation Master Zhu handles the state affairs of the Demon Nation daily, which is very busy. When I met him in Demon City before, I told him that given his busy schedule, there was no need to come see me."

"If Ao Ling Senior were to seek out Nation Master Zhu, I fear it would disrupt the operations of the Demon Nation."

"Moreover, the strength of Ao Ling Senior is undoubtedly great and well-known, there’s no need to prove oneself over such matters."

After thinking it through, Ao Ling realized this was sound reasoning. Although Zhu Tian was not technically a member of the Great Bean Dynasty, he was fundamentally working for it. He shouldn’t fall for any provocation that could waste Zhu Tian’s time.

Seeing Ao Ling give up, Lu Yang quietly breathed a sigh of relief; the crisis had passed.

Jin Caiwei, squatting nearby, listened with great interest. Although she was at the Semi-Immortal level, she lacked battle experience and couldn’t spot any flaws in the fight, so listening to Sister Ao Ling’s analysis, she gained even more than Lu Yang.

"Knock, knock—"

"Is someone knocking?" Jin Caiwei saw the two sisters arguing, with Yun Mengmeng and Sister Qinghe feeding the Young Sect Master, leaving herself idle. So, she went to open the door.

Seeing the visitor, Jin Caiwei was quite surprised.

"Uncle Zhu Tian?"

Upon hearing Jin Caiwei’s voice, everyone looked towards the abode’s entrance.

There stood Zhu Tian at the entrance of the abode, accompanied by two Transcendence Tribulation Stage great demons.

Zhu Tian rubbed his hands together and grinned sheepishly: "I heard that Brother Lu Yang was injured, so I thought I’d stop by for a visit on my way."

Chapter 1128: 1127: A Bustling Abode

Lu Yang was stunned, never expecting Zhu Tian to come.

I just spent a lot of effort persuading Senior Ao Ling.

Zhu, I even let go of the issue of you sending me a beautiful woman, practicing virtue in return for grievances, don’t say I didn’t help, I really tried my best.

"What’s, what’s up?"

Zhu Tian asked cautiously, feeling that the atmosphere was somewhat off. Why was everyone staring at him—was it a wrong time for him to come?

He heard that Lu Yang had successfully passed the Transcendence Tribulation and even broke through the limits of the Void Refinement Stage, shocking the world.

But what he cared about was another matter—Brother Lu was injured.

The injury was definitely not severe, it wouldn’t be fatal nor leave any sequela, but since he was injured, that gave him a reason to visit and get closer.

After the battle in Demon City, he had been reflecting—every time something happened, Brother Lu was there. Was it a coincidence?

The answer was no. Brother Lu’s presence wasn’t a coincidence, but rather a destiny by itself; he was the chosen demon to start the Great Era conflict, and Brother Lu was a witness, both of which were very important.

Thinking of this, Zhu Tian felt that his relationship with Brother Lu had grown deeper.

"Is it not convenient? If it’s inconvenient, I can come back later."

"It’s fine, please come in," Jin Caiwei said, making way; he was already here, it was too late to leave now.

Zhu Tian walked into the abode with two large boxes of sympathy gifts, followed by two Demon Emperors attendants empty-handed.

The boxes contained either specialty Spirit Fruits from the Demon Nation or sacred medicine for healing.

Of course, he could have taken them out from the storage ring, but bringing them directly had a bigger visual impact, having him carry the items while two Demon Emperors attendants carried nothing was the same principle.

After the two Demon Emperors entered the abode, they were extremely cautious, not daring to breathe loudly.

Originally, they were curious why the Nation Master specially came to Seek Dao Sect to visit Lu Yang.

Although Lu Yang was the foremost genius of the age, it did not seem important enough for the Nation Master to visit personally.

The Nation Master didn’t explain, simply saying to follow for appearance’s sake and not to be surprised by anything.

They still wondered what could be surprising, but now they understood.

The Dragon Clan Ancestor, the Phoenix Tribe Ancestor, the Qiongqi Clan Little Ancestor—all three were here, and recalling their identities in the Ancient Heavenly Court, Lu Yang’s true identity seemed imminent.

Was the foremost genius of the age just a superficial identity for Lu Yang?

Both great demons, learned and knowledgeable elders of their clans, couldn’t help but feel their scalps tingle when they thought of Lu Yang’s layered identities.

There might be even deeper identities, considering how two maids, stunningly beautiful, were taking care of Lu Yang, one on each side.

"Brother Lu, are you feeling better?"

Zhu Tian asked softly, though he felt that it was a redundant question. He didn’t know Yun Mengmeng, but Qinghe was an old acquaintance—Qinghe taking care of Lu Yang, even the Four Ancient Immortals didn’t receive such treatment.

"Much better," Lu Yang nodded, and it wasn’t just polite talk—the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo was really effective.

Zhu Tian noticed that the Recording Orb was still displaying the battle scenes and suddenly felt a sense of impending doom, yet he couldn’t pinpoint where the danger was coming from, so he hesitantly asked.

"Brother Lu, are you learning combat skills at the Semi-Immortal level? You truly live up to your name, Brother Lu."

The light and airy voice of Ao Ling sounded behind Zhu Tian, sending shivers down his spine.

"Zhu Tian, I heard you proclaimed yourself as the first below immortals?"

Zhu Tian’s body stiffened as if frozen, struggling to turn around. He had shouted that only a couple of times during the founding ceremony of the Demon Nation, and after Jiang Lianyi appeared, he only shouted it in his heart.

Zhu Tian nervously chuckled, "Ao Ling, you must have misheard me. I said I am the foremost among those below a Semi-Immortal."

"Is that so? Lianyi also said you’re not the same as in the Ancient Times and have become so strong that even I can’t defeat you now," Ao Ling narrowed her eyes, smiling at Zhu Tian.

Hearing this, Zhu Tian broke into a cold sweat, feeling a life-or-death crisis, "Sister Lianyi must be joking. How can I compare to you, Sister Ao Ling?"

"Let’s try and see if what Lianyi said is true or false." Ao Ling’s eyes filled with fighting spirit, she clenched her fists which made a creaking sound, making Zhu Tian even more frightened.

"Right, Zhu Tian, why are you afraid of her?" Jiang Lianyi fanned the flames from the side.

"I truly dare not, truly dare not," Zhu Tian wouldn’t dare to fight with Ao Ling, an Immortal’s Body. How could he use his head to fight Ao Ling?

Seeing that Zhu Tian was unwilling to fight, Ao Ling couldn’t forcibly drag him into battle, so she had no choice but to give up and sighed inwardly.

She agreed with one point from Jiang Lianyi, the Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Embryo was more powerful the more people it controlled. Zhu Tian’s abilities had definitely improved a notch since the Ancient Times.

Unfortunately, Zhu Tian didn’t want to fight her.

Knock, knock—

The knocking sound rang out again, "Is Lu Yang here?"

Emperor Xia.

"Cough, cough, cough—" Lu Yang suddenly coughed. My injury just needs two days of rest; it’s not life-threatening. Do they really need to visit me one after another?

Emperor Xia saw that Lu Yang’s Abode was crowded with people and was immediately stunned.

Are there so many people?

Emperor Xia came to visit Lu Yang following Jiang Pingan’s orders.

In Jiang Pingan’s view, Lu Yang was favored by Eldest Senior Sister Qinghe and was sure to become an Immortal in the future. Since he couldn’t get Lu Yang to marry into his family, he could only build their relationship in other ways.

This injury of Lu Yang was a perfect opportunity.

Emperor Xia knew that Lu Yang had ambiguous relations with many Semi-Immortals, which shouldn’t be known to others, hence even his Transcendance Tribulation Stage bodyguards stayed outside, and he entered the Seek Dao Sect alone.

Zhu Tian had a Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Embryo, perfect for controlling subordinates. His subordinates wouldn’t leak what they shouldn’t, a capability Emperor Xia lacked.

"Lu Yang, how are you feeling?" Emperor Xia also brought various gifts to console Lu Yang, all meticulously selected from the national treasury.

However, Lu Yang had already seen all kinds of Immortal Objects, and ordinary treasures no longer caught his eye, but he appreciated the sentiment.

"No serious harm, a few days of rest will suffice."

"That’s good."

Seeing Lu Yang, Emperor Xia couldn’t help but remember the words of the ancient ancestor.

He told the ancient ancestor about the Great Bean Dynasty, and he himself was serving as the Prime Minister. After listening, the ancient ancestor was silent for a long time before finally saying:

Remember to leave a position for me.

"Nation Master Jiang?" Zhu Tian called out.
