Emperor Xia and the Demon Emperor were both startled, neither expected to meet in this situation.

As nation masters, they mustn’t act rashly, and their meetings were governed by strict protocols, requiring the Ministry of Rites to busily prepare for months for each encounter.

However, the Great Bean Dynasty also had a Minister of Rites, who could arrange a meeting for the two nation masters.

Chapter 1129: 1128 Zhu Tian’s Progress

They decided to leave later.

Emperor Xia, also feeling bored, initiated a chat with Zhu Tian.

Two Demon Emperors in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage stood behind them, silently observing the scene.

Isn’t the process of negotiating trade cooperation between two countries a bit too simple?

That meant he was closer to Lu Yang.

I didn’t even mix into the Great Bean Dynasty.

Immortal Fairy noticed Zhu Tian continuously chopping wood and remembered that Zhu Tian was also a talented individual. The Great Bean Dynasty’s Ministry of Industry was still short-staffed.

This was the Great Bean Dynasty, where only the trusted confidants of immortals were qualified to join. He couldn’t believe he had such an opportunity.

In Meng Jingzhou’s Abode, Meng Junzi was checking Meng Jingzhou’s health condition.

Meng Jingzhou had broken through the limits of the Void Refinement Stage, leaving a mark in history, greatly beyond Meng Junzi’s expectations. He knew Meng Jingzhou was excellent, but not to this extent.

You should know, even he did not encounter the Yingtian Immortal when he passed his Tribulation.

"Your body is a bit troublesome; elixirs won’t work. You need rest."

Chapter 1130: 1129: The Contestants of the Great Era

"Fairy Eternity was delighted. "So, your ancient ancestor also wants to join our dynasty? How wonderful. I didn’t expect the Great Bean Dynasty’s reputation to have spread so far that people are volunteering to join. They truly have an eye for quality."

Fairy Eternity secretly gave Lu Yang a thumbs-up, praising Little Yang for a job well done, earning him a promotion of three ranks.

Her Great Bean Dynasty was made up of elite soldiers and outstanding leaders, not a place for those who were merely filling numbers.

Although Little Yang’s cultivation level might be a bit low, he had a clever mind and a way with words.

The two Demon Emperors were stunned. They had lived for thousands of years and this was the first time someone judged them as having too low cultivation levels.

They were at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, a status revered in the Cultivation World—able to sweep through all opposition, ensure the prosperity of their kin for ten thousand years, and even in demise, leave behind resources for their descendants’ rise.

Sure, people have the credentials to say that. As the head of the Five Ancient Immortals, this identity is equivalent to being the number one person in the Cultivation World.

Chapter 1131: 1130: Returning to the Court After Victory

"Second-in-command, I’m really letting go now!"

Amidst the expectant eyes of everyone, Lu Yang got out of bed and bravely took the first step, prompting everyone to celebrate joyfully.

After a week of resting in bed, Lu Yang could finally walk on the ground without the need for Yun Mengmeng and Qinghe to support him on either side.

Although Lu Yang felt that in a quiet environment, he could have gotten out of bed in five days.

However, everyone was a Semi-Immortal, setting aside their own affairs to accompany him, which was a kind gesture, and he couldn’t very well send them away.

Listening to their encouraging tone, it felt not so much like he was able to walk but as if he’d just learned to do so.

"It’s time to continue writing the cultivation technique."

Lu Yang felt he could no longer waste time idly; he needed to find something to do.

Like compiling the cultivation technique for the Void Refinement Stage.

Since Qinghe, Ao Ling, and the others were secrets within the Seek Dao Sect, Lu Yang could only be accompanied by Yun Mengmeng when going to the Scripture Pavilion.

Yun Mengmeng insisted on supporting Lu Yang to the Scripture Pavilion, while Lu Yang was determined to walk there by himself. After a standoff, Yun Mengmeng finally gave in and opted to follow him closely instead.

"Sister Mengmeng, we don’t have to be so vigilant," Lu Yang said helplessly as he looked at Yun Mengmeng.

Yun Mengmeng held her hands as if to support him in the air, ready to react immediately at the first sign of him falling.

"That won’t do. Since Sister Qinghe and Sister Ling can’t protect you now, I’m the only one who can protect you."

Along the way, any disciple who saw Lu Yang took the initiative to greet him, which made Lu Yang feel a bit shy.

Although Yun Mengmeng was not a disciple of the Seek Dao Sect, everyone in the sect knew of her relationship with Yun Zhi, so no one would stop her from entering the Scripture Pavilion.

The two entered the Scripture Pavilion and headed straight for the section dedicated to the Void Refinement Stage.

Lu Yang studied several representative Void Refinement Stage cultivation techniques, and for the first time, he gained a comprehensive understanding of the Void Refinement Stage.

Although he could directly seek guidance from Immortal Fairy Eternity and others, it would be difficult to apply their advice without deep personal understanding if he sought their help without learning anything first.

"So that’s how it is, the Void Refinement Stage doesn’t have early, middle, or late stages, the only way to differentiate them is by observing the degree of realm fluctuations."

"The smaller the realm fluctuations, the closer a cultivator in the Void Refinement Stage is to the end of their cultivation."

"When the realm fluctuations nearly cease, it indicates that the cultivation of the Void Refinement Stage has reached its limit, and one can begin to break through to the Unification Realm."

"Plus, the cultivation at the Void Refinement Stage is extremely difficult, and over ninety percent of cultivators will never break through in their lifetimes."

"There are many methods to reduce realm fluctuations, such as incarnating as a mortal to experience the vicissitudes of life, witnessing the myriad aspects of humanity, and once again comprehending the divide between immortals and mortals."

"Or entering the Dynasty as an official, which not only provides a chance to witness the myriad aspects of humanity but also allows one to protect themselves by using their status in the Dynasty, which is a relatively safe method; however, my master is Daoist Buyu, so I don’t need to consider this method."

"Another method is being imprisoned after committing a crime, observing joys and sorrows from within the prison, which can also ensure self-protection; yet again, my master is Daoist Buyu, and I should not follow in his footsteps."

"Moreover, I am a key minister in the Great Bean Dynasty, holding a position of great power, second only to one, and above thousands. I cannot serve as an official for Great Xia again, and if I were to be imprisoned, it would tarnish the Dynasty’s honor." Lu Yang mused to himself, always mindful of his own position.

"Second only to one, above thousands? Does our Great Bean Dynasty even have that many people?" Immortal Fairy Eternity wondered. She counted on her fingers several times and still couldn’t reach ten thousand people.

It was just her, Little Yang, the Chief Housekeeper, Prime Minister Emperor Xia, the Historian Qinghe, the three Great Generals, Minister of Rites Yun Mengmeng, Minister of the Ministry of Industry Zhu Tian, and Jiang Pingan who held no official title.

In total, there were only ten people.

"Besides, there’s also the likes of Third Senior Sister, who ventured into the Demon Territory for experience, honing themselves amidst danger and speeding up their cultivation progress."

Lu Yang, wounded, could not stay in the Scripture Pavilion to study indefinitely, so he could only borrow a portion of the Void Refinement Stage techniques to take back and study at his leisure.

After Lu Yang returned to his Abode, he delved into the study of the Void Refinement Stage techniques. The others didn’t dare to disturb him, leaving most of the Abode’s space to Lu Yang, taking up only a small corner for themselves.

Sitting on their tiny stools, they formed a circle, and Immortal Fairy Eternity said earnestly,

"Even though you are all Semi-Immortals with considerable strength, your ability to work together still isn’t quite up to par — it needs more practice."

Yun Mengmeng raised her hand: "Chief Housekeeper, how should we practice?"

"Good question. Little Yang often says that practice brings true knowledge, and theory must eventually be put into practice. Our Dynasty’s ultimate goal is to defeat Miss Yun, so let’s start by practicing on her!"

"I am injured and can’t participate, so I can only strategize and arrange formations to give you a fighting chance against Yun Zhi!"

"Are we going to fight Xiao Zhi again?" Yun Mengmeng was the most eager; sparring with Xiao Zhi was fun.

The group leaned back slightly, surprised that they would be engaged in this kind of real combat.

Mumbling to herself, Immortal Fairy Eternity drew diagrams on the ground, arranging interconnected strategies for battle, which everyone acknowledged as ingenious.

"Have you all remembered? Let’s go!"

There’s a saying that in cultivation, time does not exist, and Lu Yang had been engrossed in his techniques for half an hour.

When he came back to his senses, everyone had just returned from their practice, beaten and in worse shape than he was.

Lu Yang took a deep breath; what happened out there? It seemed like their injuries were even worse than his own.

Chapter 1132: 1131: Mortal Transformation or Sustained by War?

"She just lucked out in winning, failing once means nothing, everyone keep pushing, let’s make her use a second move!"

Fairy Eternity motivated everyone effectively.

Although everyone had played their best cards, expended a huge amount of energy, and after a tough battle, still got defeated by Yun Zhi, they didn’t feel tired and appeared very spirited.

Before returning to the Abode, they had all lain on the ground and slept for a long time, now waking up and feeling mostly rested.

Their injuries were more serious than Lu Yang’s, but they had various methods to heal at an astonishing rate, and they were soon back in form.

In the blink of an eye, Emperor Dou personally led the troops, everyone marched grandiosely, leaving only Lu Yang alone in the empty house.

Lu Yang blinked his eyes, not understanding what they were up to.

He ate a piece of flower cake while drinking Enlightenment Tea, and realized something was wrong, quickly picking up a book to shake it.

"Oh no, crumbs fell into the crease of the book!"

When Lu Yang was engrossed in the Cultivation Technique, looking up a second time, he just caught the Great Bean Dynasty troops returning defeated.

"Failing fifteen times means nothing, everyone keep pushing..."

Watching everyone who did not forget to close the door softly before departing, Lu Yang absentmindedly ate a Spirit Fruit, suddenly making a grimace.

"So sour, what kind of Spirit Fruit is this?"

The third time he looked up, Fairy Eternity was once again motivating everyone:

"Failing eighty times means nothing, everyone take a break."

The people were so exhausted they staggered around, no longer having the strength to fight, even Qinghe simply collapsed on the ground from fatigue.

Even though they all were Semi-Immortals, they couldn’t withstand such repeated battles.

However, there were gains too, their combined tactics were advancing rapidly, the only problem was the enemy was too strong, their progress couldn’t be seen.

Lu Yang’s lips twitched, finally he couldn’t hold back and asked, "Fairy, what are you all doing?"

"Conquering strong enemies, extending territories!"

"Establishing unparalleled achievements, to be immortalized in Bean history!"

"Challenging oneself, breaking through limits!"

"Being honored as Marquis, reaching the pinnacle among officials!"

"One conquest forever, securing the land permanently!"

Everyone’s slogan reflected their own goals, Yun Mengmeng and others were shouting excitedly, seemingly not very tired.

The history of the Great Bean Dynasty’s battles was a history of tears and blood, with over eighty battles both big and small, not only did Emperor Dou personally lead the troops, all three Great Generals were mobilized, even the Historian and Minister of Rites who were civil officials had abandoned their pens to join the grim war.

Emperor Dou grieved for the hardships of the common people, regardless of how bad the battle situations were, he absolutely refused to tax them, not adding burdens to the common people, truly a wise ruler of his generation.

"We are fighting Miss Yun!" Fairy Eternity stated with hands on hips, head up as if engaging in something very remarkable.

Boom—

The doors of the Abode opened, the target of the conquest actively sought them out.

"Eldest Senior Sister!" Lu Yang raised his hands, honestly calling out to show he was innocent.

Yun Zhi glanced at the book on Lu Yang’s table, and the notes spread all over, nodded in satisfaction, "Fairy predecessor said you were compiling a Cultivation Technique, they didn’t want to disturb you at the Abode, so they went out to find some things to do. I’m just passing by to see if that’s really the case."

Lu Yang’s eyes twitched, Fairy, you guys might as well have stayed at the Abode playing cards or Mahjong.

"Well done, keep up the studying."

With a few words of encouragement, Yun Zhi prepared to leave, but was stopped by Lu Yang.

"Eldest Senior Sister, if I want to stabilize my realm, should I choose the path of living in peace or the path of fighting to sustain fighting?"

Yun Zhi thought about it and said, "Actually, it doesn’t really matter."

Lu Yang nodded, somewhat understanding Eldest Senior Sister’s point, "Eldest Senior Sister, you mean all roads lead to Rome, and no matter which one I choose, they essentially are the process of understanding one’s heart and validating it, aligning with my cultivation technique, right?"

"No, what I mean is, given your luck, even if you choose to live in peace, it will eventually turn into fighting to sustain."

"..."

Eldest Senior Sister, that makes me sad. Although I enjoy fighting, I never intentionally start conflicts. It is just that the Great Era’s conflicts have arrived, and battles are breaking out everywhere. I just inadvertently get dragged into them.

"Don’t be afraid, Little Yang. The Great Bean Dynasty will always be your strong backbone. Just go on and live in peace, and if you encounter any battles, we’ll fight them for you!" Fairy Eternity said with great loyalty.

The Great Bean Dynasty stood unyielding for years, growing larger and even establishing Crown Prince Lu Yang, all thanks to Fairy Eternity who did not care about abilities but only about close relationships, appointing people purely based on merit and harboring the spirit of brotherhood.

After Eldest Senior Sister left, Lu Yang sighed heavily, unsure of when Eldest Senior Sister would come to assist him and when this undercover identity would end.

In cultivation, there are no seasons. Lu Yang locked himself in his abode, studying tirelessly both day and night, while the rest of the Great Bean Dynasty folks locked themselves in his abode as well, playing around day and night.

Both parties had their own things to do, and the time passed peacefully.

"It’s about time," Lu Yang picked up a stack of paper and flicked it lightly.

These days, he researched cultivation techniques, gleaned meanings, peeled layers after layers, and combined with his understanding, he finally had a piece of work to show.

Then, with a bang, the papers turned to bits of dust.

Lu Yang covered his face. Just now, he had suddenly surged to the Early Tribulation Stage and couldn’t control his strength, reducing his work to ashes.

Luckily, it was his own work, and he could rewrite it quickly.

"I’ll go to Eldest Senior Sister."

Lu Yang had mostly recovered by now; he could walk without limping and didn’t need support.

He opened the entrance to his abode, and Fairy Eternity and others followed him like bodyguards.

Lu Yang found Eldest Senior Sister at the summit of Sky Gate Mountain, where she had been spending her time recently.

Eldest Senior Sister was sitting on the Emperor’s Chair, holding a book of sword techniques while Third Senior Sister blindly played the zither by her, the scenery picturesque as the wind rustled through the grass.

Seeing Lu Yang arrive, Yun Zhi subtly put away the book of sword techniques, but Yun Mengmeng still caught her.

"Ah, Xiao Zhi, you’re looking at the sword techniques again. Can’t you be like me, if you can’t learn it, just give it up?"

Yun Zhi pretended not to hear Yun Mengmeng’s comment, not stooping to argue with her bosom sister.

"Is the cultivation technique finished?"

"Yes."

Lu Yang respectfully handed the cultivation technique to Yun Zhi.

Yun Zhi looked it over and quietly uttered two words, "Not bad."

Compared to when he first started writing cultivation techniques, the junior brother had clearly made significant progress.

"Let me see, let me see."

Hearing Xiao Zhi praise the Second-in-command, Yun Mengmeng clamored to see it, and Ao Ling, Qinghe, and others also shoved their heads together to look at the cultivation technique in Yun Mengmeng’s hands.

"Indeed, not bad." Qinghe was surprised. Although there were a few flaws, those were based on her current perception. If it were her in the Void Refinement Stage, she definitely couldn’t possibly write such a level of cultivation technique.

"Young Sect Master is really impressive."

"Of course, how can our Second-in-command be anything but impressive?"

"Eldest Senior Sister, then this cultivation technique..." Lu Yang looked at Yun Zhi with anticipation.

"Only needs forty percent modification."

Chapter 1133: 1132: Mastering the [Rules]

Compared to the past when Eldest Senior Sister needed to significantly revise the cultivation techniques, this time she only had to make a forty percent revision, a clear sign of Lu Yang’s progress.

After composing these techniques for so long, he had finally passed.

"Sit down and rest for a while, I will correct it for you," Yun Zhi said, gesturing for Lu Yang not to stand.

Yun Zhi took out the Four Treasures of the Study, while Lu Yang stood beside her, grinding ink and passing the brush, learning how Eldest Senior Sister revised his cultivation techniques.

Lu Yang watched attentively as Fairy Eternity rested her head on Lu Yang’s shoulder, watching as well.

Elegant brushstrokes smeared and inscribed on the cultivation techniques, adding and deleting words and sentences, enhancing the content.

Lu Yang suddenly realized that as the Eldest Senior Sister, her vision was indeed higher than his; it seemed that he was far from capable of writing cultivation techniques on his own.

Yun Zhi blew on a stack of papers, drying the ink, "Done."

"Yay, Second-in-command’s cultivation technique is finally complete!" Yun Mengmeng cheered, somehow obtaining a handful of candy and distributing it among everyone to celebrate the hard-won achievement of Lu Yang’s technique.

The candy was wrapped in glutinous rice paper, ready to eat without removing the packaging.

Lu Yang tasted one, sweet with a hint of sourness, probably the flavor of a grape-like spirit fruit.

Yun Zhi’s candy was peach-flavored; although Yun Mengmeng had grown tired of peaches in the secret realm, Yun Zhi hadn’t.

"Alright, Little Yang’s matter is settled, let’s get to the real work!" Immortal Fairy once again rallied everyone for a full assault.

After eighty failures, they calmed their minds, refraining from rushing into battle and instead focusing on recovery. Now, with sufficient rest, and with Yun Zhi having completed Lu Yang’s cultivation technique—rendering him of no further use—the time to strike was now!

"Miss Yun, you mustn’t have anticipated our sudden attack. You’ve probably been on guard against us these past days, but certainly, you didn’t expect that we had no intention of attacking at all. Instead, we’ve been residing in Little Yang’s abode and just playing around. This is waiting for the rabbits to come to us!"

"It’s ’waiting at one’s ease for the exhausted enemy,’ Fairy." Although Lu Yang was indeed surprised on behalf of Eldest Senior Sister, he couldn’t help but correct her.

"Ah, right, that’s the phrase, Little Yang is correct! Charge!"

At the command of Immortal Fairy, all the women attacked at once.

The three Great Generals transformed into their original forms, a black Dragon, a fire Phoenix, and a white Tiger, majestically towering with teeth as tall as a person and shining white enough to reflect Yun Zhi’s shadow.

Had anyone harbored guilt in their hearts, the sight of these ancient Immortal Beasts would send them scrambling in terror.

Qinghe held a lotus flower, activating the Longevity Dao Fruit Embryo to slow time.

Yun Mengmeng imbued the clothing of everyone present with the power of the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, ensuring that the garments would not be damaged in battle.

Looking at the eager crowd, Yun Zhi held her forehead and sighed, instructing Senior Sister Gan Tian to tidy up things like the Emperor’s Chair to avoid damaging it.

Senior Sister Gan Tian moved adeptly. Initially surprised by the women challenging Eldest Senior Sister, she was now accustomed to it.

After putting everything away, Gan Tian continued to play the guqin, the tunes no longer soothing but filled with the vigor of armed combat.

Thud, thud, dong, dong—

Inspired by Gan Tian’s music, the women, brimming with fighting spirit, set up an Array Method to besiege Yun Zhi.

"Dragon and Phoenix Tiger Transformation!" This was the combined attack technique of the three Great Generals, subtly in line with the power of the three talents. Jiang Lianyi and Jin Caiwei turned into flat tattoos, adhering to Ao Ling’s body.

"Immortality!" Yun Mengmeng and Qinghe acted together, harnessing their closely-related Dao Fruit Outlines to create a realm where time nearly stood still.

This was the result of their eighty battles, allowing them to bridge the gap between mortals and Immortals, even challenging an Immortal in combat!

Soon, the women fell one by one to the ground, sleeping sweetly like dumplings into boiling water.

Yun Zhi, step by step, approached Lu Yang, radiating a murderous aura, with a field of slain sprawled out before them, causing Lu Yang to tremble with fear.

"Junior Brother, it’s time for you to return to your cultivation."

Lu Yang nodded like a pecking chick.

Fairy Eternity was still not convinced, "Hmph, Miss Yun, don’t be so proud. If I weren’t seriously injured and unable to condense my physical body, I would definitely challenge you to a duel!"

Yun Zhi’s cheeks twitched, pretending not to hear what was said.

Lu Yang stepped in to mediate, easing the tension between Eldest Senior Sister and Fairy Eternity, "His Majesty has personally taken to the battlefield, aiming to bolster the morale and spirit of the troops, truly a wise decision. However, the military is a tool of danger; war a perilous endeavor. Victory and defeat are common in military campaigns; it’s not just the victory that is precious, but the ability to withdraw and plan anew also does not diminish a hero’s true nature!"

Fairy Eternity pondered over Lu Yang’s words seriously, "Are you suggesting that this Immortal should retreat?"

"Yes."

"Oh, then let’s go back."

Fortunately, Yun Zhi understood the principle of not pursuing a defeated enemy too far. After their victory, she did not press for a complete annihilation, giving Lu Yang and the others ample time to withdraw. Lu Yang had no choice but to carry each of the women back to the Abode one by one.

The women were still sleeping and didn’t seem like they would wake up soon, so Lu Yang began to focus on studying the Cultivation Technique.

After cycling through the Grand Circulation with the new technique, Lu Yang felt his Realm slightly stabilize, which meant that even if he did nothing and continued to cultivate step by step, he would eventually be able to advance to the Body Integration Realm.

"However, the rate is rather slow."

"That’s quite normal. It means you haven’t grasped the essence of the Void Refinement Stage," Fairy Eternity floated leisurely around the Abode, her feet off the ground, like a ghost.

"Essence?"

"I ask you, what’s the biggest difference between the Void Refinement Stage and the Body Integration Realm?"

"Difference? Control of the Rule?"

"Right, controlling the Rule. The better your grasp of the Rule, the more stable your Realm will become."

"So that’s how it is, but how exactly do you control the Rule?" asked Lu Yang, the Emperor’s Master, unashamed to seek knowledge, as he was completely clueless about the Rule.

"You are currently at the verge of the Body Integration Realm, close your eyes and try to extend your Spiritual Power outward, to sense your Abode."

Taking advantage of the momentary stability of his Realm, Lu Yang released his Spiritual Power throughout the Abode. He could see every nook and cranny clearly, even the sight of the women sleeping peacefully, as well as the crumbs from the cookies they had dropped on the ground earlier.

"Next, imagine your body becoming ethereal, merging with the space of your Abode."

Imagination was one of Lu Yang’s strengths. He imagined himself as a pool of water, spreading in all directions, covering every inch of space as though his Abode had become part of his domain, subject to his whims.

Fairy Eternity’s teachings echoed in Lu Yang’s ears, "Lastly, and most importantly, words carry power. Each phrase you utter carries your strength."

"Now, establish a Rule within this space. Any Rule will do."

"I hereby establish the [Rule: within the Abode, all cookie crumbs must go into the trash can]."

As the Rule was established, the crumbs on the floor flew up and went straight into the trash can.

"Yes, yes, yes, that’s the idea!" Fairy Eternity didn’t expect Lu Yang to succeed on his first try.

Buoyed by his success, Lu Yang continued his cultivation, "I hereby establish the [Rule: within the Abode, books must dance]."

The books on the desk, borrowed from the Scripture Pavilion, stood up, their corners like legs, hopping and dancing on the table, defying common sense.

Lu Yang felt he was close to uncovering the trick. With such a genius act, the more spectators, the better.

"I hereby establish the [Rule: Yun Mengmeng, Qinghe, Ao Ling, Jiang Lianyi, Jin Caiwei wake up]!"

"Wait, you can’t set Rules for people with a higher Cultivation Level than you!" Fairy Eternity hastily said.

"Pfft!"

The moment the Rule was established, Lu Yang sensed something was wrong, a rush of blood surged upward, and he violently spat out a mouthful of blood.

The women had just opened their eyes to see Lu Yang lying on the ground, spewing blood like a fountain.

Chapter 1134: 1133: Disregarding Sword Immortal Lu’s Kind Intentions

"Senior Brother, are you all right?"

Lu Yang genuinely frightened all the girls, who were alarmed to find him seriously injured when they had just seen him perfectly fine before they went to sleep. How could he have suddenly sustained such heavy injuries?

Was he beaten by the Eldest Senior Sister?

The girls hurriedly carried Lu Yang to the bed.

"Cough cough, just a minor accident," he said.

Lu Yang had gotten too excited about mastering a rule and now, after spitting out a few mouthfuls of hot blood, he had calmed down. How could he even think of commanding five Semi-Immortals with a Body Integration Realm rule? That was practically courting death.

Indeed, one should never become too ecstatic.

Fortunately, the rule he had set was not the kind that twisted causality against the heavens, which merely woke the girls up. Otherwise, his injuries could have been worse than when he faced the Thunder Tribulation.

Despite the blood he was spitting out, influenced by the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo, Lu Yang felt rather comfortable.

No, no, this comfort was unacceptable. Lu Yang forced himself to stop.

He worried he might one day seek out pain for pleasure, such was the terrifying effect of the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo, changing his habits without a sound.

Knock knock.

The knocking on the door came, and Fairy Eternity slipped into the Spiritual Space in a flash, Jin Caiwei hurried to answer it, and Tao Yaoye was at the door.

"Elder Brother Lu Yang, how’s your injury?"

During the time Lu Yang was healing, Tao Yaoye had visited a few times. It wasn’t her first time seeing Yun Mengmeng and a room full of people guarding Lu Yang, but every time she felt like she was losing to them.

"Senior Brother, are you all right?"

Seeing the blood at the corner of Lu Yang’s mouth and the bedsheet soaked with blood, she was shocked. How had his injuries gotten so severe?

"There’s no need to worry, Junior Sister Tao," Lu Yang said in a nonchalant tone. "I just hit a snag while cultivating. It’s common to have such minor issues; I’ll just need to be more careful next time."

Since Lu Yang acted as if nothing were amiss, Tao Yaoye didn’t feel it appropriate to say much more.

"I come on behalf of Junior Brother Man to visit you. A few days ago, Junior Brother Man wanted to visit and say farewell in person, but at the time you were in seclusion, so he asked me to pass on his message before he left."

Those few days were when Lu Yang was in seclusion, studying and writing cultivation techniques, refusing all visitors. He had only just removed the "In Seclusion" sign not long ago.

"Does Junior Brother Man have some mission that requires him to travel far?" Lu Yang wondered. Was Man Gu going to be away for a long time, that he needed to say goodbye to him?

"No, he went to the Imperial City to take part in the Imperial Examination."

"Ah?"

"Junior Brother Man believes that the best way to achieve Enlightenment among the common folk is to join the Dynasty as an official and become a regional governor."

If he waited to break through to the Void Refinement Stage before taking the exams, it would be dangerous to even leave the house. He could only take the exams before the breakthrough.

"Although our Sect could recommend him for a position in the Dynasty, he prefers to become an official through his own scholarly achievements."

Considering Man Gu was slightly above Fairy Eternity in level, Lu Yang was quite worried about him.

On the contrary, Fairy Eternity had a lot of confidence in Man Gu: "Man Gu will definitely pass. He is the only one from the Ancient Barbarian Tribe and received a blessing from Immortal herself."

This only made Lu Yang more concerned.

After Tao Yaoye left a new Recording Orb and departed, Fairy Eternity finally emerged from the Spiritual Space.

"In fact, to master the rules, staying in Seek Dao Sect is not the best option."

Lu Yangxin’s heart stirred, and he thought of an excellent place, "Are you talking about Buddha Country?"

"Exactly, the best choice is Buddha Country. Mastering the rules is fundamentally about comprehending heaven and earth. With an Immortal Body in Buddha Country, the effect is even better than if you were to ascend to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage and comprehend heaven and earth!"

Given the opportunity to go to Buddha Country, Lu Yang had no reason to disagree.

He could finally be an Immortal again.

Before heading to Buddha Country, Lu Yang kindly asked his good brother Old Meng if he would like to accompany him.

Meng Jingzhou looked at Lu Yang and the five Semi-Immortals behind him then thought about Lu Yang becoming an Immortal in Buddha Country while he was still known as a Buddha’s son. If I go to Buddha Country, will I have a single day of peace?

Lu Yang earnestly advised Meng Jingzhou, implying only his well-being, "Old Meng, you should seize this chance for an Immortal fate. Once I’m in Buddha Country, I’ll be an Immortal, and you’ll have an Immortal brother. I’m happy for you just thinking about it."

"Get lost, get lost, get lost."

Meng Jingzhou disregarded Sword Immortal Lu’s kind intentions. Sword Immortal Lu couldn’t say much more, so he, along with Fairy Eternity, Yun Mengmeng, Qinghe, Ao Ling, Jiang Lianyi, and Jin Caiwei, set out alone for Buddha Country.

Theoretically, someone at the Void Refinement Stage should go out as little as possible. There were several days of travel from Seek Dao Sect to Buddha Country; however, with many Semi-Immortals accompanying him, there shouldn’t be any issues.

Apart from Lu Yang pulling out his sword to assist whenever he saw injustice on the road, saving a damsel in distress, and subsequently finding out it was for a Dream Mirage film, in which he accidentally appeared, the director hearing of Lu Yang’s appearance sincerely invited him to make a cameo in the Dream Mirage.

And accidentally entering a sealed-off small mountain village where a female ghost at the Consummation of Qi Refinement level died with grievances, killing passers-by and casting an illusory curse on Lu Yang, only to be slain by a strand of his hair. Further investigation by Lu Yang revealed that the man who killed the ghost had since become a prince consort, prompting Lu Yang to write a letter, hoping Emperor Xia would deal with it.

In order to avoid trouble and hide his identity, Lu Yang went to a barbecue restaurant again and discovered issues with customer deception and non-fresh ingredients, with a staff member even yelling at Lu Yang, saying, "What can you do about it?" Lu Yang immediately used a talisman to contact the Jiuyou Sect, asking them to focus on quality control while expanding and to deal with this staff member as they saw fit. Upon hearing this, the Sect Master of Jiuyou Sect personally hurried over, greeting Young Sect Master Lu with a line of trembling members, scaring the staff member out of his wits.

Only then did he safely reach Buddha Country.

"I always said Eldest Senior Sister was not good at judging people, look how well this worldly transformation worked out. There was no need to fuel wars through fighting." Lu Yang felt this journey was very peaceful; he wouldn’t have needed Yun Mengmeng and the others to protect him.

After arriving in Buddha Country, Lu Yang condensed his Immortal Body and, with the help of Fairy Eternity’s Immortal Sense, became an Immortal.

"This is much more comfortable," Lu Yang couldn’t help but sigh. There really was no better place than Buddha Country, they didn’t have such good days in Seek Dao Sect.

"This is Elder Brother Lu Yang’s Immortal Body." The ladies marveled. Except for Fairy Eternity and Qinghe, they had only heard about Lu Yang being able to condense an Immortal Body and had never truly seen it.

Today, they witnessed it and indeed found it miraculous.

...

Imperial City, Imperial Examination Hall.

Today is the day of the Imperial Examination, an event held every five years, gathering talented individuals from across the world to display their abilities in writing.

Just as the examination began, a student experienced a crisis, breaking out in profuse sweat and raising his hand to signal.

The invigilator walked over unhurriedly, "What’s the matter, candidate?"

The invigilator glanced at the candidate’s ID tag: Man Gu.

"I have an issue; may I leave the room temporarily?"

The invigilator frowned and reprimanded, looking askance at Man Gu: "Of course not. The examination lasts for three days, and during this period no candidate may leave. You want to leave as soon as the exam starts. Are you trying to pass on the questions to your accomplices?"

Such incidents had happened several times in the history of the examination.

Desperate and sweating profusely, Man Gu said, "But I really can’t hold it in."

"Eating, drinking, and relieving oneself must all be done here. No leaving is permitted!"

"It’s my Realm I can’t hold in."

Chapter 1135: 1134: Seeking Help Outside the Arena

The examiner’s expression changed drastically, no longer maintaining his previously light and breezy demeanor.

Among the candidates, there were not a few cultivators. To prevent the cultivators from cheating, the Imperial Examination Hall had set up a Spirit Extinguishing Array Formation, rendering all cultivator methods useless.

But what array method could withstand the descent of Heavenly Tribulation?

"You, you need to get out of here!"

"It’s too late." Man Gu’s forehead was slick with cold sweat as he looked up at the sky. In a flash, the weather changed, with lightning flashing and thunder roaring. The Heavenly Tribulation had locked on to Man Gu!

"Candidates, evacuate the examination area quickly!" the examiner shouted. Since Man Gu couldn’t leave, the candidates needed to get out fast. This was no joke—if the tribulation struck, few present could withstand it.

Having never seen such a spectacle before, the candidates panicked. Such a tribulation was what one faced when advancing from the Divinity Transformation Stage to the Void Refinement Stage. Whether the city they were in even had cultivators at the Divinity Transformation Stage was questionable, let alone having witnessed a Heavenly Tribulation.

The candidates hurriedly evacuated the examination area, leaving Man Gu behind alone.

Man Gu’s face went pale as he looked at the rolling thunder in the sky.

This was bad. The more people there were around during the transcending of the Heavenly Tribulation, the more powerful the tribulation became. Just now, the Heavenly Tribulation had not only locked onto him but also onto the tens of thousands of candidates, causing the power of the Thunder Tribulation to skyrocket far beyond his capability!

To say nothing of the fact that he was currently only at the Divinity Transformation Stage, even if he were at the Body Integration Realm, he couldn’t withstand it.

Moreover, it was too late for the candidates to escape. The Heavenly Tribulation regarded them all as factors affecting the transcendence and intended to strike them all down with thunder!

"This definitely won’t do!"

Gritting his teeth, Man Gu sacrificed his primordial spirit and clasped his hands together, muttering a prayer, "Immortal, please protect us. Man Gu humbly summons the mightiest ancestor of the Barbarian tribe!"

His primordial spirit could call upon the strength that the ancestors of the Barbarian tribe had left in this world.

Different from the last time he had summoned an ancestor of the Barbarian tribe when he had called upon the most powerful ancestor during the Divinity Transformation Stage in Ancient Times.

This time, he cast aside levels and called upon the absolute strongest of all the ancient Barbarian ancestors!

With a crack, the resplendent thunder descended, and the candidates could no longer control their fear, crouching down while holding their heads.

A slender figure tore through the void, striding forward to face the vicious onslaught of the Thunder Tribulation. A corner of his mouth curled up in a disdainful smile, and with a casual wave of his hand, he repelled the thunderous attack.

"Someone has repelled the Thunder Tribulation with one strike—who is this Great Power?!"

"I don’t recognize him."

"He just appeared out of thin air, what realm does a Great Power need to be in to do that?"

Both the candidates and the examiners buzzed with discussion but could not identify this figure with any known world-renowned powerhouse.

The strong ones in the Imperial City also noticed the commotion at the Imperial Examination Hall. In the dark, world-covered heavens and earth, they saw only that slender white figure courageously facing the Thunder Tribulation, standing unshaken, which was awe-inspiring.

The slender figure seemed like an Immortal King seated high in the nine heavens, indifferent to the world, disregarding the discussions of the candidates.

Lu Yangxin felt secretly pleased within his heart, thinking that it was the right time to arrive. Let everyone shout louder, even louder.

This was the meaning of cultivation, wasn’t it? If he didn’t show off in front of a crowd, what was the point of his cultivation?

Having just arrived in the Buddha Country to consolidate his Immortal Body, Lu Yangxin had felt someone calling out to him, similar to how Man Gu had called out to him before.

When Man Gu last summoned him, he had been caught off guard and was directly pulled over. Now that he was an Immortal, he, of course, couldn’t be pulled over directly.

However, if Man Gu was summoning him, there must be a reason. Lu Yangxin’s true form couldn’t leave the Buddha Country, so he could only split off a fraction of his power to help Man Gu, to see what was happening.

And he had encountered the Heavenly Tribulation upon arrival.

Although the Lu Yangxin who came to the Imperial City was just a clone, it was more than enough to deal with the Heavenly Tribulation, easily shattering it with a lift of his hand.

Man Gu was also stunned. Did he have such a powerful presence among his ancestors?

"Junior Brother Man, it’s me." Lu Yang sent a message to Man Gu. He wasn’t sure of the situation on Man Gu’s side, so he had disguised himself before coming.

"Brother Lu?!" Man Gu’s mouth dropped open in disbelief. Had Lu Yang become the strongest being in their Barbarian tribe?

Boom—

The second Thunder Tribulation followed swiftly, giving Lu Yang no chance to catch his breath.

Lu Yang soared into the sky above the Imperial City and with the Buddha Land in Palm, extended one finger, which was enormously huge, easily covering more than ten Imperial Examination Halls.

Lu Yang pointed to the Cave Heaven, letting the Thunder Tribulation strike his finger while casually chatting with Man Gu to understand the whole situation.

"So, you accidentally broke through your realm during the Imperial Examination," Lu Yang found the situation amusing. Many cultivators at the Spirit Transformation Stage spent their entire lives unable to find the opportunity to break through, yet Man Gu had to suppress his realm, a shame he couldn’t keep it under control.

"The Array Method in the examination hall suppressed the Spiritual Energy within my body, causing me to lose control over my realm," Man Gu exclaimed his innocence. He had originally planned to make a breakthrough in cultivation as a celebration after securing an official position.

"Let it be, since this was supposed to be your Heavenly Tribulation, you were bound to face it."

Lu Yang intercepted most of the Thunder Tribulation, allowing only a small portion to fall on Man Gu.

To breakthrough, one must be struck by lightning; this is a necessary part of the body tempering process and simply cannot be skipped.

Man Gu shouted out loud, invoking the bloodline of the Ancient Barbarian Tribe, enduring the explosions of lightning on his body that tore skin and flesh without uttering a cry of pain, upholding the name of the Ancient Barbarian Tribe.

Fairy Eternity floated out from the Spiritual Space, with Little Yang and Junior Brother Man both undergoing their Thunder Tribulation, she seemed to be the only one unoccupied.

"Let this Immortal see what this Imperial Examination tests," she mused.

"The way of the great learning lies in enhancing illustrious virtue, in loving the people, in stopping at the utmost good. Debate—what is the great learning?"

"Is it appropriate for the Prime Minister to summon wise talents from all directions for counsel and see guests for discussion in his private residence?"

"What do all these mean?"

Fairy Eternity scratched her head, feeling the questions were incomprehensible, even more difficult to understand than Little Yang’s words.

If they were to hold an Imperial Examination in the Great Bean Dynasty, they certainly wouldn’t pose such questions.

"Little Yang will surely..."

Fairy Eternity considered seeking advice but looked up to see Lu Yang still performing rather spectacular moves to resist the Thunder Tribulation, drawing cheers from below, very popular, all the while controlling the lightning striking Man Gu, unable to disengage.

"Never mind, I’ll ask someone else."

With the examination paper in hand and brimming with excitement, Fairy Eternity found Jiang Pingan, who was farming in the Forbidden Land of the Imperial Palace.

"Hey, are you the Immortal from the Jiang Family?"

Jiang Pingan, who was farming, shuddered and almost didn’t hold his hoe steady.

What the hell, someone had managed to approach him so silently.

He turned around, and there was a very beautiful female ghost.

"Are you the Immortal Fairy from ancient times?" It wasn’t hard for Jiang Pingan to guess Fairy Eternity’s identity.

"Daoist Ping An, although you are a subject of the Great Bean Dynasty, you remain a commoner, with not even a half-official position."

"If you want to be an official of the Great Bean Dynasty, you must possess extraordinary talent."

Fairy Eternity took out the Imperial Examination paper: "If you can answer these questions correctly, you may choose any official position you desire."

Jiang Pingan took the paper and scratched his head in puzzlement; these were not his forte.

Had he been able to pass the Imperial Examination, he wouldn’t have had to rebel at the end of the Great Yu period.

"Just a moment, I’ll ask someone."

Jiang Pingan and Fairy Eternity found Emperor Xia in Daming Hall, who was reviewing memorials.

"Do you know how to answer these questions?"

Emperor Xia looked up at his ancestor and the Ancient Immortal, then back down at the examination paper, falling silent for a long time.

Aren’t these the exam questions I set?

Chapter 1136: 1135: Does Ancestral Blessing Work Wonders?

"I suppose so..." Emperor Xia hesitated before nodding.

He had come up with the questions himself to prevent the examiners from leaking them.

The problem was that he hadn’t considered that he would have to answer the questions himself.

However, he had become the Emperor through true merit; his cultural level among the emperors of Great Xia was exceptionally high, so he was definitely capable of answering the questions.

"Then stop dawdling and write!" Jiang Pingan glared. "Out of us three, you’re the only one with any education. What are you waiting for, me to write it for you?"

Fairy Eternity felt her governance philosophies were quite sound.

As the ruler of a country, why should she know everything? She was responsible for setting policies and directing the overall strategy—like defeating Miss Yun, for example.

As for the trivial matter of the Imperial Examination, that was for her subordinates to handle.

"Oh right, you must copy the handwriting from this."

Fairy Eternity pulled out notes that Man Gu had made and instructed Emperor Xia to imitate them. She was meticulous and left no loopholes.

Man Gu might look rough and hulking, like he could eat three Emperor Xias in one sitting, but as a Confucian Cultivator, his handwriting was actually quite beautiful—neat with a grand and powerful aura.

Emperor Xia: "..."

Facing his ancestor and immediate superior, Emperor Xia had no choice but to put aside the memorial and start answering questions on Man Gu’s behalf.

When he had come up with the questions, Emperor Xia had a rough idea about them and only needed a bit of thought to smoothly write out the answers.

Jiang Pingan and Fairy Eternity were waiting for the answers while casually chatting on the side.

Jiang Pingan slapped his thigh, agreeing wholeheartedly with Fairy Eternity, "I told you, ’the esteemed of ninety-five’ refers to Immortal Jiuchong inventing the game of Five-in-a-Row. The officials of Great Yu are uneducated. They stubbornly argued with me, claiming that it referred to the Emperor, causing me to fail even the county examination."

He felt as though a wrong had been righted. See, as an Ancient Immortal, one simply knew more.

"I’ve always thought that the Five Immortals of Ancient Times symbolize the heavens and the earth on a chessboard. Forming a line with five pieces signifies victory, representing that when the Five Immortals unite, they can conquer any obstacle!"

"Tsk, no one believed me back then. They even accused me of being uneducated, of being jealous of the virtuous."

Jiang Pingan thought the state of affairs at the end of the Great Yu period was deplorable, forcing a scholar like him to become the founding Emperor.

Emperor Xia listened to their conversation while writing. He thought to himself, if you, ancestor, believe you’re educated, why don’t you come answer the questions? Do you even understand them?

"Are you badmouthing me, boy?" Jiang Pingan looked suspiciously at Emperor Xia.

"How could I dare to?" Emperor Xia replied with a placating smile and handed the completed examination to Jiang Pingan, "Please review it, great ancestor."

Jiang Pingan glanced at the paper and, not understanding what Emperor Xia had written, patted the saintly grandson’s shoulder, "Hey, what are you looking at? I trust you!"

Jiang Pingan passed the paper to Fairy Eternity, "Daoist Immortal, does this mean I’ve passed the exam?"

Fairy Eternity didn’t understand what Emperor Xia had written either. His level was far below that of Little Yang—had Little Yang been here, she certainly would have understood.

But she couldn’t show weakness and had to pretend that Emperor Xia had written excellently.

"Of course, you are now the Imperial College Sacrificial Officer of the Great Bean Dynasty!"

Jiang Pingan’s eyes lit up with joy. The Imperial College Sacrificial Officer was a high-ranking official of the most prestigious educational institution, a position that could only be held by the truly gifted.

Although he could have claimed the title himself in Great Xia, nothing compared to being acknowledged by the chief of the Five Ancient Immortals.

"Daoist Immortal, let’s chat more some other time."

Knowing Fairy Eternity was busy, Jiang Pingan didn’t try to detain her.

When Fairy Eternity returned to the Imperial Examination Hall with the perfect score paper, Lu Yang and Man Gu were still busy with their Tribulation. Man Gu was disheveled from the Thunder Tribulation but still alive and vigorous, able to continue resisting.

She put down the examination paper and slipped back into Lu Yang’s Spiritual Space.

"Fairy, where did you run off to?" Lu Yang was puzzled. The Fairy had been gone for nearly half a day, and the lack of chatter in the Spiritual Space was oddly unsettling.

"Fairy Eternity was of course looking out for Junior Brother Man while also paying a visit to Jiang Pingan. Let me tell you, Jiang Pingan’s level of education is not low. He’s on par with me, so I appointed him as the Imperial College Sacrificial Officer," Fairy Eternity gestured flamboyantly. Even without Little Yang’s help, she felt like she was already quite remarkable at appointing officials on her own.

She looked at Lu Yang expectantly.

Lu Yang: "..."

What do you want me to say, Fairy? I think this Great Bean Dynasty is doomed sooner or later.

Fortunately, he still belonged to Eldest Senior Sister. If the Great Bean Dynasty fell, he could still take Fairy Eternity and seek refuge with Eldest Senior Sister.

"Fairy is really awesome!" Lu Yang understood Fairy Eternity’s expression and gave a heartfelt thumbs-up.

"That’s for sure. Who do you think I am?"

Fairy Eternity’s face beamed with delight.

Although she could praise herself, it wasn’t as genuine as being praised by a great loyalist like Little Yang.

Lu Yang faced the Thunder Tribulation in the Imperial City for three days, making quite a scene.

Of course, the main reason was to help Man Gu’s body become tempered by the heavenly thunder. The longer the body tempering, the better the effect, and making a scene was something he had to do.

"Junior Brother Man, I’m leaving first. Oh, he passed out."

Lu Yang noticed his power dissipating, which meant that even with Man Gu’s summoning spell, this clone could only be sustained for three days.

It was time to leave.

Before leaving, Lu Yang calculated the time for the Heavenly Tribulation to disperse and, like a giant towering into the sky, he pointed at the tribulation clouds and issued a word with an arrogant and unruly tone.

"Disperse!"

The word ’disperse’ spread throughout the entire Imperial City, deafeningly loud. The tribulation clouds were affected by this force, turning from black to white, and the Heavenly Tribulation vanished.

Man Gu was struck unconscious by the heavenly thunder, and divine rain descended, healing his wounds. After Lu Yang and Fairy Eternity left, he woke up in a daze, looking at the fully written exam paper in confusion.

"Do I still have a habit of sleepwalking?"

"Your Majesty, this is the top scorer of this Imperial Examination." The chief examiner presented Emperor Xia with a clean and tidy exam paper.

Emperor Xia saw that it was the exact one he had written and felt great.

His abilities had not declined after all.

"Minister Chu, what do you think of how this exam paper is answered?"

The chief examiner replied respectfully, "Your Majesty, this candidate has average ability, with obscure reasoning and vague explanations that are incomprehensible. His becoming top scorer is just a matter of luck."

Click—

Emperor Xia’s veins bulged as he quietly snapped the writing brush, asking with a smile that did not reach his eyes, "Luck?"

"Because during this top scorer examination, we encountered a Pass Tribulation, resulting in only this candidate completing all the questions, while the other candidates had only blank papers, which were not included in the scoring."

"In such a case, even if this candidate’s answers were a complete mess, he would still receive some marks for completing the paper, allowing him to become the top scorer."

Emperor Xia maintained a subtle smile, not letting anyone catch on.

"Is that so? Well, I find this exam paper to be well answered and quite pleasing to me."

"Let’s do this. This candidate will be the top scholar of this Imperial Examination. I will set a new set of questions, and the other candidates can retake the examination in three days."

...

"Ah? I’m the top scholar?"

Man Gu learned of his rank as the top scholar while he was reminiscing about the taste of his hometown at a barbecue shop.

"Are the blessings from the ancestors that effective?"

Chapter 1137: 1136: Soybean Dynasty Ancestral Temple

Lu Yang and Fairy Eternity’s consciousness had returned to Buddha Country at the same time.

"Hm? Where is this?"

Lu Yang found himself in a dilapidated temple, sitting cross-legged on a lotus platform, fingers posed like a lotus flower, almost resembling a Buddha statue.

Outside, there was the steady drizzle of rain.

"Ah, has the Young Sect Master awoken?" Jin Caiwei had been staying by Lu Yang’s side. Realizing that Lu Yang had finally awoken, she went out to call back the other women.

Lu Yang was a bit confused. He dimly remembered being summoned away by Man Gu three days ago, just when he had arrived in Buddha Country, surrounded by a desert. It had only been three days, so how had he been moved to a temple?

And which temple was this?

"Are you awake?"

"Oh, this temple is quite interesting." Fairy Eternity floated out, curiously inspecting the temple; the person who had built this temple had quite some ideas.

"Has the Second-in-command awoken?"

"Is Immortal Sister back as well?"

"Greetings to the lord on your return."

The women had waited for three days and finally, both Fairy Eternity and Lu Yang had awoken.

"Third Boss, was it you who built this temple?" Fairy Eternity asked with a smile. Lu Yang was inexperienced and couldn’t tell, but she had immediately noticed something unique about the temple.

Yun Mengmeng chuckled sheepishly, "Well, it wasn’t just my idea alone. The Chief Housekeeper and Second-in-command left without saying when they’d return, and we couldn’t just keep the bosses standing in the desert forever. We thought about entering the city, but with so many of us and since you always say we should keep a low profile when out, we decided to just build a temple right here to shelter us from the wind and rain."

"Then I suggested that since we were building a temple, why not build it out of delicious ingredients."

As she spoke, Yun Mengmeng walked up to Lu Yang, plucked a piece of the lotus he was sitting on, and put it in his mouth.

"Second-in-command, try this, isn’t it delicious?"

Lu Yang subconsciously took a bite and was surprised to find that the lotus was incredibly tasty, although he had no idea what it was made of.

"This isn’t lotus, is it? It’s a leaf from the Eternal Life Spiritual Fruit, right?" asked Fairy Eternity.

"Reporting to the lord, yes," said Qinghe. The leaves were cultivated from the Longevity Dao Fruit Embryo, barely distinguishable from immortal objects, and too precious to be valued with Spirit Stones.

"These windows are made of flat Spirit Fruits!" Jin Caiwei excitedly added. She had put quite an effort into building the temple.

"The lamp oil is honey I brought from Demon Territory."

"...So this whole temple is edible?"

Lu Yang finally understood why Fairy Eternity had praised the temple’s unique construction—it truly was unique, functioning as both shelter and food.

"It’s raining outside, right? Is the temple going to be okay?" Judging from the sound, it was raining heavily outside.

"No worries, the materials we used to build the temple are all waterproof!" Yun Mengmeng confidently said. Their main goal in building the temple was indeed to provide shelter from the rain and wind, so how could it possibly be affected by the rain.

"Is it not completed yet? Some parts still look broken. If it’s not finished, I can help."

Lu Yang pointed to a part of a wall with a small hole in it.

Jin Caiwei cautiously raised her hand, "That was where I ate from."

"..."

So you guys were building and eating at the same time?

Yun Mengmeng, grinning, took Fairy Eternity and Lu Yang by the hands and led them outside the temple.

"Look, this is the name everyone came up with for the temple."

The plaque above read, "Soybean Temple," the characters exceptionally eye-catching.

"This temple is indeed nicely built, let’s just treat it as the ancestral temple of our Great Bean Dynasty," Fairy Eternity suggested. Every dynasty had built ancestral temples, and though their Great Bean Dynasty was new, sooner or later, they would need one too.

Everyone except Lu Yang was delighted, which meant that Immortal Fairy had definitely approved of their three days of labor.

The Great Bean Dynasty finally had a foothold in the real world.

Lu Yang felt that the construction of the Ancestral Temple was indescribable, but since everyone agreed, he, as an Immortal, felt it inappropriate to comment further.

"I’ll also lend a hand to perfect our Ancestral Temple."

"Here I establish the [Rule: Refurbishing the Great Bean Dynasty Ancestral Temple]."

In Buddha Country, using his Immortal Body to set down rules was a thousand times easier than at the Seek Dao Sect. Buddha Country truly was a wonderful place.

Countless golden Power of Faith emerged, transforming into food to fill the gaps in the Ancestral Temple.

In the blink of an eye, the Ancestral Temple was as good as new, just like when it was first built.

Jin Caiwei, curious, had a taste of the repaired part and shook her head, "Young Sect Master, this won’t do; the food produced by the power of rules lacks soul."

"I’d like to try too." Yun Mengmeng, Qinghe, and the others came forward to taste it, all feeling it wasn’t as delicious as what they had made.

"Little Yang, you need to have a respectful heart toward food," Immortal Fairy also came out to point out the flaws in Lu Yang’s thinking.

Lu Yang: "..."

I’m sorry I was wrong. May I try practicing the power of rules in another way?

Out of respect for food, Lu Yang withdrew the power of the rules and reverted the Ancestral Temple to its dilapidated state.

"Someone’s coming."

Suddenly, Lu Yang spoke as he released his Immortal Sense to perceive the world and detected a group of people rushing this way, all mortals, seemingly seeking shelter from the rain.

"Hurry, hide."

Everyone slipped into the temple, either sitting or standing on the Lotus Platform, altering their appearances to mimic Buddha or Bodhisattva figures.

"Uncle Wang, there’s a temple here, let’s take shelter from the rain!"

A hurried group arrived, overjoyed to see the temple.

"Strange, when did a temple appear here?" Uncle Wang stood outside, feeling something was amiss.

"Soybean Temple, I’ve never heard of such a place."

"Let’s just hurry inside."

If the rain kept pouring, they’d surely catch a cold, making it troublesome to continue their journey.

They rushed into the temple, finding it rundown but still good enough to shelter them from the wind and rain.

"Phew—survival."

They unloaded their luggage and checked for any wet spots, stripping off clothes to dry them.

"Which Buddha is this?" They grew curious about the temple during their downtime and didn’t recognize any of the Buddha or Bodhisattva statues, but all the statues were dignified, inspiring awe.

"Look at this little Bodhisattva, so small."

Referencing Jin Caiwei.

"Silence, maintain devotion, do not be disrespectful!" Uncle Wang scolded everyone, leading them to kneel before each statue of Buddha and Bodhisattva, thanking them for providing shelter from the rain.

Finally, Uncle Wang took out a bag of Spirit Stones, not many but still a sign of goodwill, and placed it at the feet of a Buddha statue inside a Merit Box.

"Uncle Wang, aren’t those the Spirit Stones you picked up from the grave yesterday? Why give them to a Buddha statue?" a fellow traveler asked, puzzled.

Uncle Wang sighed deeply, reflecting, "Yesterday I found this bag of Spirit Stones, and today we encountered a temple. Where in the world does such a coincidence occur? It’s fate. These Spirit Stones shouldn’t belong to me; they should be donated to the temple."

After the rain cleared, Uncle Wang and others led their horses away from the temple.

Lu Yang hopped down from the Lotus Platform, looking at the Merit Box below his feet with a strange expression, sensing that this bag of Spirit Stones had formed a connection with his lifespan.

"How come this looks so much like life-purchasing money?"

Chapter 1138: 1137: ’Meng Family’s Campaign to Eradicate the Ruthless Sect in Buddha Country Report

"Life-purchasing Money?" The Bodhisattva statues all came to life, surrounding the Merit Box with curiosity, not having heard of such a thing before.

"Perhaps it didn’t exist in Ancient Times, it was probably invented during the Great Qian or Great Yu Era," Lu Yang explained.

Lu Yang felt a connection forming between this bag of Spirit Stones and his own lifespan, the other party was trying to use the Spirit Stones to buy his lifespan away.

"Life-purchasing Money is a kind of life-extending Evil Method, normally practiced by placing a bag of money in a graveyard, or at a crossroads. Whomever picks up the money allows the other party to take away their lifespan. It’s essentially a forced transaction."

"Someone has the audacity to attempt buying Senior Brother Lu’s life!" the women said indignantly, finding this utterly lawless.

"Let’s see who did it."

"Establish a Rule: Find the owner of the Life-purchasing Money."

...

As night fell, crows cawed chaotically on dry branches, their eyes dull, shifting only occasionally.

This place was an abandoned graveyard outside of Jin’an City, where the unidentifiable dead were buried, the air heavy with Yin Energy and grudges, so much so that even daring thieves would not linger here for fear of provoking ominous entities.

Suddenly, a desiccated arm burst through the soil, wrist wound with beads, numbering exactly one hundred and eight, coinciding with the number of the Grand Circulation.

"Hehe, someone has picked up this old man’s money again."

The ground quivered and collapsed, revealing the owner of the dry arm, an old man so emaciated he was skin and bones, looking less like a human and more like a skeleton draped in flesh.

"Seeing this old man’s money, this life should belong to the old man now."

The old man opened his eyes to find himself being observed by a man and six women.

"Is it him, he’s so ugly," Jiang Lianyi said with disdain, overpowered by the stench of decay emanating from the old man’s body.

A flicker of confusion passed through the old man’s eyes, but he quickly identified the key figure, his gaze settling on Lu Yang, sensing it was Lu Yang who had taken his Life-purchasing Money.

"Is it you? It seems you’re somewhat knowledgeable to recognize this as Life-purchasing Money and even know to find help."

The old man’s laugh was hoarse: "It’s useless, this is a method of Causality, the only solution for the one caught in this method is to gift the money to someone else."

"Who are you?" asked Lu Yang, intrigued.

"Who am I?"

The old man stood up, his body quivering with mirth, finding the question ludicrous, his laughter suffused with an ancient desolation, as if mocking himself, as well as the myriad mortals of this world.

"Hehe, I’ve lived too long, I’ve already forgotten who I am."

Lu Yang fell silent for a moment, quietly rolling up his sleeves.

...

"My name is Zhuang Yumi, known as Daoist Yu, one hundred and ninety-eight years old, with a Cultivation Level in the Late Stage of Foundation Establishment, proficient in the Corpse-Driving Spell. I grew up without parents, raised by scavenging through trash, spending my days with mosquitoes, and by chance one day found a Cultivation method in a pile of rubbish..."

Daoist Yu knelt on the ground, beaten until his nose was swollen and his face bruised, the handprints particularly evident as he tearfully recounted his pitiable past.

"Only a little over a hundred years old and acting senile, pretending to forget your own name!"

Lu Yang looked around and realized that aside from himself, all six women were much older than Daoist Yu.

"Enough chit-chat, hand over the Corpse-Driving Spell," Lu Yang wasn’t in the mood to listen to his rambling.

"Okay, okay."

Daoist Yu was so frightened by Lu Yang’s methods that he was beside himself with fear. This was at least a Late Stage Golden Core Great Power! He had hardly seen a few at this stage in his lifetime; why did he have to encounter one?

He didn’t even dare to look at the six women surrounding Lu Yang for fear of having his eyes dug out; the women were so beautiful they looked like fairies from legends.

Lu Yang flipped through the Corpse-Driving Spell a couple of times and confirmed it was indeed from the Corpse Chasing Sect, but it was a fragment so damaged it couldn’t be any more incomplete—apparently, Daoist Yu had stumbled upon it by chance.

"We’ve met by fate, so hurry up and confess where you learned the Life-purchasing Money from, then choose a way to die," Lu Yang believed in leniency to those who confess.

Daoist Yu said in a heavy voice, "Senior, do you really need to exterminate me completely!"

He wasn’t inferior to anyone when it came to showing toughness and bravery!

"Although I can’t beat you, Senior, all six of your companions are here. Can you keep them safe?"

With a flick of his wrist, Daoist Yu activated his 108 beads, which glowed brightly. They were his Life-bound Magical Treasure, with each bead engraved with a different magic skill: Basic Fire Control Skill, Basic Water Control Skill... a total of 108 skills that combined in battle to be unpredictable and relentless.

"Hey, hey, hey, you can’t just say whatever you want," Lu Yang’s expression changed dramatically; he hurriedly slapped Daoist Yu to death to prevent the old man from uttering any more blasphemous words.

After performing a Soul Search, Lu Yang figured out how Daoist Yu had come by his Life-purchasing Money.

"It turns out that there is a secret Soul Organization in Buddha Country; its members all specialize in skills related to souls and corpses. The organization has a strict hierarchy divided into Heavenly, Earthly, Mysterious, and Yellow levels. Each level is considered a higher realm, with ten members at the Heavenly level known as Heaven One through Heaven Ten, all with exceptional abilities. This old man was a Yellow-level member and part of the periphery of the Soul Organization."

"He got his Life-purchasing Money directly from within the organization."

"Moreover, the Soul Organization has many hideouts, both real and fake too numerous to count. Even though this old man was a member, he didn’t know where the true hideouts of the organization were."

"Doesn’t that mean we can’t find the Soul Organization?" Yun Mengmeng hee-hawed as she gestured; she was eager to show off her skills in a great battle—the different levels of the organization and the mention of Heaven One through Heaven Ten made it sound formidable.

"Indeed, we can’t find them, but we don’t need to."

"Ah, why?"

"In the past few years, the Meng Family and the Western Paradise Temple jointly investigated Ruthless Sect members hiding in Buddha Country. Somehow, they found and destroyed the Soul Organization’s main base. This old man was of too low a rank for the Meng Family to have noticed him."

As he spoke, Lu Yang pulled out a small booklet from his Identity Jade.

The women gathered around and noticed the booklet was titled "Meng Family’s Action Report on Eradicating the Ruthless Sect in Buddha Country."

"After the action was over, the Meng Family held a commendation meeting, and I was invited. This is the report made by Old Meng’s father, Meng Potian, during the meeting."

Lu Yang browsed through the booklet, voicing his confusion: "Strange, I don’t remember any mention of the Soul Organization when I was reading this report. How come this old guy said the Soul Organization was finished by the Meng Family?"

Lu Yang reviewed the booklet again to make sure his memory hadn’t failed him.

"Is it here?" Qinghe pointed to an inconspicuous line in the report.

"In this action, not only did we capture the Ruthless Sect Elders, but we also banned more than a dozen illegal organizations such as ’Kanglong Sect’ and ’No Heaven Alliance’."

"But it doesn’t mention the Soul Organization, does it?" Yun Mengmeng asked, puzzled.

"Could the Soul Organization be among these ’more than a dozen’ organizations?"

Chapter 1139: 1138 [Rule: Remove Things That Affect My Cultivation]

Qinghe’s speculation wasn’t without reason. In ancient times, she was responsible for organizing the followers who worshipped Fairy Eternity and dealt with numerous big and small matters, such as proclaiming the greatness of Fairy Eternity to the world, sculpting statues, and leading the believers in daily prayers and so on.

When she reported her achievements to Fairy Eternity, she would always omit the trivialities, only discussing the important matters, to save the great one’s time, allowing them time to play.

"The work report from the Meng Family is poorly written. The remaining organization names should be noted in small print here," Lu Yang pointed out a flaw in the booklet. If it were him, such an oversight would never occur.

Ultimately, it was the Meng Family’s attitude that was at fault. Lu Yang served Immortals, and the Immortal in question was even the founding Emperor. As the saying goes, serving a king is like serving a tiger; he, of course, would be utterly respectful and never allowed such flaws in his daily duties or work.

"Indeed," Qinghe nodded. She did the same in ancient times. Although she wouldn’t report to the great one, she would write down her work experiences, so if the great one ever spontaneously desired to check on the work, there’d be something to review.

The only problem was that the great one had never shown such a whim.

"Alright, since this Soul Organization has been taken care of, everyone go back," Lu Yang said. To him, someone like Daoist Yu was a minor character whose only purpose was to disclose the organization behind him. As for his Storage Ring and such, they were all worthless trinkets he couldn’t be bothered to pick up if dropped on the ground.

Unknown to them how long they had been gone, a heavy downpour made the soil of the graveyard muddy and soft.

A youth desperately dug through the mud, emerging from the ground, gasping for breath and greedily inhaling the hard-won fresh air.

With a fierce look, the youth threw his arms wide and stood in the rain, laughing maniacally, "Second brother, to compete for the inheritance our father left behind, you even conspired with Wild Cultivators to trap and bury me alive!"

"Bet you didn’t expect that the only magic skill I mastered is the Turtle Breath Technique, and I’m still alive!"

After the laughter, the youth’s mood plummeted. Although alive, the thought of revenge seemed as unattainable as ascending to heaven. His second brother had a Cultivator as a backer, and though a Loose Cultivator, he was not someone his mere first level of Qi Refinement could provoke.

"What’s this?"

The youth noticed a decrepit corpse not far away, dressed strangely, with a string of beads tied to the wrist.

"Could this be... a magic artifact?"

The youth noticed the tiny characters engraved on the artifact, all magic skills amounting to a total of one hundred and eight.

He tried to channel spiritual essence into the beads, but sadly, he could only barely activate the Basic Fire Control Skill.

"Perhaps after my Realm improves, I’ll be able to activate more beads... and there’s also the Storage Ring!"

Both the beads and Storage Ring were Daoist Yu’s life-bound items. Now that their owner was gone, they could be opened at will.

Seeing the contents of the Storage Ring, the youth’s body shook uncontrollably.

"Cultivation Techniques, supreme techniques that enable cultivation to the peak of Foundation Establishment! And all these Spirit Stones, magic artifacts!"

He had never seen so many Spirit Stones and magic artifacts in his life.

The entire wealth of a Foundation Establishment Great Power felt like a stroke of tremendous luck falling from the sky.

"Exactly what formidable foe did this Foundation Establishment Great Power encounter to come to such an end?"

The youth quickly calmed himself, understanding that whoever had killed Daoist Yu was also gravely wounded and had not bothered to take away the Storage Ring and beads, leaving these treasures behind.

"May the Worldly Venerable bless me."

The youth knelt and bowed in all directions, overcome with gratitude.

...

"Where is this?" Uncle Wang found himself in a pure white space.

A tall and hazy figure appeared, immeasurably towering; Uncle Wang wasn’t even as tall as its finger.

"Well done, well done. Wang Han, you have shown integrity by not keeping the Life-purchasing Money for yourself, but instead donating it to our temple, reclaiming a life, thus creating good karma."

Uncle Wang shuddered. The money he had found was the legendary Life-purchasing Money. May the Worldly Venerable be praised, how lucky he was to have donated it.

"Continue to do good deeds in the future, and you shall reap good rewards."

"Yes!"

Uncle Wang woke up a bit confused, was everything just a dream?

But when he opened his palm and discovered the character for "good" written there, he immediately understood that it was a dream sent by the Buddha.

The Buddha worshipped at the Soybean Temple was a real Buddha!

Without even bothering to eat, Uncle Wang quickly mounted his camel and searched for the Soybean Temple, but when he arrived at the place from his memory, he couldn’t find anything, no matter how long he searched.

Just then, the voice from the dream echoed in his ears once more.

"Wang Han, your destiny with our temple has ended, seek no further."

Uncle Wang then gave up, utterly dispirited.

...

In the Great Bean Dynasty, at the Ancestral Temple.

Lu Yang had sent a dream to Uncle Wang simply to tell him to be more cautious and not pick up just any money.

After sorting out Uncle Wang’s issue, he sat on the Lotus Platform, expanded his Immortal Sense, and meditated on the Dao Charm of heaven and earth to deepen his understanding of the rules.

Within the range of his Immortal Sense, he saw Yun Mengmeng and Jin Caiwei setting up a large pot to cook syrup to repair the Ancestral Temple’s leaks, meanwhile conveniently eating other parts of the temple, effectively robbing Peter to pay Paul.

Qinghe was sneakily writing something on her own, and when she sensed Lu Yang’s Immortal Sense, she quickly covered up her writing and warned him with her eyes not to peek.

The Dragon and Phoenix ancestors had dug out a large space right below the Ancestral Temple, using the Qilin Immortal as an imaginary enemy to train for battle. After discussing, they decided that once they found the Qilin Immortal, they would give him a good thrashing!

"The Extinguish Immortal Sword Array that Elder Brother Lu Yang knows originated from the Sword Array that my husband often kneels to, he deceived us for so long, he deserves a beating!"

"But even if we join forces, I’m afraid we can’t hurt my husband, what should we do?" Jiang Lianyi asked, worried.

Ao Ling laughed charmingly, already having a plan in mind: "No worries, when the time comes, we’ll lure my husband to Buddha Country and ask Elder Brother Lu Yang to take action. The three of us will teach him a lesson together!"

"That’s a good idea, let’s wait until Elder Brother Lu Yang has fully grasped the rules and then practice our combined attack!"

In her Spiritual Space, Fairy Eternity was sitting cross-legged on the throne, swaying like a wobble doll, sharing her experiences with those who came after her: "Mastering rules relies on inspiration, it’s that ’click’ moment, you understand the feeling, right?"

"Don’t you understand? Let me repeat it again..."

Lu Yang gazed up hopelessly at the temple rooftop that had been nibbled on several times, who said Buddha Country was a good place? One might as well see a ghost than to calm the mind and cultivate here.

"Fairy, can I use the rules on myself?"

"You can, but you can’t use excessively powerful rules, like increasing your strength tenfold, or solidifying your realm, or anything like that."

"That’s fine, I don’t need exaggerated rules. Here I declare [Rule: Remove things that distract my cultivation]!"

Lu Yang didn’t want his Immortal Sense to see Yun Mengmeng and the others again, as it would distract him.

Setting the rule for himself, Lu Yang triggered an unnoticed change.

"Why can I still see Yun Mengmeng and the others?"

Lu Yang was puzzled, surely the rule wasn’t set right.

Suddenly, he was overcome with an inexplicable urge and earnestly said to Fairy Eternity, "Fairy, from now on, I am yours!"

"Ah? Really?"

"..."

Immediately, Lu Yang gave himself two slaps to wake up, what kind of ridiculous rule was this that stripped away his self-respect?

Chapter 1140: 1139: As a Cultivator, You Must Have Backbone!

"Really?" Fairy Eternity heard Lu Yang say this and excitedly jumped down from the throne, forcibly pulling Lu Yang to sit with her on the throne.

"Little Yang, rest assured, as long as you follow this Immortal, whatever you want, this Immortal will grant, and Xiao Ling, Little Lotus, and the like will all listen to you!"

"Do you want to ascend to the throne now?"
