"Is the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit still sufficient, and though you have one Immortal Body, you still don’t have your true form, do you? Shall this Immortal give you another?"

Lu Yang hurriedly used the Rule to restore his self-esteem.

No, no, no, as a Cultivator, you must have the backbone to stand tall between heaven and earth; how can you succumb to benefits? That would damage your Dao Heart!

He, of course, knew that following Fairy Eternity, becoming a Semi-Immortal would be effortless, and he might even become the strongest Semi-Immortal, surpassing Ao Ling.

But his goal was to become an Immortal; how could he be content with being a Semi-Immortal?

Abandoning his self-esteem only worked for the Fairy, not for the Eldest Senior Sister; she would still have him cultivate step by step.

How could Lu Yang resist Fairy Eternity? Seeing that he was about to be forcibly pulled to the throne, he quickly turned and hugged Fairy Eternity’s arm in desperation, voicing his feelings.

"Your Majesty, this subject is terrified, Your Majesty, I was just reminiscing about the grace you’ve shown me in my cultivation journey that led to these sentiments!"

"This subject started from insignificance, it was Your Majesty who did not disdain my weakness, condescended to reside in the Spiritual Space, posed challenges for me, provided me with opportunities; I, your subject, am already unable to repay you, and now Your Majesty wants me to inherit this great authority – how can I bear such a heavy responsibility!"

"Your Majesty, once again providing me with opportunities, how shall I repay Your Majesty in the future?"

This time Fairy Eternity understood; it was just that Little Yang was too self-deprecating.

"Little Yang, be confident; you are the pillar of the state that this Immortal favors..."

"Sinew of my arm," Lu Yang quickly interjected softly.

"Oh, sinew of my arm, this vast empire that this Immortal has conquered, will sooner or later be yours; what this Immortal gives you, you just accept."

"Absolutely not, Your Majesty. The three Great Generals are campaigning far and wide, the Historian, Minister of Rites and others work tirelessly and ask for nothing, and I have not yet established any merit; how can I receive more grace from Your Majesty!"

"Please grant me time, Your Majesty, to establish achievements and make a name. It is not too late for Your Majesty to bestow rewards upon me then."

"Oh, alright then." Fairy Eternity had thought she could finally present the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit to Little Yang, but she was still refused.

But on second thought, Little Yang was already her own, giving the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit was just a matter of time, right?

Thinking this, Fairy Eternity laughed again.

"Chief Housekeeper, Second-in-command, look what Senior Caiwei and I have drawn..."

"Hush, the Young Sect Master is cultivating, don’t wake him up." Jin Caiwei hurriedly signaled Yun Mengmeng to be quiet.

"Ah, oh." Yun Mengmeng slowed her steps, tiptoeing over to Lu Yang.

Lu Yang opened his eyes, helplessly looking at the two: "I’ve been awake all along."

Or rather, he had never been asleep; his current method of cultivation was to release his Immortal Sense, not to shut himself off completely.

"Look, what we’ve drawn."

Yun Mengmeng was holding a bunch of sugar paintings, the raw material being wall-repairing syrup, shaped into the likenesses of Ao Ling, Jiang Lianyi, and others.

"Chief Housekeeper, Second-in-command, here, these are for you, they just accidentally stuck together."

Yun Mengmeng picked out two sugar paintings, depicting Lu Yang and Fairy Eternity side by side.

"Sister Mengmeng has gone through a lot of effort."

Lu Yang took the sugar painting and licked it once, Fairy Eternity, who had possessed Lu Yang, also licked it.

"It tastes pretty good."

"Right? This is a syrup ratio I meticulously researched!"

Having earned the approval of two Spirit Chefs, Yun Mengmeng gained confidence and shared the remaining sugar painting with others.

While licking the sugar painting, Lu Yang pondered what kind of rules he should establish.

With the lesson just learned, he couldn’t just establish rules carelessly, as one misstep could lead to serious trouble.

"Let it be established here, ’Rule: Centered on me, construct a desert city within a ten-mile radius!’"

As soon as the rule was established, they could hear rumbling outside the temple, the sand and stones shook, rising from the ground to form desert walls. Inside the walls, buildings appeared, all condensed from sand grains but they were greatly distorted compared to real buildings, leaning and uneven in height.

Then, the desert city collapsed with a thunderous crash.

"Why did it collapse?"

"Your focus was too spread out, trying to construct everything well since it’s your first construction. You can’t cover all bases, and that’s what happened," Fairy Eternity said cheerfully. "But the method was right, continue practicing."

"Alright."

Lu Yang wasn’t greedy this time and gradually progressed, "Let it be established here, ’Rule: Centered on me, build a wall for a desert city within a ten-mile radius!’"

Just focusing on the wall and not the houses, Lu Yang felt less pressure, and soon a wall hundred meters tall appeared, surrounding the ancestral temple.

Not far from the wall, a caravan, seeing the desert city suddenly appear, was so frightened that they almost fell off their camels, having never seen such a grand spectacle.

"Whose temple lies ahead? Or has the Worldly Venerable manifested?"

But then, as the city collapsed back to normality, the travelers thought they had seen a remarkably rare mirage, yet something seemed off to them.

"May the Worldly Venerable not blame us, may the Worldly Venerable protect us!"

Whether it was a mirage or something else, they hurriedly ran away.

Sitting in the center of the ancestral temple, the successor to the Worldly Venerable, Lu Yang, shook his head and chuckled, stretching languidly. "Since they prayed for protection, I should be of some help."

He extended his index finger and pointed to a spot behind the caravan, instantly causing several venomous snakes to die unexpectedly.

These snakes had been following the caravan, waiting for a suitable opportunity to strike, but Lu Yang noticed them and poked them to death with one finger.

Days later, numerous caravans noted the appearance and disappearance of the desert city. Soon after, teams of Gold Core Period Cultivators emerged from the city to investigate the situation but found nothing.

This was Lu Yang utilizing the power of rules.

"How strange, who could this great person be, setting such a scene?"

A few Gold Core Period Cultivators gathered and discussed, unable to come to a conclusion. It was certainly not a simple mirage but rather the act of a Great Power.

However, the methods of the Great Power were beyond their understanding and they couldn’t discern exactly what it was.

"Let it be. Perhaps this great person does not wish to meet us ordinary beings," they discussed more and felt the person’s cultivation level was unfathomable, possibly even stronger than the elders of the Western Paradise Temple, such a powerful person transcending the mundane, beyond the comprehension of ordinary thoughts.

Since the Gold Core Cultivators could not locate the desert city and there were no casualties, the ancestral temple gradually became a legend in nearby cities, adding an air of mystery.

...

"Young Sect Master, look, I found something interesting when I went shopping in a nearby city." Jin Caiwei held a Recording Orb.

"Isn’t this just a Recording Orb?" Lu Yang wondered.

Jin Caiwei spoke mysteriously, "It is said that as soon as you play this Recording Orb, a female ghost will appear, and everyone who sees the ghost will encounter misfortune."

Chapter 1141: 1140: The Female Ghost in the Recording Orb

"Is that so?" Lu Yang took the Recording Orb, finding it somewhat interesting.

"I heard about these Recording Orbs at the black market and bought one." As a leader in the Recording Orb industry, the Qiongqi Clan was very interested in any changes to the Recording Orbs.

"Do all women ghosts who see the Recording Orb suffer misfortune?" Qinghe closed her book and came over.

"It sounds like a Curse Technique that can lower your luck for a period," Qinghe said, her knowledge of curse techniques was profound.

"The process of casting a curse technique is complex, the minimum requirement being to see an item in full view. It shouldn’t be possible to cast curse techniques using a Recording Orb, so it must be Wei creating a massive amount of these orbs with projection effects, turning the images three-dimensional, meeting the minimum requirements," Qinghe analyzed earnestly.

Previously, the Recording Orbs displayed flat images, but now they showed three-dimensional ones.

It was the first time Ao Ling and the others heard that Recording Orbs had such capabilities; they gathered around to watch, holding sugar paintings made by Yun Mengmeng and licking them occasionally.

Lu Yang placed the Recording Orb right in front, channeled Spiritual Energy into it, and the women unfolded like a peacock spreading its feathers, watching intently.

The orb projected a three-dimensional image, accompanied by the eerie sound of an erhu, and an old, dry well appeared before everyone, followed by a disheveled female ghost climbing out, soaking wet with water dripping from her hair and clothes.

As the erhu sound became more and more terrifying, more and more of the female ghost emerged.

Finally, with a clang, like a string of the erhu had snapped, the female ghost abruptly looked up, revealing a ghastly pale face!

Then the wind howled, candles flickered, brightening and dimming intermittently.

Everyone watched the female ghost intently.

The female ghost was puzzled; this reaction wasn’t right. Normally, by now, the people who saw her would have been scared into fleeing everywhere or attempting to turn off the Recording Orb but unable to, and just smashing the orb altogether.

"Awooo—awooo—" The female ghost bared her teeth and clawed at the air, trying to scare everyone.

"Is that it?" Yun Mengmeng was still expecting more, but discovered the female ghost had no further moves, just baring teeth and clawing, not frightening at all.

"That’s it? It’s not even as scary as Xiao Zhi laughing."

Having originally not found the female ghost scary, Lu Yang and Immortal Fairy shivered involuntarily upon hearing Yun Mengmeng’s mutterings.

Eldest Senior Sister only laughed under one circumstance—that was when she was infuriated, and indeed that could be frightening.

"Qinghe, are you sure this is a Curse Technique?" Lu Yang looked at Qinghe skeptically, as this didn’t seem like one at all. Even though he now had an Immortal Body, immune to curses, there should be an immunity process, but the whole time it was just the female ghost baring teeth and clawing.

Qinghe was somewhat embarrassed, admitting it didn’t seem like a curse technique: "Perhaps this female ghost can cast curse techniques, but seeing that we are not afraid, she chose not to cast?"

After showing her teeth and claws for a while and seeing no one cared, the ghost was preparing to sneak back into the Recording Orb when Lu Yang directly pulled her out.

"Explain yourself, what’s going on?"

The female ghost was properly hiding inside the Recording Orb. It was too strange.

The female ghost knelt on the ground with a plop, crying bitterly, "Please spare my life, I haven’t done anything bad, my master ordered me to hide in the Recording Orb!"

"Who is your master?"

"It’s Lord Shi! I don’t know my master’s true identity; he only lets me call him Lord Shi!"

"Where is he?"

"Jin’an City."

"Lead us there."

Initially hesitant, the female ghost saw Lu Yang narrowing his eyes and dared not utter a word of refusal; trembling, she returned to the Recording Orb, and using an arrow that appeared, she guided the way.

"Are you guys going to Jin’an City too?" Lu Yang asked, immediately feeling it was a silly question. Knowing these people, none of them were straightforward, all wearing expressions eager to watch the spectacle.

"With your disguises, you can’t go to Jin’an City." Lu Yang kindly reminded them; he blended in with the crowd, appearing completely ordinary after his disguise.

"The ladies were different though, they claimed they could disguise themselves, but even after disguising, they still possessed an otherworldly beauty. Anyone who saw them on the streets would definitely take a second look, maybe even attracting some reckless young masters and other such incidents."

"This is simple, just let Wei transform us into a plain surface," Ao Ling said, having already thought of a solution.

After speaking, Ao Ling transformed into her True Dragon form, and Jin Caiwei activated the Plane Dao Fruit Outline to transform the True Dragon Ao Ling into a flat surface, imprinting her onto Lu Yang’s robe.

Jiang Lianyi did the same.

Qinghe also transformed, becoming a lotus and imprinting herself upon Lu Yang’s robe.

In the blink of an eye, Lu Yang’s white robe was filled with patterns, dragons, phoenixes, Qiongqi, lotuses, and images of Mengmeng.

"Wait, why is Qinghe a lotus?" Lu Yang asked in astonishment.

"Hmm, haven’t I told you? Little Lotus’ original form is a lotus," Fairy Eternity said. She recounted tales of ancient times to Lu Yang every day, not sure if she had mentioned this before.

"Alright then."

Lu Yang changed his appearance and, wearing the patterned white robe and holding a Recording Orb, entered Jin’an City.

Jin’an City was a small city within the Buddha Country, quite inconspicuous. There weren’t even five Gold Core Period Cultivators there; otherwise, Daoist Yu wouldn’t dare to distribute Life-purchasing Money in Jin’an City.

Nevertheless, the city was bustling. As Lu Yang walked down the streets, he often heard people discussing the elusive desert city.

After becoming flat, the ladies weren’t well-behaved, moving around on the clothing. However, Lu Yang was unnoticeable, and consequently, his clothes went unnoticed as well, with no one spotting the changing patterns.

"It’s said that at the center of the desert city there’s a temple housing a Buddha, and one can make a wish if they manage to find the temple and enter the desert city!"

"Really? There’s such a thing?"

"But it’s just hearsay. See how many have searched for that desert city, who has found it?"

The ghost heard the discussions and broke out in a cold sweat, nearly dissipating her soul in fright. This temple sounded more and more like the place she had come from.

The arrow on the Recording Orb constantly changed direction, and Lu Yang made left and right turns, eventually reaching a dark alley.

"This is the place," the ghost’s voice came from the Recording Orb.

Pushing open the door, Lu Yang entered a small courtyard.

"Who dares trespass on my territory!"

A towering cultivator noticed Lu Yang’s arrival, instantly moved from the study to the courtyard, and demanded loudly.

"Sir Shi!" the ghost called out from the Recording Orb.

"Miao’er!" Sir Shi’s expression changed slightly. The other party was actually using Miao’er to restrain him.

"Who are you? To kidnap Miao’er, are you trying to provoke the Qiongqi Clan!" Sir Shi’s head transformed into a tiger’s, radiating the fluctuations of the Gold Core Period.

Lu Yang was momentarily stunned, his expression turning strange.

The winged white tiger on his white robe stirred, leaping from the fabric and transforming into a human form, eyeing Sir Shi coldly.

"Little, Little Ancestor!" Sir Shi’s heart stirred a massive upheaval. How could this be possible, why would the little ancestor appear here?

"Jin Cuoshi pays respects to Little Ancestor!" Sir Shi thudded onto his knees with a loud bang, repeatedly kowtowing.

"The ghost here says it was you who told her to hide in the Recording Orb, what do you mean by this!" Jin Caiwei had not expected that after all this rigmarole, it was their own clan member causing trouble.

Sir Shi hesitated, unable to respond.

"Speak!"

"It, it was my idea to boost our Recording Orb sales, so I arranged for Miao’er, Ke’er, An’er, and others to hide inside them to scare people, hoping they’d get frightened enough to break the orbs and buy new ones."

Chapter 1142: 1141: The Cursed Recording Orb

On Lu Yang’s white robe, various patterns moved to his chest, silently staring at Jin Caiwei.

Even without looking back, Jin Caiwei could feel many eyes focussing on her, making her want to find a crack in the ground to crawl into.

How embarrassing!

Upon learning that Lord Shi was from the Qiongqi Clan, Lu Yang suddenly remembered the Tiger Demon they had first encountered.

That Half-step Golden Core Stage Tiger Demon seemed to have a thin Qiongqi bloodline, adept at controlling ghouls—the idiom "Aiding a tiger in its cruelty" comes from this.

So to die for... it seemed that the Ghoul Skill was indeed the Qiongqi Clan’s signature ability.

Although he hadn’t seen Jin Caiwei use it.

Jin Caiwei indeed noticed that Miao’er was a ghoul but given that not only their Qiongqi Clan possessed the Ghoul Skill, she hadn’t thought about her own family in that regard. Not to mention, that guy named Man Gu also knew the Ghoul Skill.

"You you you you don’t talk nonsense, do we need to increase sales in this way?" Jin Caiwei was so angry she stamped her foot, her face reddening and paling by turns, wishing she could strangle this person surnamed Shi, ugh, surnamed Jin.

"Young Sect Master, you don’t know, our Recording Orbs are of such high quality, not only do they have large storage, they are also cheap and durable, lasting for ten or twenty years without breaking. Our sales of Recording Orbs in Jin’an City have already started to decline!"

"In fact, we wanted to make a Dream Mirage that looked very scary. Miao’er and the others’ actions in the Recording Orbs were the scenes I wanted to shoot, perfect for testing the audience’s reactions."

Lord Shi was on the verge of tears. As the person in charge of Jin’an City, he faced immense pressure and was trying various methods.

"But you can’t use this kind of method!" Jin Caiwei said through gritted teeth, wanting to pretend this incident had never happened, "You’ve turned it into a creepy legend!"

"What creepy legend?" Lord Shi was stunned.

"They’re spreading rumors in the black market, saying that a female ghost appears in the Recording Orbs, and anyone who sees the female ghost encounters misfortune!"

Jin Caiwei bought the Recording Orb based on this legend.

"Oh, you were talking about that legend. That wasn’t my doing," Lord Shi quickly disassociated himself.

"If not you, then who?"

"Really, it wasn’t me, young sect master. I’m also investigating this matter."

"Really not you?"

"I swear, it’s really not me!" Lord Shi vowed earnestly, he wouldn’t dare lie in front of the young sect master, not for all the courage in the world.

"Alright, get up. I presume you wouldn’t dare to lie." Jin Caiwei said, without much warmth, and headed straight to the hall, with Lu Yang following.

Lord Shi quickly brought out good tea to entertain the Young Sect Master and Lu Yang.

Behind the hall’s screen, several female ghosts poked out their heads, watching cautiously as Lord Shi entertained the guests. They had no idea what had happened, only seeing the all-powerful Lord Shi kneeling in front of Jin Caiwei just moments before.

"Why are all the ghouls you’re cultivating female?" Jin Caiwei raised an eyebrow.

"To answer the young sect master, female ghosts are more frightening than male ghosts."

"..." Jin Caiwei didn’t want to discuss this topic any further.

"You just said that the black market legend has nothing to do with you. What’s that about? Did I buy the wrong Recording Orb?"

Lord Shi wouldn’t dare say that the Young Sect Master made a wrong purchase, adding fuel to the fire—that would really be courting death.

He didn’t answer directly and instead took out a Recording Orb from his Storage Ring.

"This is the cursed Recording Orb that brings misfortune. I watched the contents of this orb once, and I had several days of bad luck!" Saying this, Lord Shi looked a bit scared. Even he, a Gold Core Period Cultivator, had been affected.

"Mine was light, those with lower cultivation than me suffered even worse luck after watching. I’ve even heard that these cursed orbs have appeared not only in Jin’an City but other cities too."

"Although Jin’an Temple and temples elsewhere are collecting and confiscating them on a large scale, they can’t fully clean them out. The worst part is that some people don’t believe in the curse and insist on watching."

"So such things are happening." Lu Yang frowned. This was no small matter.

Lu Yang took the Recording Orb, infused it with Spiritual Energy, and played the contents of the orb.

At first, the projection from the Recording Orb was blank, followed suddenly by a hideously faced female ghost crawling out from below. It was extremely terrifying.

Lu Yang felt a force trying to entwine him, his Immortal Body forged by the Power of Virtue automatically rebounded, purifying this force, which was also wrapped around Lord Shi. Lu Yang also purified it incidentally.

"Correct, this is the Curse Technique." On Lu Yang’s white robe, a green lotus appeared, transforming into Qinghe.

Though Lord Shi did not recognise Qinghe, he dared not ask more.

"Just as I initially suspected, projections can spread more content, thus deploying the Curse Technique to decrease luck," Qinghe stated, the one who understood the Curse Technique best among them all.

"If you don’t understand the Curse Technique, even those at the Golden Core Stage would be ensnared. And the female ghost is just a symbol—it doesn’t truly exist."

Lu Yang nodded. To ensnare someone at the Golden Core Stage meant that the level of the Curse Technique was quite advanced.

Although in the Seek Dao Sect, Gold Core Period Cultivators were common, and Lu Yang even fought a Half-step Golden Core Tiger Demon during his debut battle at the Foundation Establishment Stage, in reality, more than 99% of cultivators never reach the Golden Core Stage. A small city might not even boast five Golden Core Cultivators.

"It seems we’ll need to thoroughly investigate the source of the Curse Technique." Lu Yang said. The spread of the Curse Technique through Recording Orbs meant countless people could be affected.

"We can’t find it." Lord Shi poured out his troubles to Lu Yang, "I’ve reported this matter to the higher-ups long ago, but they also have no leads. They wanted to trace back from the cursed Recording Orbs, but these orbs have passed through so many hands, especially in the black market, that the source is long lost."

"Can’t find it? Use the power of rules to find the source?" Lu Yang mused to himself, then quickly shook his head, dismissing the idea.

The cursed Recording Orbs were not like Life-purchasing Money.

The power of rules was limited in scope. Daoist Yu was in Jin’an City, and the city was near the Ancestral Temple, within range. This time, it was clear that the entire Buddha Country had been affected, making the power of rules unfeasible.

"Young Sect Master, do you have a way?" Jin Caiwei asked anxiously. Using the Qiongqi Clan’s Recording Orbs to deploy the Curse Technique, she felt responsible. But the Qiongqi Clan had been investigating to no avail, and she had no idea where to start.

She could say she was completely clueless about where to begin.

"I have a way," Lu Yang answered with a nod.

"Going by what Lord Shi said, there’s a considerable number of cursed orbs circulating in the black market now."

"Correct."

Lu Yang smiled. "Then it’s easy to handle. Since the culprit has produced a large number of Recording Orbs, it means they must have purchased them in bulk, and to buy Recording Orbs, they could only do so from your Qiongqi Clan, through sales points in cities like what Lord Shi manages."

"So we just need to check who purchased a large quantity of Recording Orbs from the Qiongqi Clan around the time when the cursed orbs started appearing. Then we can lock onto the target."

Chapter 1143: 1142: Substantial Gain

"Eh, this is a good idea, why didn’t I think of that!" Jin Caiwei realized immediately that the method was feasible. She took out a token, contacted the Gold Clan Leader, and asked them to find the head of the Qiongqi Clan in Buddha Country.

Mr. Shi was so scared he swallowed his saliva and his body shuddered. What did the ancestor call this person just now, Young Sect Master?

Could this be the legendary Heavenly Court Young Master?

"That’s enough, there’s nothing here for you, you can go now," Jin Caiwei waved her hand, signaling Mr. Shi to leave.

"Yes, yes!"

In Jin’an City, Mr. Shi was among the top five powerful cultivators who could call the wind and summon the rain, but whether it was the ancestor or Young Master Lu, they were far beyond Mr. Shi’s comprehension. Being around them was a great pressure for Mr. Shi, so leaving was actually a good thing.

His attitude is still not right, no wonder he stopped at the Golden Core Early Stage, Lu Yang shook his head. When he was at the Foundation Establishment Stage, he used to live with Fairy Eternity every day and didn’t feel any pressure.

The Gold Clan Leader was very efficient and quickly obtained the purchase records of the Recording Orb before and after the time of the curse.

"There are a total of seven people with large purchase records during that period?" Lu Yang looked thoughtful upon receiving the information, seeming to have no other choice but to investigate them one by one.

"Let me see who’s the closest, it should be this one, Hongfa Temple, a young monk from Hongfa Temple purchased five hundred Recording Orbs."

"What is this young monk buying so many Recording Orbs for?"

"Let’s go and find out, come."

"Make sure to behave properly, and stop scaring people with a female ghost every day!" Jin Caiwei warned Mr. Shi before transforming into the pattern on Lu Yang’s white robe, making Mr. Shi repeatedly agree.

"But the idea of making very scary Dream Mirages is good. Keep on researching it." Lu Yang reminded him that if Mr. Shi really came up with something, just the Spirit Stones he earned could send him to the Nascent Soul Stage.

...

Hongfa Temple, secret chamber.

"Junior Brother Zhi Jie, it would be wise for you to voluntarily drop out of the Abbot election this time. If you let your Senior Brother take your place willingly, it would also show off your high monk image."

Junior Brother Zhi Jie was taken aback at first but then burst into laughter as if he had heard the funniest joke, "Me, drop out voluntarily? After knowing Senior Brother Zhi Kai for so long, I didn’t know you had a sense of humor."

"Although we are at the same realm, both at the Early Integration Stage, when has your Buddhist debate ever surpassed mine? And how many monks in Hongfa Temple support you! Let me tell you the truth, the position of Abbot will definitely be mine!"

"A joke? It seems the Junior Brother has a poor memory, do you need me to remind you? A month ago, you broke the precept of lust, physically bestowing charity to a good family’s young lady."

Junior Brother Zhi Jie looked at his Senior Brother in disbelief and exclaimed, "That’s impossible, how did you know that!"

"I not only know about it, but I also recorded it with a Recording Orb. Would you like to see it?"

Without waiting for Junior Brother Zhi Jie to respond, Senior Brother Zhi Kong took out a Recording Orb from his sleeve, and what played was the scene of Zhi Jie breaking the precept of lust a month ago, extremely erotic!

The evidence was irrefutable!

With this Recording Orb, Junior Brother Zhi Jie had no qualification to compete for the position of Abbot!

Snap—

In a fit of rage, Junior Brother Zhi Jie smashed the Recording Orb with a slap.

"You don’t think I only have this one Recording Orb, do you?" Senior Brother Zhi Kai chuckled and revealed a hundred Recording Orbs from his Storage Ring, all displaying that same scene.

Snap—

"If Junior Brother doesn’t find breaking one to be satisfying, you can destroy all of these Recording Orbs. You might want to guess how many Recording Orbs I still have," Senior Brother Zhi Kai said calmly.

Junior Brother Zhi Jie, like a bull enraged, had scary red eyes as he stared fixedly at Senior Brother Zhi Kai.

"Fine, I give up, but you must ensure that these Recording Orbs do not spread!"

"Of course."

Senior Brother Zhi Kai always maintained a confident smile, having predicted Junior Brother Zhi Jie would react this way. For this purpose, he had his young disciple make hundreds of copies of this Recording Orb, all to crush Junior Brother Zhi Jie’s psychological defenses.

"I guess you have four hundred Recording Orbs left." A third voice rang out in the secret chamber.

"Who’s there!" The brothers grew vigilant together; this was the secret chamber of Hongfa Temple—how could anyone have gotten in!

A young man in a white robe embroidered with dragons and phoenixes suddenly appeared in the chamber, looking helplessly at the two monks.

He had followed the little monk’s clues from Hongfa Temple all the way here.

"You two, come with me to Western Paradise Temple," said Lu Yang. Since it was a matter concerning Buddha Country, it was best to let Western Paradise Temple handle it.

Lu Yang’s gaze fell upon Senior Brother Zhi Kai. From the angle of the shots in the Recording Orb, it was clear that Junior Brother Zhi Jie’s breaking of the precepts was premeditated. Such a person becoming Abbot would certainly be unacceptable.

"Such big talk!"

The brothers panicked at the same time; they absolutely couldn’t let Western Paradise Temple find out about this!

They fully unleashed their Cultivation Level, shaking the secret chamber to its core.

"Canghuang Sky Fist!"

"Demon-suppressing Eighteen Forms!"

"Two at the Early Integration Stage, huh..." Lu Yang’s tone remained even, "Let me set a ’Rule: In this secret chamber, use of spiritual essence is forbidden.’"

Without the support of spiritual essence, the brothers’ moves collapsed midway, and the chamber returned to calm.

The brothers looked frantic: "This kind of rule suppression... You can’t be at the Integration Stage, who, who are you!"

...

"Deputy Saint, this batch is the collective purchase of Recording Orbs, still on the usual terms. The receipts issued by the Qiongqi Clan show the full price, our actual purchase cost is at a 20% discount, and the remaining profit is ours."

"No one has discovered this, have they?"

"Absolutely no one!"

"Good, I trust your work." The Deputy Saint nodded his approval.

"You two, come with me to Western Paradise Temple as well." The young man in the white robe with the dragon and phoenix pattern appeared out of nowhere.

"Who are you!" The Deputy Saint was shocked and immediately unleashed his Great Divine Skill at Lu Yang.

"Mid Intergration Stage, not bad."

...

"Boss, this is the profit from our smuggling this time..."

"You two, come with me to Western Paradise Temple."

"You dare to threaten me, courting death!"

"Integration Late Stage, also decent."

...

In a damp cave, a disheveled cultivator clutched a Recording Orb and laughed maniacally.

"That’s it, just like that, my Curse Technique has reached a new level. Now who in the world can be my opponent!"

"Finally found the right person." The white-robed young man appeared, looking like a Banished Immortal, completely out of place in the damp and filthy cave environment.

"Who are you!" The disheveled cultivator barked fiercely, shaking the bell in his hand as an eerie ring filled the space, sending shivers down one’s spine.

Lu Yang could see the cultivator’s Cultivation Level at a glance: "Initial Stage of Spirit Transformation, huh?"

He sighed, counting on his fingers, "Do you know how many wrong people I’ve found while trying to catch you? On this journey, I’ve captured one at the Void Refinement Stage, three at Early Integration Stage, two at Mid Intergration Stage, and one at Integration Late Stage."

Chapter 1144: 1143: Another Use of Curse Technique (Monthly Ticket Lottery)

Lu Yang didn’t have many acquaintances at Western Paradise Temple, and Time Immortal was one of them. However, this minor issue didn’t necessitate bothering Time Immortal, so Lu Yang contacted Abbot Ming Yu directly. Through Time Immortal, Abbot Ming Yu had long been aware of Lu Yang’s identity.

Originally, when Lu Yang had started sending people to Western Paradise Temple, Abbot Ming Yu had been quite thankful, for Lu Yang had helped Buddha Country to eliminate hidden dangers.

However, as Lu Yang sent more and more people, Abbot Ming Yu’s gaze towards him started becoming strange, as if asking why he kept sending people this way, and whether he had any grievances against Buddha Country.

Although Lu Yang had once tried hard to explain that the issue lay in the management level of Buddha Country and wasn’t related to him, obviously, he couldn’t convince Abbot Ming Yu.

Fortunately, when Lu Yang found the seventh clue, he finally located the main party involved.

Lu Yang was terrified that the seventh clue would directly point to Western Paradise Temple; that would truly be hard to explain.

"I’m telling you, the public security in Buddha Country is just not up to par," Immortal Fairy defended Lu Yang and tried to persuade everyone.

"Since coming to Buddha Country just to investigate a curse technique, we have uncovered so many people involved. Look at our time in Great Xia, when did we ever encounter such situations?"

"Right, right!" The various patterns on the white robes swam about, echoing seriously in agreement with Immortal Fairy’s point of view.

...

"Bone Layman, is it? Are you going to confess the events obediently, or shall I have to beat you up first before you do?"

"You have captured six Combined Great Abilities? Who are you trying to fool!" Initially, Bone Layman was stunned and then became furious.

How was that possible? A Combined Great Ability was an incredibly rare being, one he could scarcely dream of meeting; aspiring to connect with their kind was almost impossible, let alone capture them, and more so, six at once!

The sound of Bone Layman’s ringing bell grew louder and louder, almost deafening. Even though he was at the Initial Stage of Spirit Transformation, he mastered a highly rare curse technique, which even those in the Late Stage of Spirit Transformation didn’t want to confront!

Now, he was spreading the curse technique through a Recording Orb, observing the reactions post-curse, gaining new insights, and taking his curse technique to a higher level, becoming invincible among those in the Divinity Transformation Stage!

However, Lu Yang was not at all afraid of the curse technique; he could use "Heavenly Reversal" to change causality and avoid the curse technique, even if he weren’t an Immortal.

"You will be plagued by bad fortune!" Bone Layman shouted as he frantically shook the bell.

The moment the power released from the bell touched Lu Yang, it cracked in half with a snap!

"How is this possible!" Bone Layman was startled, continuously spitting blood, as his life-bound bell had backfired!

This showed that the other party’s fate was extraordinarily noble, utterly beyond his reach!

Before Lu Yang even made a move, the Qiongqi totem on his white robe drifted to his chest, eyes glaring. Bone Layman’s body stiffened, hair stood on end, as if targeted by an Ancient Demon, unable to move.

Bone Layman was terrified to the core, daring not to move at all, screaming internally, what kind of technique was this, utterly unheard of, who exactly was the other party, and why were they after him?!

Lu Yang completely ignored Bone Layman’s existence, letting him stand as punishment. He strolled around the cave, observing Bone Layman’s living environment; the cave was messy, indicating Bone Layman did not care much for his living conditions.

Continuing, Lu Yang picked up a book from the table and flipped through two pages: "It seems this is your insight into the curse technique."

"This certainly is about spreading the curse technique through a three-dimensional Recording Orb," Qinghe examined the book with Spiritual Sense, easily understanding the principle and frowned in displeasure.

"If this method falls into the wrong hands, I fear it could be spread widely and cause great harm."

Jin Caiwei glared fiercely at the book, the Qiongqi Clan had finally made some money through the Recording Orb, and now someone was using it for evil, causing so much harm, truly despicable.

If not for the sake of her image, she would have transformed back to her original shape and torn the book to shreds with a growling howl.

"There, there, Senior Caiwei, this book is actually a great treasure." Lu Yang said, soothing Jin Caiwei, feeling the fierce energy emanating from her.

"Huh? A great treasure? What do you mean?" Jin Caiwei was confused by Lu Yang’s comment.

"I’m thinking of using the Recording Orb to spread the Curse Technique. That seems like a good idea."

"What?" Qinghe thought she had heard wrong.

"Senior Qinghe, the Curse Technique has effects like making people sick, right?"

"Yes, what about it?"

Lu Yang, stroking his chin, asked a question that Qinghe had never considered: "It is said that Magic Spells are neutral and don’t have an inherent good or evil, so can I use the Curse Technique in reverse to bless someone with a long life?"

Qinghe looked perplexed, as Curse Techniques had always been used to bring misfortune to others.

But the issue was that Lu Yang was not just daydreaming. From the principles of the Curse Technique, it seemed possible to bring good fortune to others as well.

"It seems... possible?"

Encouraged by Qinghe’s affirmation, Lu Yang’s eyes shone with excitement, "Then could I use the Recording Orb to spread the Curse Technique and bless everyone with health, longevity, and long life?"

Qinghe hissed, her expression grave.

"It seems... possible too."

"Master, I’ve captured the person." Lu Yang brought the person to the Western Paradise Temple.

Master Ming Yu’s eyes twitched; he didn’t even want to count how many people Lu Yang had captured, and asked tentatively, "That’s all, right?"

"That’s all!"

"That’s good then." Master Ming Yu breathed a sigh of relief. Every time Lu Yang came, he would capture someone or a pair of people, as if Lu Yang knew exactly where the problems were and headed straight for the targets.

"Good indeed, spreading the Curse Technique, Benefactor Lu can rest assured, this person will be handled by our Western Paradise Temple."

"You say our brothers were having a good talk in the secret room, how were we discovered!"

And they still didn’t know who had captured them.

"Who knows, I’ve issued so many receipts in secret and never got discovered, but this time we were!"

"When I was captured, I heard the person talking to himself, saying that he was originally looking for the cursed Recording Orbs and then traced them to us from the records of bulk purchases."

"So, this is a calamity without cause!"

"Alas, tough luck, what can we do but accept our fate."

With a creak, the cell door opened, and Bone Layman was pushed in.

"Oh, got a new one here. Your Cultivation Level seems pretty low. What did you do?" The prisoners, feeling that their own luck was bad enough, looked for others in similarly unfortunate situations to feel a bit better.

Bone Layman grinned sheepishly, "Hello, brothers. I was idly trying to combine the Curse Technique with the Recording Orbs, and that got me caught."

Upon his words, the cell fell dead silent.

"How, how so?" Bone Layman grinned sheepishly, feeling that the way these people looked at him changed after he explained.

A group of Combined Great Ability Cultivators looked hostile and slowly forced Bone Layman into a corner.

Chapter 1145: 1144 Zhuyou Technique

"The basic principle of Curse Technique is by using Karma Magic to influence the opponent’s fate, Essence, Qi, and Spirit, causing their mind to become disoriented and disrupting the balance of Yin Yang and the Five Elements in their body."

"The concept of causality has probably been explained to you, sir, that both observable and perceptible things can establish causal connections, and the Recording Orb satisfies the ’observable’ causal connection."

Inside the Ancestral Temple, Qinghe detailed the fundamental knowledge of Curse Technique to Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng. Ao Ling and others were familiar with Curse Technique; even when Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi were opponents, they had used each other’s hair as a medium to cast Curse Techniques.

"According to Lu Zhuguo’s idea, if you think about it carefully, Curse Technique can affect fate and Essence, Qi, and Spirit through Karma Magic, so it can be positive as well as negative."

"If it positively increases fate and Essence, Qi, and Spirit, then it could strengthen the body and eliminate illness."

Immortal Fairy wasn’t planning to attend the lesson, just lying half asleep in the Spiritual Space, but she suddenly popped out of the Spiritual Space when she heard this.

"Strengthening the body and eliminating illness?"

"Fairy, is there a problem with that?" Lu Yang noticed something off about Immortal Fairy.

Immortal Fairy tilted her head while trying hard to remember something: "Listening to Little Lotus explaining this, isn’t this just the long-lost Zhuyou Technique?"

"What is Zhuyou Technique?" Everyone looked at Immortal Fairy at once; even Qinghe and Ao Ling had never heard of this magic skill.

"Huh, you don’t know? It was mentioned by Immortal Jiuchong when he was researching history."

"Isn’t Immortal Jiuchong the first to become Immortal? To prove he was the first, he often studied ancient texts and legends to demonstrate that there were no beings surpassing the Transcendance Tribulation Stage earlier."

"He said that a long, long time ago, no one knows how far back, even before the cultivation system was perfected, there was a way of healing. It didn’t focus on specific treatments for illnesses but used an unknown magic skill to heal. It could heal not only the body but also treat the spirit."

"This healing method was called Zhuyou Technique, and only cultivators known as ’Witch Doctors’ knew this technique."

"As for who created Zhuyou Technique, what its principle was, and how it was lost, nobody knows; even Immortal Jiuchong couldn’t find any more information after searching through ancient texts."

"Don’t you think that if you just reverse Curse Technique, it becomes Zhuyou Technique?"

"It does sound similar; we’ll know if we try it." Lu Yang said, as he trusted the Fairy’s intuition.

Jin’an City slums.

Zhong Cai was lying in bed, feeling feverish all over. He woke up feeling terrible today, and just as he was about to get up for some water, his expression became dazed, he wobbled after standing up and, without regaining his balance, sat back down, unable to exert any strength.

"Is it because I worked too hard yesterday?" Zhong Cai sweat on his forehead, feeling uncomfortable, with a bad premonition that he might be suffering from a wind-heat disease.

Zhong Cai’s vision blurred, and he fell back into a deep sleep.

In his haze, he seemed to see a white-robed figure appear at the bedside, face obscured by soft luminescence, indiscernible.

Was it the legendary Buddha Bodhisattva?

A shimmer flickered on the white-robed figure’s Lotus Totem, and Zhong Cai lost consciousness.

"Yawn—what a comfortable sleep." Zhong Cai stretched and yawned, not knowing how long he had slept but feeling incredibly refreshed and clearer in mind than ever before.

...

"It really works." Lu Yang used his clothes to bring everyone back to the Ancestral Temple.

Not just Zhong Cai, but he had treated over ten individuals in Jin’an City, with various ailments like wind-heat, heatstroke, and summer heat stroke, curing all without any medication, all through Qinghe’s Curse Technique.

"So, can we say that Curse Technique and the lost Zhuyou Technique are essentially the same thing?"

Lu Yang frowned, feeling there was something wrong: "Fairy, do you know when Curse Technique appeared?"

Although Karma Magic is difficult to master, if Curse Technique and Zhuyou Technique are the same thing, why did Curse Technique survive while Zhuyou Technique was lost?

Logically, if Curse Technique was lost, Zhuyou Technique should not have been lost either.

Immortal Fairy shook her head: "I don’t know."

Ao Ling then said: "Many magic skills were created at the time of the cultivation system’s establishment. During that period, the pioneers of the Human race tried various methods of cultivation relentlessly, and magic skills were a byproduct of this process."

"Curse Technique most likely appeared during the time when the cultivation system was being established, but that era is too far gone, at least three hundred thousand years ago, and few ancient texts have survived; more about that era was handed down orally, and much information cannot be verified."

"I agree with Sister Ling’s view," Qinghe said. "When I was researching Curse Technique, I tried to trace its origins. Although I didn’t find its true origin, I’m sure that Curse Technique has been around for at least two hundred thousand years."

"Okay." Lu Yang could understand; after all, the cultivation system was created by the Human race, and at that time, even surviving was difficult, so how much could be transmitted on paper?

Now looking back at Ancient Times, Ancient Times is a legend, and the establishment period of the cultivation system viewed from Ancient Times is also a kind of legend.

"Speaking of Immortal Jiuchong, I have a question," Lu Yang suddenly asked.

"What is it?"

"Since the concept of Dao Fruit was created by you, Fairy, and defining the realm after Transcendance Tribulation Stage as ’Immortal’ was also your idea."

"Yes, any problem with that?"

"Then how did the concept of Immortal body come about?"

Without waiting for Immortal Fairy to respond, Lu Yang, stroking his chin, talked to himself: "Of course, initially, the Human race just referred to a special physique as Immortal body, a hyperbolic description, and then grouped it as the nine great Immortal bodies."

"Do you think there’s another possibility, that a long time ago, the transmitted realms weren’t complete, and originally there was the ’Immortal’ realm above the Transcendance Tribulation period, which gave rise to the term ’Immortal body’?"

...

"Little Ancestor, you don’t know, but our Recording Orbs are of such high quality, not only do they have large storage, they are cheap, and durable too; they last ten or twenty years without breaking. Our sales of Recording Orbs in Jin’an City have started to decline!"

"Actually, we were thinking about making a really scary Dream Mirage, with Miao’er and her group’s movements in the Recording Orb just the clips I wanted to shoot, which could be a good test of the audience’s reaction."

Mr. Shi, teary-eyed, as he was Jin’an City’s head, faced immense pressure, trying various methods.

"But you can’t use this method!" Jin Caiwei gritted her teeth, wishing she could pretend this incident never happened. "This has turned into a creepy legend!"

"I definitely won’t dare next time!" Mr. Shi promised earnestly, never expecting the issue to blow up this big.

Chapter 1146: 1145: Forgotten

Jin Caiwei had not expected that after so much hassle, it was all due to her own clan’s foolish actions.

How embarrassing it was.

"Behave yourself, if I hear any more tales about cursed Recording Orbs bringing misfortune in the black market, you’ll have me to answer to!" Jin Caiwei glared fiercely at Stone Master.

The Qiongqi Clan had never had a good reputation, having been reduced to one of the Four Evils, notoriously named Qiong Xiong. Now that the Recording Orb business was just taking off in the Cultivation World, if word about Stone Master’s actions got out, outsiders could only guess what malicious speculations would arise about the Qiongqi Clan.

"Forget it, forget it, Senior Caiwei, please calm down."

Lu Yang quickly stepped forward to soothe Jin Caiwei, seeing that Little Tiger was genuinely infuriated, possibly enough to strike Stone Master at any moment.

Stone Master, merely at the Golden Core Early Stage, certainly wouldn’t withstand a blow from Little Tiger.

"Look on the bright side, at least no one is using the Recording Orbs to cast Curse Techniques, right?"

The idea of the Recording Orb was Lu Yang’s creation, and regardless of his identity as the Young Sect Master and a Pillar State Minister, Lu Yang held favor with the entire Qiongqi Clan, and his words were highly effective on Jin Caiwei.

"Please, calm yourself, I assure you it will not happen again!" Stone Master gave a forced smile, gratefully looking at Lu Yang.

"Alright, alright, let’s go." Jin Caiwei huffed angrily as she left Stone Master’s residence, transforming back into the Qiongqi totem.

The women struggled to hold back their laughter, not daring to laugh for fear of provoking Little Tiger to scratch them.

...

Western Paradise Temple, Mahavira Hall.

The sleeping Buddha opened his eyes, showing a strange expression.

"Has something been changed?"

He stretched out his golden Buddha hand, and with a grasp in the void, a Dao Fruit, resembling condensed golden river water, appeared in his hand.

He activated the Time Dao Fruit and stepped alone into the current of time, with infinite time swirling around him, like a moment yet also like an eternity.

"Strange, time has not retrospected, is it my misconception?"

Time Immortal paced back and forth in the Mahavira Hall, somewhat confused, though his intuition was not as accurate as Immortal Fairy’s, it rarely misled him.

"Immortal Fairy should be in the Seek Dao Sect, and Daoist Yun Zhi too. I’ll ask them."

This was his first time leaving the Mahavira Hall in three hundred thousand years.

...

Soybean Dynasty Ancestor Temple.

"Senior Caiwei, what do you think about filling the empty spaces in the temple walls with biscuits and then coating them with syrup?" Yun Mengmeng cheerfully squatted next to Jin Caiwei in a corner.

The previously sullen Jin Caiwei, having heard Yun Mengmeng’s suggestion, redirected her attention and started to think seriously.

"That is indeed a good idea, using syrup alone to fill the holes is a bit too sweet."

"Let’s go, let’s research what kind of biscuits would be suitable."

"Fairy, are you pondering some issue?" Lu Yang noticed that Immortal Fairy had been frequently zoning out ever since she returned from Jin’an City.

For instance, now Immortal Fairy sat on the bed with legs crossed, hands clasped, and brow furrowed, appearing to be deep in thought.

"Little Yang, why does this Immortal have such a poor memory at such a young age? I always feel like I’m forgetting something."

"What have you forgotten?"

"Little Yang is really silly. If this Immortal knew what he had forgotten, how could it still be called forgetting?"

"Actually, I also always feel like I forgot something," Lu Yang said, somewhat embarrassed.

"Do both the Fairy and the Junior Brother also feel like they’ve forgotten something?" A chill voice arose beside Lu Yang and the Immortal Fairy, startling Lu Yang into a shudder.

"Eldest Senior Sister!"

Lu Yang thought it would have been nice if Eldest Senior Sister had given a heads-up before arriving; it was quite frightening.

"Wait, too?"

Lu Yang noticed the word usage of Eldest Senior Sister.

"So you mean to say Eldest Senior Sister, you also forgot something?"

"My intuition is seldom wrong," Time Immortal appeared in the Spiritual Space. After he had arrived at the Seek Dao Sect, he had only found Yun Zhi and hadn’t located the Immortal Fairy.

Yun Zhi also sensed something unusual, so she and Time Immortal returned to Buddha Country.

"Did something happen?"

Lu Yang dropped his playful attitude and his expression turned serious. All four of them sensed something unusual—it likely couldn’t just be described as a mere illusion.

"Please, take a seat first." Immortal Fairy, being very polite, knew guests shouldn’t stand at home; thus, she conjured two beds for Miss Yun and Time Immortal to sit on. Then, she patted her own bed, gesturing for Lu Yang to sit beside her.

Time Immortal spoke first, "Just now, while I was taking a nap in the Mahavira Hall, a thought suddenly emerged, unprovoked. I always felt like time underwent some unknown changes."

"Was it a time retrospection?"

Time Immortal shook his head, rejecting Lu Yang’s idea, "No, I pushed the Time Dao Fruit with full power to check. The rivers of time flowed onward, with no signs of regression, meaning no time retrospection occurred."

"Besides, only the Time Dao Fruit is capable of time retrospection, not even a Dao Fruit Outline can do it. If it really happened, it could only be done by me."

"Originally, I thought it was an illusion, but I felt something was off, so I visited Daoist Yun Zhi. She mentioned that she seemed to have lost a patch of memory, but upon careful reflection, she found her memories were coherent and without any issues."

Yun Zhi continued, "I asked the Yingtian Immortal, Jiang Pingan, and Meng Junzi; none of the three noticed anything unusual."

"So, does this mean only the three senior folks and I sensed something is wrong?" Lu Yang was astounded. Neither the Yingtian Immortal nor other Immortals had noticed, making the situation more serious than he had imagined.

Though he was an Immortal, he was still somewhat weaker compared to a true Immortal. Probably his ability to sense something strange was due to living in the Spiritual Space with the Immortal Fairy.

"Did the hidden manipulator make a move?" Lu Yang frowned. Now, there was only one explanation.

"This is the first time the hidden manipulator has truly made a move." Although the hidden manipulator had encountered the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord before, it did not seem like the full extent of the hidden manipulator’s capabilities.

What exactly happened to make the hidden manipulator act?

...

Under the pitch-black night, two Tribulation Cultivators concealed their aura and quietly approached a damp cave.

"Big brother, would we not better call it off?" one of the Tribulation Cultivators whispered, always feeling uneasy.

"What’s there to fear? I’ve made inquiries. This Bone Layman is only at the Initial Stage of Spirit Transformation, without any significant achievements, constantly hiding and not knowing what he’s messing with. With the prepared taking an unprepared victim, wouldn’t it be easy for us two in the Divinity Transformation Stage to deal with him?"

The timid Tribulation Cultivator clenched his teeth—it was decided, he’d listen to his big brother!

Both approached the damp cave, took out their Life-bound Magical Treasures, one being a bell and the other a cauldron. They crazily poured spiritual essence into these objects, instantly activating them.

They both rushed into the cave, their expressions ferocious, as they shouted threateningly, "Bone Layman, meet your demise!"

"There’s no one here?"

The two Tribulation Cultivators looked at each other. The cave was spotlessly clean, not at all like anyone had ever lived there.

Chapter 1147: 1146 One of the Behind-the-scenes Manipulator’s Methods

"Where is Bone Layman?" The Life-bound Magical Treasures of the two Tribulation Cultivators activated, but they lost their target.

They had been worried about being detected by Bone Layman and didn’t dare to use their Spiritual Sense to probe, and now, after all the trouble, they didn’t know where the person had gone.

"Big brother, is your information accurate?"

"Of course it’s accurate, Bone Layman was definitely here before!" Big brother asserted confidently, his source of information was absolutely reliable, "It seems he has moved locations!"

"If he’s not here, then he’s not here. Let’s go, big brother." The little brother had a chilling feeling, regardless of whether Bone Layman had been here before, he found the place to be eerily strange.

Although they were Divine Transformation Stage Cultivators, they were far from invincible, and there were many unknowns in the world; the more you know, the faster you died.

Big brother, skeptical of superstitions, explored the cave inside and out, and couldn’t find a single trace; he couldn’t help but curse, "Damn it, cleaned up so thoroughly, nothing left behind, let’s just go."

On the way back, the little brother saw that big brother was in low spirits and kindly tried to cheer him up, "Big brother, let’s think positively. It’s lucky that Bone Layman wasn’t here. If he had been, with his curse techniques and other wicked methods, we would have fallen right there."

Even though they were both Divine Transformation Stage Cultivators and numerically had the advantage, it didn’t necessarily decide the outcome.

For example, people like Lu Yang, who had extraordinary talents and could fight above their level, or those Cultivators who mastered unusual magic skills like time or causality.

Big brother waved his hand impatiently, "Get lost, you’re just trying to comfort me. Curse techniques, those aren’t so easy to come by."

...

Spiritual Space.

Lu Yang pondered for a moment, then looked up and asked, "Time Immortal, who is stronger between you and Senior Ying Tian?"

The Time Immortal was taken aback, then quickly understood Lu Yang’s meaning, and said with an odd expression, "Although I always say I’m stronger than the three of them, when it really comes down to understanding the Dao Fruit, Ying Tian and I aren’t much different."

"Are you saying Immortal Fairy and Daoist Yun Zhi sensed something because they are strong enough, and I sensed something because this matter is related to ’time’?"

Lu Yang nodded, "That’s what I mean."

To understand the root of this matter, the first thing was to find the differences between Eldest Senior Sister, the Fairy, and the Time Immortal.

And the biggest difference was that the Time Immortal wasn’t strong enough.

Since the Time Immortal wasn’t strong enough but still sensed the change, while Ying Tian Immortal and others didn’t, it suggested that the problem lay with "time."

The Time Immortal was about to nod, then suddenly realized something was off, "Wait, didn’t you also sense something vaguely?"

Although you’re an Immortal Body, you’re not stronger than me.

Lu Yang frankly admitted, "I have the blessing of Immortal Fairy."

The Time Immortal: "..."

Lu Yang then remembered another thing, "And Time Immortal, you were ambushed at the end of the Fire Dynasty, and you suspect that the unseen hand’s ability is related to time, right?"

"Yes, that’s what I think."

The Time Immortal continued, "I’ve checked with Time Dao Fruit, and there was no occurrence of time reversal, which means it wasn’t time reversal."

The Time Immortal had great confidence in his Dao Fruit; no matter how powerful the unseen hand was, it couldn’t have tampered with it and still deceived him.

Having said that, the Time Immortal stood up, hands behind his back, and circled the three, his thoughts somewhat stuck.

Related to time, yet not time reversal, yet feeling like something was forgotten, what exactly was the method used by the unseen hand?

"Maybe what Zuo Shi Immortal saw in the past is also related to the method used by the unseen hand now," Lu Yang murmured to himself.

Hearing Lu Yang’s murmuring, the Time Immortal suddenly stopped walking, his eyes lighting up, "Wait, could it be..."

"What is it?"

"Time line! The unseen hand can manipulate the time line!"

"Time line?" Yun Zhi repeated, a term she had never heard before.

Lu Yang had actually meant to guess the time line, but he wasn’t sure if the Time Immortal would describe the phenomenon as "time line," hence he hinted through murmuring.

The Time Immortal squatted on the ground and used a branch to draw a straight line with no beginning or end.

"This line represents time."

He could have demonstrated with magic skills, but his true form being the Firmament Tree, he preferred to use branches to draw and explain principles, it was in his nature.

He then selected a point on the line, "This is the present."

Using this point as the starting point, he drew a semicircle back towards the past, "This is called Time Retrospection."

"The other end of the semicircle where it meets the time line is the ’past’."

"Tang Chuanwu was just in this situation; after he went back to the past and did something, the future would change."

The Time Immortal erased the line from "past" to "future" and drew a new line from the "past," slightly deviating from the original future.

"After changing the future, the original future disappears; this is the fundamental principle of Time Retrospection."

The Time Immortal then pointed at the time point representing the "past," with an unprecedentedly solemn tone, "I suspect the unseen hand’s method is to change the past directly without going through Time Retrospection, causing the future to change!"

"That’s why Immortal Fairy and Daoist Yun Zhi experienced the phenomenon of feeling like they forgot something!"

"Lu’s words reminded me, do you still remember the past that Zuo Shi Immortal saw? Our four personalities changed significantly, and Immortal Fairy alone deduced the path to becoming immortal through Dao Fruit."

"Maybe this is a false world line created by the unseen hand, deliberately shown to Zuo Shi Immortal!"

"Yes, it all makes sense this way!"

The Time Immortal grew more excited as he spoke, and after four hundred thousand years, he was finally able to confirm one of the unseen hand’s methods, or at least one of them.

"No, I can’t conclude so recklessly, maybe there’s something I haven’t guessed yet."

The Time Immortal quickly calmed down again; the assassination three hundred thousand years ago made him cautious, and the unseen hand still had many mysteries unexplained, making rash conclusions could lead them further astray on the wrong path.

Still lacking proof of the method.

And the abilities of Time Dao Fruit couldn’t verify it.

Seeing the atmosphere turn somewhat awkward, with the Time Immortal remaining silent, Eldest Senior Sister and Immortal Fairy also not speaking, pondering methods, Lu Yang cautiously raised his hand to make his presence felt.

"Maybe I have a way."

"What way do you have?" The Time Immortal was very surprised; he couldn’t think of a way to prove it, and you have one?

Eldest Senior Sister and Immortal Fairy also looked towards Lu Yang, wanting to know his method.

Being watched by them, especially with Eldest Senior Sister and Immortal Fairy’s eyes seemingly revealing unreasonable trust and expectation, put a lot of pressure on Lu Yang.

"Maybe, I just mean maybe, I’ve never tried it."

"I can predict the future."

Chapter 1148: 1147: An Impossible Future

"Predict the future?"

Time Immortal showed a look of disappointment. He had thought Lu Yang might come up with an unexpected method, but it turned out to be just predicting the future.

What was prediction of the future as a proof of ability? He was capable of that too.

Not only could he do it, but he had also taught Fairy Eternity, although Fairy Eternity had no talent in this area and could only predict the weather.

"Oh, right! Little Yang, you can predict the future!"

Fairy Eternity excitedly patted Lu Yang’s shoulder, "This Immortal knew Little Yang had skills!"

Yun Zhi also showed a knowing smile; that’s right, her Junior Brother had this trick up his sleeve.

Time Immortal noticed the reactions of Fairy Eternity and Yun Zhi, feeling a sense of absurdity. Was predicting the future really such an effective method?

"Time Immortal may not know this, but my prediction of the future is slightly different from yours. My prediction of the future is able to foresee a future that will not come to pass."

"?"

Time Immortal was full of question marks. How could his prediction of the future be different from his own?

Lu Yang had learned predicting the future from Fairy Eternity. After mastering it, when performing his magic skill, in the predicted future, he saw himself indulging in food and drink all day, shirking responsibilities, with Eldest Senior Sister patting his back and saying she would support him in the future.

To verify the results of his cultivation, in reality, he told Eldest Senior Sister he would indulge in such a lifestyle, which enraged her so much that it almost led to a life-threatening crisis for him.

Since then, Lu Yang knew how inaccurate his "predict the future" was—it always showed impossibilities!

"Performing it in Spiritual Space might not be very effective. It has to be done outside," said Lu Yang. While his physical form was an Immortal Body, his Soul Body was not.

Naturally, the higher the realm one was in when performing magic spells, the better the effects.

Lu Yang’s consciousness left the Spiritual Space and walked out of the Ancestral Temple.

"Second-in-command, are you going out?" Yun Mengmeng and Jin Caiwei were each carrying a shovel at the temple gate, digging a hole, presumably for some reason.

Yun Zhi and Time Immortal went straight to Lu Yang’s Spiritual Space; the others didn’t know about their visit.

"Oh, just stepping out for a bit. I won’t go far."

"Remember to come back for dinner."

"Alright."

Lu Yang didn’t go far, arriving at an endless desert where even experienced caravan traders would not cross, as it was easy to lose one’s way.

Lu Yang chanted solemn ancient words, his middle finger and index finger joined together, and he shouted, "Predict the future!"

This was a technique he had learned during the Foundation Establishment Stage; now, upon performing it again, it was naturally extraordinary.

Black rivers spread out from under his feet, and droplets transformed into fragments of time, appearing before Lu Yang’s eyes:

...

"Fairy, from now on, I am yours!"

"Ah? Really? Do you want to ascend the throne now?"

"Yes, I’ll obey everything you say, Fairy!"

Lu Yang abandoned his pride, becoming Fairy Eternity’s man, ascending the throne in the Ancestral Temple. Chief of Staff Yun Mengmeng busily oversaw the inauguration ceremony while Historian Qinghe recorded the event. The three Great Generals returned to their original forms, howling to the sky to display their might.

Lu Yang and Fairy Eternity both sat on the Emperor’s Chair, with many ladies coming to the Ancestral Temple, kneeling before Lu Yang in unison, shouting "His Majesty."

Lu Yang accepted the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit bestowed by Fairy Eternity, becoming the true Second-in-command and a Semi-Immortal, completely obedient to the Fairy, while Fairy Eternity was delighted all day long, her mouth never closing from so much smiling.

...

Yun Zhi and Time Immortal silently turned their heads to look at Lu Yang with indescribable expressions in their eyes.

Lu Yang coughed into his fist twice, "Ahem, this is one of the futures that won’t happen. Don’t overthink it; let’s look at other fragments."

...

"Sir, I wish to leave for a bit." In the Ancestral Temple, Qinghe seemed to have thought of something, and, after bidding farewell to Fairy Eternity, vanished without a trace.

Indistinctly, Qinghe was heard muttering something about a "river" before she left.

When Lu Yang and the others returned to the Seek Dao Sect, they saw Qinghe and Eldest Senior Sister standing at the front gates of the sect.

Lu Yang didn’t quite understand why Qinghe, after leaving the Ancestral Temple, had come back to the Seek Dao Sect or why she was standing with Eldest Senior Sister.

Upon seeing Fairy Eternity, Eldest Senior Sister’s eyes were unusually anxious, unsure of what had transpired.

...

"Enough, this segment doesn’t provide any clues; let’s look at the other segments." Saying this, Eldest Senior Sister quietly blocked one of the futures that could not possibly occur, preventing anyone from further indulging their curiosity.

...

Inside the Ancestral Temple, Lu Yang sat cross-legged on a lotus flower, contemplating heaven and earth and the rules, when suddenly a thought struck him, "Hey, Fairy, since the Time Immortal can use the Power of Faith to shape an Immortal Body, as an Immortal, can you do it too?"

"Is it possible or not?" When asked this question, Fairy Eternity looked panicked, glancing left and right, unable to meet Lu Yang’s gaze.

"Let’s just try and see." Lu Yang encouraged, feeling it would be great if he could help the Fairy regain her Immortal Body.

Fairy Eternity stood above the Ancestral Temple, wrapped in the boundless Power of Faith, which helped her condense a perfect Immortal Body identical to her original one.

"Fairy, congratulations!" Lu Yang, beaming with joy, ascended to the sky to applaud and congratulate her.

"How can it really be successful?" Fairy Eternity was tearfully pleading, her eyes brimming with tears.

"I don’t want this, I want to live in Little Yang’s Spiritual Space!"

...

Everyone looked together at Fairy Eternity, her cheeks flushed with embarrassment.

"This, this segment isn’t worth watching, it’s too far-fetched to be true!" Fairy Eternity hurriedly blocked this future.

"It’s just like when you, Little Yang, saw that Miss Yun wanted to raise you. It’s a very false future; let’s watch the next segment!"

Time Immortal was breaking a cold sweat, feeling that he might have glimpsed some dire secrets. If the next segment didn’t include him, that would be hard to explain.

The next one must be about me.

Wait, that’s not right, aren’t we here to verify the methods of the hidden antagonist?

...

"You can’t use this method, either!" Jin Caiwei said through gritted teeth, "You’ve turned into a ghost story!"

"What ghost story?" Mr. Shi was startled.

"At the black market, they’re saying that a female ghost appears in the Recording Orb, and whoever sees the female ghost will suffer misfortune!"

"So you’re talking about that story; I didn’t start that one," Mr. Shi quickly disassociated himself.

The segment disappeared.

"This is it!" Lu Yang pointed excitedly at this segment.

"Huh? Why?" Fairy Eternity didn’t understand, "Is it because the Qiongqi Clan youngster’s response was different from what we have seen?"

Yun Zhi also agreed with Junior Brother’s point of view and explained, "It’s because this segment is the shortest among all segments, so short it’s practically nonsensical. The other segments depict a timeframe of at least several days, but this segment is just a few lines of dialogue."

"Although Junior Brother cast the future prediction with an Immortal Body, he is still only at the ’Magic Spells stage’ and not yet at the ’Dao Fruit level’; the hidden antagonist surely uses the Dao Fruit to modify time points."

"Therefore, Junior Brother cannot show a complete timeline that has been modified."

"Conversely, among all the impossible future segments, only the shortest one has been modified by the hidden antagonist."

"This confirms that one of the hidden antagonist’s abilities is indeed to modify time lines."

Chapter 1149: 1148: Modifying the Constraints of the Timeline

"Was it really about altering the timeline? Such a troublesome ability," the Time Immortal frowned. This ability was truly indefensible.

"And that’s just one of the abilities," Lu Yang said, "The Fairy’s downfall should be unrelated to the alteration of the timeline."

"How so? Is it because when the Immortal Fairy fell, the world forgot about her, but the four of us still remember? If it’s about altering the timeline, shouldn’t the four of us be unable to remember her as well?"

"Not just for that reason. From what has been shown so far, the mysterious manipulator behind the scenes does not alter the timeline at will."

"During the ten thousand years from the disappearance of the Immortal Fairy to the fall of the Fire Dynasty, he did not alter the timeline to one where the ’Four Ancient Immortals’ did not exist, which suggests that there are restrictions on altering the timeline. As for what those restrictions are, it’s still unknown; it could be that they cannot erase beings that are too powerful or they cannot modify time nodes that are too far apart."

If even the Four Ancient Immortals could not be erased, how could the Immortal Fairy be erased?

"How did you conclude that time nodes too far apart cannot be modified?" The Immortal Fairy was a bit confused.

"Let me give you a simple example. What would be the easiest way for the manipulator behind the scenes to erase the Yingtian Immortal by altering the timeline?"

"Senior Ying Tian was once buried as a sacrificial offering and later escaped. The easiest way would be to alter the timeline to prevent his escape, making him die in the tomb."

"But the manipulator behind the scenes didn’t do that."

"That’s why I said that the manipulator behind the scenes probably can’t modify time nodes that are too far apart."

"As for why the manipulator behind the scenes can alter the timeline, I suspect it’s due to the ability of the Cause and Effect Dao Fruit to reverse cause and effect."

Everyone nodded; Lu Yang’s counterstrategy was quite reasonable.

"However, I still haven’t figured out why the manipulator behind the scenes would want to alter the timeline this time."

Lu Yang’s gaze fell on the segment of time involving Jin Caiwei and Mr. Shi.

...

"Jin Cuoshi, are you sure the bizarre legends in the black market originated with you?" Lu Yang once again found Mr. Shi, causing him to tremble with fear.

Although he didn’t know Lu Yang’s identity, the fact that he could even calm a furious ancestor spoke volumes of the terrifying presence he was.

"It really was me." Mr. Shi shook like a sieve. Wasn’t this matter already in the past? Why was it being brought up again?

Lu Yang was certain Mr. Shi was not lying. He had also visited the black market; the bizarre legends there were exactly the same as Mr. Shi’s actions.

However, in the original timeline, Mr. Shi had denied this, meaning the original bizarre legends of the black market weren’t his doing, but someone else’s, and the manipulator behind the scenes wanted to conceal this person.

Had the manipulator behind the scenes transferred the cause onto Mr. Shi?

"Are these bizarre legends only present in this city, or do they exist in other cities as well?"

"Of course not," Mr. Shi said confidently. He used the Recording Orb to hide a female ghost to boost the sales of the Recording Orb. He wasn’t in charge of other cities; boosting the sales of the Recording Orb in other cities had nothing to do with him.

Lu Yang lowered his head in thought, then shook his head with a sigh. It seemed he really couldn’t ask anything more; the current clues could only lead to this point.

Plus, if he truly could deduce the hidden matters the manipulator behind the scenes wanted to conceal, he feared the manipulator would continue to alter the timeline.

Unable to trace the next step in the clues, there was no need for Yun Zhi and the Time Immortal to stay any longer.

"Junior Brother, you must cultivate diligently and strive to reach the Body Integration Realm soon," Yun Zhi advised before leaving, her cold eyes darting back and forth between Lu Yang and the Immortal Fairy, somewhat worried that her Junior Brother might one day succumb to temptation and join the Immortal Fairy.

But since the Junior Brother in the impossible future had joined the Immortal Fairy, that meant this future wouldn’t happen...right?

"Use methods like predicting the future sparingly," Yun Zhi cautioned again, fearing her Junior Brother’s curiosity might lead him to watch the blocked segments.

"Alright!"

"Then I’ll be leaving too; if you encounter any difficulties in the Buddha Country, feel free to seek me out," the Time Immortal said, not having known until now that Lu Yang had come to the Buddha Country.

After Yun Zhi and the Time Immortal left, Lu Yang returned to the Ancestral Temple.

At the entrance of the Ancestral Temple, Yun Mengmeng and Jin Caiwei were planting trees, with dirt on their faces, probably from the planting.

"This way, we can cultivate the Immortal Spirit Fruit. How amazing," Yun Mengmeng was learning how to cultivate the Immortal Spirit Fruit from Jin Caiwei.

"Definitely, that’s how I cultivate the plane Spirit Fruit. I just wonder what it would taste like to add the Immortal Spirit Fruit to cookies."

"Second-in-command, you’re back!" Yun Mengmeng looked up, saw Lu Yang returning, and happily wiped the dirt from her face with her arm, smearing it more.

"I’m back. Just now my Time Magic had some advancement, but this Ancestral Temple is too small for proper casting, so I had to step out for a while," Lu Yang boasted casually.

"Time Magic, that’s amazing!" Yun Mengmeng was astounded, having never seen Time Magic in her life.

Sitting on the Lotus Platform of the Ancestral Temple, Lu Yang’s thoughts were a mess, continuously recalling the impossible futures he had seen.

"Fairy, can you really not use the Power of Faith to gather an Immortal Body?"

"Sh-shouldn’t be able to?" The Immortal Fairy’s eyes darted anxiously. What to do, what to do, she was not good at lying.

"Right, Little Lotus, come here for a moment." Inspiration struck the Immortal Fairy, and she called Qinghe in from outside the Ancestral Temple.

"Greetings, my lord." Qinghe flicked her sleeves, curling them around her arms, slightly bending her body, hands overlapped blocking her face, demonstrating that the holy face should not be seen.

"Just now, Little Yang said that I could use the Power of Faith to gather an Immortal Body. Little Lotus, you are most proficient with the Power of Faith, what do you think?"

Qinghe’s eyes brightened, and she couldn’t hide the smile on her face. Had the lord finally come to her senses, about to leave Lu Yang?

"Reporting to my lord, of course it is possible..."

"Hmm?" The Immortal Fairy questioned with a nasal tone.

"Is it possible...?" Qinghe hesitated, unsure of her lord’s intentions. Having served the Immortal Fairy for so long, she hadn’t encountered this type of situation before.

"I’m asking you." The Immortal Fairy avoided Lu Yang’s gaze and made faces at Qinghe.

"...Of course it is not possible," Qinghe stated seriously.

"The lord’s Immortal Body is noble beyond words. Even a glance is an affront, let alone using the Power of Faith, which would defile the lord’s precious body. Lu Guozhu, refrain from mentioning this matter again!"

"Really? It’s not possible?" Lu Yang looked at Qinghe skeptically, "Qinghe, you seem to be in a bad mood?"

He had a feeling that Qinghe was holding back tears while speaking.

Qinghe swallowed hard, determined not to show weakness in front of Lu Yang: "No, I’m not in a bad mood. I just feel sad thinking that the lord can’t gather an Immortal Body."

Chapter 1150: 1149: Yun Mengmeng’s Weapon

Lu Yang’s heart was kind, and he could tell that Qinghe was putting on a brave face. Although the two of them were in competition, they were both servants of the same master, so there was no need for their relationship to be so tense.

He offered her some comforting words, but it seemed to make Senior Qinghe even sadder.
